TL;DR: BayLing-Duplex 是一种原生全双工语音语言模型，无需外部 VAD 模块，由单一自回归 LLM 自主决定何时听、何时说、何时停止，仅通过添加少量特殊 token 实现，在保持响应质量的同时显著提升了交互自然性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音语言模型（如 LLaMA-Omni、GLM-4-Voice）仍为轮次式，依赖外部 VAD 模块标记用户话语结束，无法处理重叠、犹豫、插话等自然对话现象，从根本上限制了交互能力。因此需要一种原生全双工架构，使模型能够同时听与说。

Method: 提出 BayLing-Duplex，基于公开的 GLM-4-Voice 检查点，仅在标准词表中添加少量特殊 token（用于控制听/说/停止状态），无需架构改动；使用 40 万条全双工样本进行微调，随后进行轻量级 DPO 阶段训练；单一大语言模型同时承担听、说和轮次决策，无需辅助轮次管理模块。

Result: 在 InstructS2S-Eval 上达到 92% 轮次切换成功率和 100% 打断成功率；语音响应评分从 Moshi 的 2.17 提升至 3.39；在 Llama Questions、Web Questions 和 Alpaca-Eval 上达到或超越其轮次式对应模型的表现。

Conclusion: 同时听与说的全双工建模不会牺牲响应质量，BayLing-Duplex 以极简的设计（几个特殊 token + 现有训练/推理框架复用）实现了高自然度的实时全双工语音交互，为下一代口语聊天机器人提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Real-time, full-duplex speech interaction is a key feature of next-generation spoken chatbots, allowing the model to listen and speak at the same time and to handle natural phenomena such as overlap, hesitation, and barge-in. Existing speech language models (SpeechLMs) such as LLaMA-Omni and GLM-4-Voice are still turn-based and rely on an external Voice Activity Detection (VAD) module to mark the end of the user's turn, which fundamentally limits their interactive ability. In this paper, we introduce BayLing-Duplex, a native full-duplex SpeechLM where a single autoregressive LLM decides when to listen, when to speak, and when to stop, with no auxiliary turn-taking module. The design adds only a few special tokens to the standard vocabulary, so it transfers across LLMs and reuses existing training and serving stacks with no architectural adaptation. Starting from the public GLM-4-Voice checkpoint and using only 400K full-duplex samples for fine-tuning followed by a lightweight DPO stage, BayLing-Duplex reaches 92% turn-taking success and 100% interruption success on InstructS2S-Eval, while improving the speech-response score from 2.17 to 3.39 over Moshi. BayLing-Duplex also matches or surpasses its turn-based counterpart on Llama Questions, Web Questions, and Alpaca-Eval, showing that simultaneous listen-and-speak modeling does not sacrifice response quality.

</details>


### [106] [SIMMER: Benchmarking Latent Failures in LLM Executable Planning with a World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14574)
*Xiaoxin Lu,Ranran Haoran Zhang,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SIMMER是一个用于评估LLM在家庭环境中规划时潜在失败（latent failures）的基准测试，基于厨房领域的符号化世界模型。实验表明，即使最先进的模型，无错误规划率也仅17%，高达56%的规划包含潜在失败（多数导致不可逆后果）。通过反事实前瞻模拟进行显式状态推理，可将潜在失败减少72%、不可逆案例减少75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM规划基准仅评估规划是否成功执行，忽视了"潜在失败"——这类失败不会在执行时立即触发反馈，但会悄然损害目标达成，严重时可造成不可逆危害。学术界缺乏系统评估此类失败的方法和基准。

Method: 构建SIMMER基准：①定义基于真实烹饪脚本的符号化世界模型，包含77种动作、262种独特对象、约46,800种语义真实的交互；②构建状态机执行器，用于验证规划是否符合世界模型，并检测即时前置条件违规、潜在危害和不可逆失败；③在六种LLM上进行实验评估；④提出反事实前瞻模拟（counterfactual foresight simulation）方法，通过显式状态推理来减少潜在失败。

Result: ①前沿模型的无错误规划率最高仅17%；②高达56%的规划包含潜在失败，且其中大多数导致不可逆后果；③采用反事实前瞻模拟后，潜在失败减少高达72%，不可逆案例减少高达75%。

Conclusion: LLM作为自主代理规划器时，潜在失败是严重但被忽视的问题。通过显式状态推理（反事实前瞻模拟）可显著缓解该问题，为构建更鲁棒的LLM规划器指明了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as planners for autonomous agents in household environments. While existing benchmarks evaluate whether LLM-generated plans execute successfully, they overlook a critical type of failure: latent failures. Unlike immediate failures that trigger instant feedback at execution time and enable timely correction, latent failures do not immediately halt plan execution but silently compromise goal achievement. In severe cases, they cause irreversible harm. To address this gap, we introduce SIMMER, a benchmark for evaluating latent failures in LLM planning through a human-curated symbolic world model grounded in the kitchen domain. SIMMER defines a world model comprising 77 actions, 262 unique objects, and approximately 46,800 possible interactions that are semantically realistic, derived from real-world cooking scripts. It then leverages a state machine executor that validates plans against the world model and detects immediate precondition violations, latent hazards, and irreversible failures. Experiments across six LLMs show that even frontier models achieve at most 17% error-free plans. Moreover, up to 56% of plans contain latent failures, the majority of which lead to irreversible consequences. We further demonstrate that explicit state reasoning via counterfactual foresight simulation can reduce latent failures by up to 72% and irreversible cases by up to 75%, suggesting a promising direction for more robust LLM planners.

</details>


### [107] [Persuasion Index: A Theory-Guided Framework for Persuasion Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14580)
*Liancheng Gong,Zhiyang Wang,Yiwei Xu,Julia Mendelsohn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了说服力指数（PI）——一个基于心理学和传播学说服理论、包含15个维度和55个子特征的透明分类法，用于识别修辞说服线索。在四个公开数据集上验证，PI提供了可解释的共享特征空间，线性模型即能有效预测说服相关结果，已作为开源工具发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别说服性修辞线索在信息操纵检测、AI安全提升和公共健康传播等多个领域至关重要，但当前缺乏一个理论基础扎实、透明可解释且模块化的统一分析框架。

Method: 提出Persuasion Index (PI)分类法：15个维度扎根于心理学和传播学说服理论；采用55个基于词典和规则检测器的子特征实现；设计为模块化架构，单个检测器可替换而理论结构不变；在领域、风格和结果指标各异的四个公开数据集上评估；使用线性模型检验PI特征的预测信号。

Result: PI提供了一个跨数据集的共享特征空间，能够解释与说服结果相关的修辞模式；线性模型显示PI特征具有有意义的预测信号且计算轻量；维度层面分析揭示了PI维度与说服结果之间跨数据集的反复出现的关联，同时也凸显了主题和立场特定的变异。

Conclusion: PI作为一个有原则、可审计的人类与AI中介沟通分析框架，兼具理论基础、透明性和实用性，已作为开源包和Web界面发布，有助于推进多领域的说服性修辞研究。

Abstract: Identifying persuasive rhetorical cues is critical across domains, from detecting information manipulation and improving AI safety to advancing public health communication. We propose Persuasion Index (PI), a taxonomy of 15 dimensions grounded in persuasion theories from psychology and communication, and one transparent implementation using 55 sub-features built from lexicons and rule-based detectors. The taxonomy is modular: individual detectors can be replaced while preserving the theoretical structure. By evaluating PI on four public datasets varying in domain, style, and outcome measures, we show that PI provides a shared feature space for interpreting rhetorical patterns associated with persuasion-related outcomes. Linear models show that PI features carry meaningful predictive signal while remaining computationally lightweight. Dimension-level analyses reveal recurring associations between PI dimensions and persuasion outcomes across datasets, while also highlighting topic- and stance-specific variation. We release PI as an open-source package and web interface for principled and auditable analysis of human and AI-mediated communication.

</details>


### [108] [LoSoNA: A Benchmark for Local Social Norm Adaptation in Group Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14600)
*Mateusz Winiarek,Maksymilian Bilski,Mateusz Jacniacki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoSoNA是一个用于评估LLM在多轮群聊中推断并适应当地社交规范的基准测试。测试中，模型需从对话先例中推断隐藏的会话规范，并在最终回合中做出符合该规范的回应。结果显示，仅有部分模型在显式规范感知提示下表现显著提升（如Gemini 3.1 Pro达84.2%，Claude Fable 5达81.6%），多数模型收益有限甚至退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线群聊中普遍存在未明说的本地会话规范，但LLM智能体识别并适应这些规范的能力尚未被充分探索。现有评估忽视了对模型从对话先例中推断隐性社会规范的测试需求。

Method: 构建LoSoNA基准：为每个场景提供一段精心策划的群聊记录，其中非受试参与者展示一个隐藏的本地规范，最后以"引出轮"（elicitor turn）强制受试模型做出揭示其是否已推断出该规范的回应。在四种提示条件下评估8个前沿及开源模型，提示条件按"是否显式告知模型将先前对话作为回答依据"的程度变化。

Result: 朴素提示下多数模型表现有限；显式规范感知提示带来不均衡的提升——Gemini 3.1 Pro达84.2%，Claude Fable 5达81.6%，但其他若干模型仅显示微弱增益甚至性能退化。

Conclusion: LoSoNA回应了近期对评估LLM社交能力的呼吁，证明当前模型从对话先例中推断本地会话规范并在单回合群聊回应中加以运用的能力仍不均衡，且高度依赖提示策略。

Abstract: Online group chats are social spaces with local conversational norms that are rarely stated explicitly. The ability and willingness of LLM-based agents to recognize and adapt to these norms remains mostly unexplored. We introduce LoSoNA, a benchmark for local social norm adaptation in multi-party chat. Each scenario gives a subject model a curated group-chat transcript in which non-subject participants demonstrate a hidden local norm, followed by a final elicitor turn that forces a response revealing whether the subject has inferred that norm. We evaluate eight frontier and open-weight models under four prompting conditions that vary how explicitly the model is told to treat the prior conversation as evidence for how it should answer. Naive prompting remains limited for most models; explicit norm-aware prompting helps unevenly, with Gemini 3.1 Pro reaching $84.2\%$ and Claude Fable 5 reaching $81.6\%$, while several other models show small gains or regressions. LoSoNA contributes to recent calls for evaluating LLM social capabilities by testing whether models can infer local conversational norms from precedent and use them in a one-turn group-chat response.

</details>


### [109] [Characterizing Cultural Localization in AI-Generated Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14626)
*Shaily Bhatt,Supriti Vijay,Jeremiah Milbauer,Fernando Diaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种检测故事生成中"模板化本地化"程度的方法：通过识别区分不同国籍故事的词汇标记（文化标记），移除后分析剩余叙事的相似性。对5个模型在125个主题、193个国家生成的故事分析发现，仅9-17%的词汇解释了跨国差异，剩余叙事包含重复的多词序列，表明存在文化无关的通用叙事模板。此外，对文化标记的刻板印象和冒犯性评估显示，19个国家（主要位于全球南方）的文化标记平均具有冒犯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在全球的广泛应用，评估其生成文化本地化内容（尤其是故事）的能力变得愈发重要。然而，现有研究缺乏有效方法来区分"模板化本地化"（仅替换文化标记，如人名、地名）与"整体本地化"（情节、价值观、主题也随之变化），这使得难以判断AI是否真正实现了文化适应，还是仅仅在通用模板上做了表面替换。

Method: 提出一种量化模板化本地化程度的方法：(1) 通过统计方法识别区分不同国籍故事的词汇标记（cultural markers）；(2) 移除这些标记后，测量剩余叙事文本的相似性（含多词序列重复检测）；(3) 对识别出的文化标记进行刻板印象和冒犯性评估。实验覆盖5个模型、125个主题、193个国籍的故事生成。

Result: （1）仅9-17%的词汇量解释了跨国故事差异，说明文化本地化集中在少数词汇标记上；（2）移除文化标记后，剩余叙事包含大量重复的多词序列，表明模型依赖一个文化无关的通用叙事模板；（3）19个国家（多位于全球南方）的文化标记平均具有冒犯性，暴露出模型在文化表征上的偏见问题。

Conclusion: 当前AI模型在生成文化本地化故事时，主要采用的是"模板化本地化"策略——在通用叙事模板上替换少量文化标记，而非进行真正的整体文化适应。这种方法导致两方面问题：叙事本质上千篇一律，缺乏文化多样性；且所用的文化标记常带有刻板印象和冒犯性，尤其对全球南方国家不利。研究呼吁开发能够实现更深层次文化本地化的生成方法。

Abstract: The global use of artificial intelligence has increased interest in assessing the ability to generate culturally localized content, including stories. Cultural localization in stories often occurs through either templated localization -- the use of cultural markers (e.g., names, locations) in a generic narrative -- or holistic localization -- the variation of plots, values, and themes, in addition to cultural markers. We propose a method to measure the degree to which content was generated through templated localization. Specifically, we identify the lexical tokens that distinguish stories across nationalities and measure the similarity of the narratives that remain after removing them. In stories generated by five models on 125 topics for 193 nationalities, our method is able to detect that only a small subset (9-17%) of the vocabulary accounts for the variation across nationalities and that the narratives that remain after removing them contain repeated multi-word sequences, suggesting the presence of a shared culturally-agnostic narrative template. Finally, we characterize the cultural markers for their stereotypicality and offensiveness, finding that markers from 19 countries, mostly located in the Global South, are on average offensive.

</details>


### [110] [AgentSpec: Understanding Embodied Agent Scaffolds Through Controlled Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14674)
*Jixuan Chen,Jianzhi Shen,Haoqiang Kang,Zhi Hong,Qingyi Jiang,Soham Bose,Yiming Zhang,Leon Leng,Amit Vyas,Lingjun Mao,Siru Ouyang,Kun Zhou,Lianhui Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AgentSpec 是一个模块化框架，将具身LLM智能体定义为可复用策略组件的类型化组合。研究发现智能体性能受支架兼容性和交互效应主导，而非孤立模块强度——结构化多粒度记忆改善长时状态追踪，推理与记忆在不同环境中存在非均匀交互，反思模块在纠错与成本间权衡，RL策略与部署时支架结构联合优化时组合效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体通常以紧耦合流水线构建，难以隔离各组件贡献、比较替代设计或理解模块间交互如何塑造智能体行为。需要一个标准化的模块化框架来实现组件的受控交换与重组。

Method: 提出AgentSpec——一个模块化规范框架，将具身智能体表示为具有标准化接口的可复用策略组件的类型化组合。框架标准化了感知、记忆、推理、反思、行动和可选学习模块之间的接口，支持在受控条件下交换和重组组件。在DeliveryBench、ALFRED、MiniGrid和RoboTHOR四个具身环境中实例化该框架，跨不同模型骨架分析推理、记忆、反思和强化学习模块。

Result: （1）结构化多粒度记忆改善了长时域状态追踪；（2）推理与记忆在不同环境中存在非均匀交互效应；（3）反思模块在纠错能力与计算成本之间权衡；（4）RL训练策略在与部署时的支架结构联合优化时组合效果最佳。核心发现：智能体性能由支架兼容性和交互效应主导，而非孤立模块的强度。

Conclusion: AgentSpec为研究、比较和设计可组合LLM智能体提供了受控基础。代码、基线和交互式实验平台已公开。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly built not as single model calls, but as scaffolded systems that combine reasoning, memory, reflection, action execution, and learning. While such scaffolds often improve performance, they are often embedded in tightly coupled pipelines, making it difficult to isolate component contributions, compare alternative designs, or understand how module interactions shape agent behavior. We introduce AgentSpec, a modular specification framework that represents embodied agents as typed compositions of reusable policy components with standardized interfaces. AgentSpec standardizes the interfaces among perception, memory, reasoning, reflection, action, and optional learning, enabling components to be swapped and recombined under controlled conditions. We instantiate this framework across DeliveryBench, ALFRED, MiniGrid, and RoboTHOR, and analyze reasoning, memory, reflection, and reinforcement-learning modules across model backbones. Our results show that agent performance is governed by scaffold compatibility and interaction effects rather than isolated module strength. In particular, structured multi-granularity memory improves long-horizon state tracking, reasoning and memory interact non-uniformly across environments, reflection trades off correction and cost, and RL-trained policies compose best when optimized with deployment-time scaffold structure. AgentSpec provides a controlled foundation for studying, comparing, and designing composable LLM agents. Our code, baselines and interactive playground are publicly available at https://agentspec-embodied.github.io.

</details>


### [111] [CORA: Analyzing and bridging thinking-answer gap in Multimodal RLVR via Consistency-Oriented Reasoning Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14691)
*Jiayue Cao,Zhicong Lu,Xuehan Sun,Wei Jia,Hongling Zheng,Changyuan Tian,Zichuan Lin,Wenqian Lv,Nayu Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉语言模型强化学习中"思考过程与最终答案语义不一致"的问题，提出一致性导向的推理对齐方法CORA，通过即插即用的一致性奖励模型和混合奖励优势拆分策略，有效缓解该不一致性并提升推理保真度与任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法在扩展到多模态场景时，主要关注视觉覆盖和幻觉缓解，却忽视了推理过程（thinking）与最终答案（answer）之间的语义不一致问题。作者通过分析GRPO训练过程中的rollouts和训练后的评估输出，发现该不一致性在训练和推理阶段持续存在，亟需针对性解决。

Method: 提出CORA方法，包含两个核心组件：(1) 轻量级即插即用的一致性奖励模型（Consistency Reward Model），将thinking-answer语义一致性引入RLVR；(2) 混合奖励优势拆分（HRAS, Hybrid Reward Advantage Splitting），用于稳定协调任务优化与一致性优化两个目标。

Result: 在多个代表性多模态推理基准和主流LVLM上的广泛实验表明，CORA在提升任务性能的同时，有效缓解了thinking-answer不一致问题，产生了更忠实的推理轨迹。

Conclusion: CORA通过显式建模thinking-answer语义一致性，弥补了现有RLVR方法在多模态推理中的关键缺陷，为LVLM的可靠推理对齐提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has successfully elicited the reasoning capabilities of large language models, motivating its extension to multimodal scenarios. Existing methods primarily focus on improving the visual coverage of reasoning traces and mitigating visual hallucinations, but underestimate the semantic inconsistency between the reasoning process and the final answer. In this paper, we delve into thinking-answer inconsistency in RLVR for large vision-language models (LVLMs), showing thorough analyses of rollouts collected throughout Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) training process and post-RLVR evaluation outputs that this issue persists during training and remains present during inference. Motivated by the analysis, we propose Consistency-Oriented Reasoning Alignment (CORA), which introduces thinking-answer semantic consistency into RLVR through a lightweight plug-and-play consistency reward model, and further incorporates Hybrid Reward Advantage Splitting (HRAS) to stably coordinate task and consistency optimization. Extensive experiments across representative multimodal reasoning benchmarks and mainstream LVLMs show that CORA improves task performance while effectively mitigating thinking-answer inconsistency, leading to more faithful reasoning traces.

</details>


### [112] [GAGPO: Generalized Advantage Grouped Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13217)
*Siyuan Zhu,Chao Yu,Rongxin Yang,Zongkai Liu,Jinjun Hu,Qiwen Chen,Yibo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GAGPO 是一种无 critic 的强化学习方法，通过从采样轨迹构建非参数分组价值代理，实现多轮智能体环境中的逐步时空信用分配，在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮交互环境中，奖励通常是稀疏的、轨迹级别的，仅在回合结束时给出，导致难以判断哪些中间动作对最终成败有贡献。现有方案要么依赖昂贵的辅助价值模型（如 critic），要么信用分配粒度不够精细。因此，需要一种无需 critic 即可将延迟结果精确传播到每个决策步骤的方法。

Method: 提出 GAGPO（Generalized Advantage Grouped Policy Optimization）：(1) 从采样 rollout 中构建非参数分组价值代理（grouped value proxy）；(2) 利用该代理计算 TD/GAE 风格的时序优势（temporal advantages），将结果监督递归地反向传播到各时间步；(3) 引入分组优势归一化（group-wise advantage normalization）和动作级重要性比率（action-level importance ratio），从多轮轨迹中提取稳定、局部的优化信号。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 两个基准上，GAGPO 均优于现有强 RL 基线方法。进一步分析表明，GAGPO 具有更快的早期学习速度、更高的交互效率以及更平滑的优化动态。

Conclusion: GAGPO 为多轮智能体强化学习提供了一个简洁而有效的框架，无需 critic 即可实现精确的步级信用分配，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a powerful paradigm for post-training large language model agents, yet credit assignment in multi-turn environments remains a challenge. Agents often receive sparse, trajectory-level rewards only at the end of an episode, making it difficult to determine which intermediate actions contributed to success or failure. As a result, propagating delayed outcomes back to individual decision steps without relying on costly auxiliary value models remains an open problem. We propose Generalized Advantage Grouped Policy Optimization (GAGPO), a critic-free reinforcement learning method for precise, step-aligned temporal credit assignment. GAGPO constructs a non-parametric grouped value proxy from sampled rollouts and uses it to compute TD/GAE-style temporal advantages, recursively propagating outcome supervision backward through time. Combined with group-wise advantage normalization and an action-level importance ratio, GAGPO extracts stable, localized optimization signals directly from multi-turn trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show that GAGPO outperforms strong reinforcement learning baselines. Further analyses demonstrate faster early-stage learning, improved interaction efficiency, and smoother optimization dynamics, suggesting that GAGPO offers a simple yet effective framework for multi-turn agentic reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [113] [AdaSR: Adaptive Streaming Reasoning with Hierarchical Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14694)
*Junlong Tong,Wenqi Xu,Yingqi Fan,Anhao Zhao,Xuan Lu,Yang Tan,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaSR 提出了一种自适应流式推理框架，让大模型能够在输入流持续到达时进行边读边思考，并在流结束后进行最终深度推理。通过分层相对策略优化（HRPO），模型学会在何时思考以及分配多少计算量，在推理准确率、计算效率和流式延迟之间取得了更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大推理模型采用"先读后想"范式，不适合音频、视频流等动态场景；而现有流式推理方法依赖监督模仿预构造轨迹，灵活性不足。需要一种能让模型在流式输入中自适应决定何时思考、分配多少计算量的框架。

Method: 提出 AdaSR（自适应流式推理框架），将推理过程分为流式推理和深度推理两个阶段。设计 HRPO（分层相对策略优化），将策略优化分解为流式推理和深度推理两个层次，提供更细粒度的优势分配，而非将单一序列级优势均匀分布到所有 token。同时整合格式奖励、准确率奖励和自适应思考奖励，以约束推理协议、保持任务性能并鼓励延迟感知的计算分配。

Result: 实验表明，与监督微调基线相比，AdaSR 在推理准确率、计算效率和流式延迟之间取得了更好的平衡。

Conclusion: AdaSR 通过自适应流式推理和分层策略优化，成功解决了动态流式场景下推理的灵活性与效率问题，具有优于监督微调基线的综合性能。

Abstract: Large reasoning models typically follow a read-then-think paradigm: they observe the complete input, reason over a static context, and then produce the answer. Yet many real-world scenarios are inherently dynamic, such as audio and video stream, where information arrives as a continuous stream and models must reason, update, and respond under partial observations. Recent streaming reasoning methods allow models to think while reading, but they largely rely on supervised imitation of pre-constructed trajectories, which limits their flexibility. In this paper, we propose AdaSR, an adaptive streaming reasoning framework that enables models to reason during input streaming and perform final deliberation once the stream is complete, learning when to think, and how much computation to allocate across different stages. To optimize this hierarchical reasoning process, we introduce Hierarchical Relative Policy Optimization (HRPO), which decomposes policy optimization into streaming reasoning and deep reasoning phases, providing more fine-grained advantage assignment instead of uniformly distributing a single sequence-level advantage over all tokens. HRPO integrates format, accuracy, and adaptive thinking rewards to enforce valid reasoning protocols, preserve final task performance, and encourage latency-aware computation allocation. Experiments show that AdaSR achieves a better balance among reasoning accuracy, computational efficiency, and streaming latency compared with supervised fine-tuning baseline. We release our code at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/StreamingLLM/tree/main/AdaSR.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Can Editing 1 Neuron Fix Repetition Loops in LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13705)
*Aristotelis Lazaridis,Aman Sharma,Dylan Bates,Brian King,Vincent Lu,Jack FitzGerald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Gemma 4 指令微调模型在长文本事实枚举任务中会陷入灾难性重复循环（doom loop），本文通过神经元归因和权重编辑（weight surgery）成功定位并抑制了导致该问题的少量 MLP 神经元，虽能消除循环但无法解决底层知识缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gemma 4 系列模型在列举长串事实（如星座、宝可梦）时，以高达 95% 的概率陷入重复循环，且对提示改写和采样调整具有鲁棒性。作者希望探究该病理行为是否可被定位到具体参数并通过权重编辑移除。

Method: 采用逐层消融（per-layer ablation）和逐神经元归因（per-neuron attribution）定位导致循环的神经元；对候选神经元进行全生成扫描（full-generation sweeps）验证；通过静态权重编辑（包括符号翻转单个神经元）抑制问题神经元；在正常生成预算和较长思考预算下评估编辑效果。

Result: 循环可追溯至少量 MLP 神经元（MoE 模型中为少数路由专家），最小编辑仅需翻转单个神经元符号（E2B 模型）；编辑规模随模型增大而增长，但均可在正常生成预算下消除循环，同时保持通用基准分数；在较长思考预算下，更大模型出现"真正 doom loop"（非收敛性自我修正循环），权重编辑可减轻但无法消除该问题。

Conclusion: 权重手术可删除循环，但无法补充缺失的事实——这本质上是知识精度问题而非可移除电路。该工作既是可行性证明（具体生成病理可定位至少量参数并编辑消除），也明确了该方法的边界。

Abstract: Yes. Can it cure doom loops? Probably not.
  The Gemma 4 instruction-tuned models share a reproducible failure: on long factual enumeration prompts, such as listing every episode of a TV series, the 88 IAU constellations, or the 151 original Pokemon, they collapse into repetition, either a tight verbatim loop or a list whose entries decay onto a single answer. These loops occur at rates as high as 95% and survive prompt rewording, inference-engine changes, and most sampling adjustments. In this paper we explore whether this behavior is localized enough to remove by weight edits. To localize the cause, we use per-layer ablation and per-neuron attribution, then confirm the strongest candidates with full-generation sweeps. The loops trace to a small set of MLP neurons (or, in the 26B-A4B Mixture-of-Experts model, a few routed experts) which we suppress with static weight edits. These "surgeries" can be as small as a single sign-inverted neuron (in the E2B model). The size of the effective edits grows with model scale, but in all cases, the loop patterns can be addressed at normal generation budgets while preserving general-purpose benchmark scores. However, the edits do not solve everything: we also study longer thinking budgets, where the two larger models most visibly enter doom looping, i.e. a non-convergent regime in which the model self-corrects in circles over a fact it cannot recall, exhausting the budget without committing to a final answer. We show this residual failure is reduced but not eliminated by the same edits, and argue it is fundamentally a knowledge-precision problem rather than a removable circuit; weight surgery can delete a loop, but it cannot supply a missing fact. Our results are both a feasibility demonstration, that is, evidence that a concrete generation pathology can be localized to a few parameters and edited out, and a delineation of where that approach stops.

</details>


### [115] [Efficient On-Device Diffusion LLM Inference with Mobile NPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13740)
*Tuowei Wang,Yanfan Sun,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: llada.cpp 是首个面向智能手机 NPU 的扩散大语言模型推理框架，通过多块推测解码、双路径渐进式修正和交换优化内存运行时三项技术，在保持生成质量的前提下实现 17-42 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）通过并行去噪加速生成，适合延迟敏感的移动端推理，但重复去噪计算量大。移动 NPU 虽能提供高吞吐矩阵计算，却面临三大挑战：（1）token 提交导致每块有效负载缩减；（2）token 修正使 KV 缓存复用困难；（3）NPU 可见地址空间有限，引发昂贵的重映射与数据搬运开销。

Method: 设计三项关键技术：（1）Multi-Block Speculative Decoding——在当前块解码后期，以推测性未来块 token 填充缩减的工作负载；（2）Dual-Path Progressive Revision——已提交 token 保持可修正直至稳定，通过 CPU 侧路径刷新不稳定 token，不阻塞 NPU 密集计算；（3）Swap-Optimized Memory Runtime——压缩 NPU 可见地址布局，将数据暂存与 NPU 计算重叠，降低重映射和传输开销。

Result: 在多种硬件平台和 dLLM 负载上评估，llada.cpp 相比带前缀 KV 缓存复用的 CPU 基线，LLaDA-8B 生成延迟降低 17-42 倍，且保持生成质量。

Conclusion: llada.cpp 首次将移动 NPU 高效用于扩散大语言模型推理，通过算法-系统协同设计有效克服了 token 提交、修正和内存三大瓶颈，为延迟敏感的移动端 dLLM 部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) accelerate generation by denoising multiple tokens in parallel, making them attractive for latency-sensitive mobile inference. However, repeated denoising introduces substantial computation on smartphones. Mobile neural processing units (NPUs) offer high-throughput dense matrix computation, but efficiently exploiting them remains challenging: token commitment shrinks per-block effective workloads, token revision complicates KV cache reuse, and limited NPU-visible address space incurs costly remapping and data transfer overheads.
  In this paper, we propose llada.cpp, the first NPU-aware inference framework for accelerating dLLMs on smartphones. llada.cpp aligns block-wise dLLM inference with the execution characteristics of mobile NPUs through three techniques. (1) Multi-Block Speculative Decoding fills the shrinking workload in late-stage current-block decoding with speculative future-block tokens. (2) Dual-Path Progressive Revision keeps committed tokens revisable until stable and refreshes unstable tokens through a CPU-side path without stalling dense NPU execution. (3) Swap-Optimized Memory Runtime compacts NPU-visible address layouts and overlaps data staging with NPU computation to reduce remapping and transfer overheads. We implement llada.cpp as an end-to-end framework and evaluate it across diverse hardware platforms and dLLM workloads. llada.cpp reduces LLaDA-8B generation latency by 17x-42x over the CPU baseline with prefix KV cache reuse, while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [116] [High-Frequency Pricing at Scale for E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13741)
*Stefan Birr,Tobias Huelden,Mones Raslan,Adele Gouttes,Andreas Schmitt,Mateusz Koren,Johannes Stephan,Robert Streek,Manuel Kunz,Tim Januschowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款面向时尚电商促销活动的"先预测后优化"算法定价工具，结合梯度提升树日度需求预测与多目标优化框架，在Zalando的23次A/B测试中实现约6%的利润提升，并已成功部署上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚电商促销活动面临需求模式波动大、定价决策需快速响应、以及需在短期营收与长期利润间取得平衡等挑战。现有周度粒度的定价系统决策耗时长（数小时），无法满足促销场景下快速定价的需求。

Method: 采用"先预测后优化"架构：（1）使用梯度提升树（gradient-boosted trees）进行日粒度的需求预测；（2）构建多目标优化框架，同时最大化长期利润与净商品价值（net merchandise value），覆盖超过500万件商品。该架构将定价决策时间从数小时缩短至数分钟。

Result: 在2023-2024年Zalando的12个市场的23次A/B测试中，新定价系统相比此前的人工-算法混合方案，实现约6%的利润提升，同时在销量和营收上保持同等表现。

Conclusion: 该算法工具在利润指标上显著优于原有方案且不影响其他关键指标，已成功部署至生产环境，目前承担Zalando促销活动中的大部分算法定价决策。

Abstract: This paper presents the design, development, and implementation of a specialized forecast-then-optimize algorithmic pricing tool for sales campaigns in fashion e-commerce. Sales events present unique challenges for pricing including volatile demand patterns, rapid pricing decisions, and the need to balance short-term revenue with long-term profitability. We describe our approach combining daily-resolution demand forecasting using gradient-boosted trees with a multi-objective optimization framework that maximizes both long-term profit and net merchandise value for more than 5 million articles. Our solution addresses key limitations of existing weekly-granularity systems by implementing a forecast-then-optimize architecture that reduces pricing decision time from hours to minutes. We validate our approach through 23 A/B tests across 12 markets during 2023-2024 sales campaigns at Zalando, one of Europe's leading online fashion retailers. Experimental results demonstrate that the new pricing system achieves approximately 6% higher profit while maintaining equivalent performance on sales and revenue compared to the previous manual-algorithmic hybrid approach. Based on these results, the algorithm was successfully deployed to production and now handles the majority of algorithmic pricing decisions for sales campaigns at the company.

</details>


### [117] [A fully GPU-based workflow for building physics emulators of hypersonic flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13742)
*Fabian Paischer,Dylan Rubini,Deniz A. Bezgin,Aaron B. Buhendwa,David Hauser,Florian Sestak,Johannes Brandstetter,Sebastian Kaltenbach,Nikolaus A. Adams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全GPU工作流，将可微高保真求解器JAX-Fluids与神经网络仿真器相结合，通过残差驱动精化增强物理一致性，解决高超声速流动中激波等陡峭梯度的建模难题，使代理模型在训练分布之外仍保持可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高超声速流动中激波位置与强度的精确预测对工程至关重要，但传统降阶模型和神经仿真器难以在工业相关应用中捕捉流动状态的陡峭梯度并保持物理一致性，亟需一种低成本、高保真的替代方案。

Method: 采用可微高保真CFD求解器JAX-Fluids进行快速数据集生成；构建多种神经网络架构并进行尺度化行为分析；引入残差驱动精化机制，在仅有网格和输入参数的情形下训练，利用可微求解器计算残差以增强物理一致性；集成不确定性量化。

Result: 残差驱动精化显著降低了残差并提升了物理一致性；可微仿真与残差精化相结合，使物理仿真器在训练分布之外仍保持可靠性，满足实际工程设计回路中对代理模型的关键需求。

Conclusion: 可微仿真与残差驱动的物理感知精化是构建可靠神经物理仿真器的有效范式，尤其适用于激波主导的高超声速流动，为工业级代理模型部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The ability to resolve complex physical phenomena with high fidelity and at low computational cost is central to addressing key challenges in modern engineering. A prime example lies in hypersonic flows, where the precise prediction of the full flowfield topology, in particular with respect to shock wave location and intensity, is critical. Yet supersonic and hypersonic flows continue to be a stumbling block for traditional reduced-order models and neural emulators that struggle to capture steep gradients in flow states with physical consistency in applications of industrial relevance. To that end, we introduce a fully GPU based workflow that integrates accelerated data generation with the training of neural emulators augmented by uncertainty quantification and physics-aware refinement. Our workflow is enabled by a differentiable high-fidelity solver (JAX-Fluids) which we employ for rapid dataset creation and residual-based improvement of the neural emulator to enhance physical consistency. Building on this framework, we first present a suite of model architectures and analyze their scaling behavior to expose their strengths and shortcomings. We then show that residual-based refinement enables training on cases where only mesh and input parameters are available, substantially reducing residuals and improving physical consistency. Together, differentiable simulation and residual-based refinement yield physics emulators that remain reliable beyond their training distribution, a key requirement for deploying surrogates in real-world engineering design loops.

</details>


### [118] [FedSPC: Shared Parameter Correction for Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13748)
*Kannanthodath Induchoodan Ajay Menon,Christian Prehofer,Yunfei Xu,Toru Hirano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedSPC 是一种模块化的个性化联邦学习校正方法，通过对共享参数施加控制变量校正来解决不同客户端本地优化目标不一致导致的共享更新冲突问题，可无缝集成到多种现有PFL方法中并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在PFL中，模型被分割为共享参数和个性化参数并在各客户端联合训练。但共享参数被不同客户端用各自不同的本地目标优化，导致共享更新方向不一致，从而削弱共享表示的质量。现有PFL方法缺乏专门针对这一共享参数更新冲突问题的机制。

Method: 提出FedSPC（Federated Shared Parameter Correction），核心思想是仅对共享参数施加控制变量校正（control-variate correction），而保持个性化参数不变。具体地，引入客户端级别的共享参数更新修正项，利用全局共享参数状态与本地更新之间的差异来校正梯度方向，从而缓解不同客户端共享更新之间的冲突。该方法可模块化集成到三种常见PFL设置中：共享特征提取器、共享分类器、以及带局部正则化的全共享模型。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet数据集上，使用ViT、ResNet-34和VGG-11等多种架构进行实验。FedSPC在FedPer、FedRep、FedBABU、LG-FedAvg和Ditto等代表性PFL方法上均实现了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: FedSPC通过简单而有效的控制变量校正机制解决了PFL中共享参数更新不一致的核心问题，其模块化设计使其具有广泛的适用性，能够显著提升多种现有PFL方法的性能。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning (PFL) is one of the important approaches in federated learning for addressing statistical heterogeneity while enabling client-specific adaptation. Many PFL methods split the model into shared and personalized parameters, which are jointly trained on each client. However, this creates an optimization issue: shared parameters are updated by clients optimizing different local objectives, which can lead to inconsistent shared updates and weaken the shared representation. To address this problem, we propose Federated Shared Parameter Correction (FedSPC), a modular correction method for PFL. FedSPC applies control-variate correction only to the shared parameters of a given PFL method, while leaving personalized parameters unchanged. It can be integrated into three common PFL settings: shared feature extractors, shared classifiers, and fully shared models with local regularization. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and Tiny-ImageNet with ViT, ResNet-34, and VGG-11 show that FedSPC improves performance across representative PFL methods, including FedPer, FedRep, FedBABU, LG-FedAvg, and Ditto.

</details>


### [119] [The Weight Norm Sets the Grokking Timescale: A Causal Delay Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13753)
*Truong Xuan Khanh,Doan Hoang Viet,Luu Duc Trung,Phan Thanh Duc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过干预训练过程中的权重范数，揭示了神经网络中“grokking”现象（延迟泛化）的机制：权重范数达到临界值Wc是触发grokking的关键，但将范数钳制在Wc以上会以指数方式延迟泛化，而非阻止它。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界对权重范数是否是grokking延迟泛化的原因存在争议：部分研究认为存在一个临界范数触发转变，另一些则观察到grokking发生时不伴随固定范数。本文旨在通过主动干预（而非被动观测）范数来澄清这一矛盾，并量化范数对延迟的因果效应。

Method: 在带权重衰减的模块化算术任务上训练Transformer。首先在自由训练下观测到grokking发生在权重范数达到临界值Wc时（Wc跨种子和学习率稳定，CV仅1-2%，且随模数呈幂律增长）。然后进行干预实验：将权重范数钳制在Wc的固定倍数ρ处，测量grokking延迟T_grok与ρ的关系，并对比有无LayerNorm的情况。

Result: 自由训练下Wc高度稳健。钳制范数后，grokking仍发生，但延迟遵循T_grok ∝ exp(αρ)，单一指数α≈7.5可拟合四个模数下的延迟（R²=0.996）。在扫描范围内，范数变化引起约19倍的延迟变化，而学习率仅约2倍。将范数钳制在Wc以上会减慢而非阻止grokking。添加LayerNorm后该指数关系消失，去除LayerNorm后指数律恢复，表明LayerNorm通过解耦权重尺度与网络功能消除了这一依赖。

Conclusion: 权重范数是grokking延迟的直接因果因素：钳制范数产生的指数延迟是自由衰减范数对数延迟的“指数对应物”。该工作在模块化算术领域澄清了范数角色的争议，并提供了对grokking动力学的定量、可预测的控制手段。

Abstract: Grokking is the delayed onset of generalization in neural networks, arising long after they fit the training data. Whether the weight norm causes this delay is disputed: some studies report a critical norm at the transition, others observe grokking with no fixed norm at all. We settle this by intervening on the norm during training rather than only observing it. Under free training with weight decay, networks grok when the weight norm reaches a value Wc that varies little across seeds and learning rates (CV 1 to 2 percent) and grows with the modular base as a power law. When we instead clamp the norm to a fixed multiple rho of Wc and hold it there, the network still groks, but the delay follows T_grok proportional to exp(alpha rho). One exponent, alpha near 7.5, fits this delay across four moduli (R^2 = 0.996). Over the swept ranges the held norm moves the delay by about 19x and the learning rate by only about 2x, and holding the norm above Wc slows grokking rather than preventing it. A final LayerNorm removes the dependence by decoupling weight scale from the network function; without it the exponential law returns. This pinned-norm delay is the exponential counterpart to the logarithmic delay predicted for a freely contracting norm.

</details>


### [120] [D2H-AD: A Hybrid Model Utilizing Hyperdimensional Computing for Advanced Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13754)
*Ghazal Ghajari,Elaheh Ghajari,Ashutosh Ghimire,Saeid Ataei,Faris Alsulami,Fathi Amsaad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 D2H-AD，一种基于超维计算（HDC）的异常检测框架，将基于距离的相似度与密度感知编码统一整合，在多个基准数据集上优于五种现有基线方法，同时具备轻量、可解释、低延迟的特性，适合 TinyML 和边缘 AI 部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习和深度学习方法在异常检测中依赖大规模标注数据、计算成本高，且在边缘和高维场景下面临可扩展性挑战；现有 HDC 方法未能有效融合距离与密度信息来提升异常表征能力。

Method: 提出 D2H-AD 框架，核心是将基于距离的相似性度量与密度感知编码统一嵌入超维计算范式。通过超维编码将原始特征映射到高维分布式向量空间，再结合密度-距离联合评分机制进行异常判别。消融实验对比了在原始特征空间与超维编码空间中使用相同密度-距离评分的性能差异。

Result: 消融实验表明，仅超维编码即可带来最高 5.4% 的 ROC-AUC 提升。在五个基准数据集上，D2H-AD 在 F1-score 和 ROC-AUC 上一致优于 HDAD、ODHD、One-Class SVM、Isolation Forest 和 Autoencoders 五种基线方法，并对类别不平衡、噪声和数据复杂性表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: D2H-AD 将 HDC 的脑启发式高维表征与距离-密度联合异常评分相结合，实现了高精度、可解释、低资源消耗的异常检测，特别适用于资源受限的实时边缘场景，展示了 HDC 在动态环境异常检测中的潜力。

Abstract: Anomaly detection is a fundamental component of intelligent systems with applications in healthcare, cybersecurity, smart grids, and IoT environments. Although conventional machine learning and deep learning methods have demonstrated effectiveness in identifying anomalies, they often rely on large labeled datasets, incur high computational costs, and face scalability challenges in edge and high-dimensional settings. This paper presents D2H-AD, a novel anomaly detection framework based on Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC), a brain-inspired paradigm that represents information using high-dimensional distributed vectors. Unlike existing HDC-based methods, D2H-AD integrates distance-based similarity and density-aware encoding within a unified framework, improving anomaly representation and detection performance. Ablation studies show that hyperdimensional encoding alone yields up to 5.4% higher ROC-AUC than applying the same density-distance scoring directly in the original feature space. Furthermore, D2H-AD consistently outperforms five established baselines, namely HDAD, ODHD, One-Class SVM, Isolation Forest, and Autoencoders, across all evaluated datasets. The framework is lightweight, interpretable, and computationally efficient, making it suitable for resource-constrained and real-time applications. We validate D2H-AD on five benchmark datasets and demonstrate superior F1-score and ROC-AUC performance, together with robustness to class imbalance, noise, and data complexity. In addition to improved accuracy, D2H-AD offers scalability, a small memory footprint, and low-latency operation enabled by binary computations and a compact design. These properties make it particularly attractive for TinyML and edge AI deployments. The proposed framework highlights the potential of HDC for accurate, interpretable, and energy-efficient anomaly detection in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [121] [Beyond LoRA: Is Sparsity-Induced Adaptation Better?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13767)
*Elijah Cadenhead,Cristian McGee,Xin Li,El Houcine Bergou,Aritra Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了LoRA及变体的发展历程，提出两种更廉价、参数高效的稀疏LoRA扩展方法（cLA和c³LA），从信息论角度给出了泛化误差界，并通过大规模实证研究（11种方法、10个预训练模型、14个数据集）表明：将低秩适配限制在稀疏结构化列子空间内，在保持与参数匹配基线相当性能的同时，可节省高达10%的训练时间和15%的GPU显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LoRA及其变体虽已广泛应用，但其相对泛化能力的比较、低秩更新的结构约束如何影响适配性能等问题尚未得到系统回答。此外，如何在保持性能的前提下进一步降低LoRA类方法的计算与存储开销，也是亟待探索的方向。

Method: 首先对LoRA进行历史梳理（过去：全微调与原始LoRA；现在：各类LoRA变体）。然后提出两种稀疏诱导的参数高效扩展：(1) Cheap LoRA (cLA)，仅训练一个低秩因子，另一个因子固定（确定性或随机初始化）；(2) 链式循环变体c³LA。将cLA定位为非对称LoRA的结构化实例，即全微调的受控列子空间约束。从信息论角度为这些变体推导泛化误差下界。实证部分评估了11种微调方法在10个预训练模型和14个数据集上的表现，使用损失景观和谱分析等工具分析模型性能与泛化性。

Result: 将LoRA类参数高效微调方法的适配空间限制在稀疏结构化列子空间，在各类任务上可与参数匹配的基线方法竞争。即使采用朴素、未优化的稀疏实现，仍可减少高达10%的训练时间和15%的峰值GPU显存。提出的理论与经验泛化度量比常用分析工具更具一致性和原则性。

Conclusion: 稀疏结构化列子空间约束可作为LoRA类PEFT方法的有效设计原则，在性能、效率与泛化性之间取得有利平衡。理论泛化界的推导为该方向提供了更严谨的分析框架。cLA和c³LA虽设计简单，但在成本效益适配方面具有竞争力。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and its variants provide a memory- and compute-efficient alternative to full fine-tuning of pre-trained models. However, questions remain about the comparative generalizability of these approaches and how the structural restrictions on low-rank updates preserve effective adaptation performance. We present a historical framing, covering the past (full fine-tuning and original LoRA), the present (different variants of LoRA), and propose simpler, cheaper, parameter-efficient extensions by inducing sparsity within existing LoRA variants: Cheap LoRA (cLA), training a single low-rank factor with the other fixed (deterministically or, in its randomized variant, stochastically), and the chained circulant variant, ${c}^3$LA. We frame cLA as a structured instance of asymmetric LoRA, serving as a controlled column-subspace restriction of full fine-tuning. We derive information-theoretic generalization error bounds for these variants, marking one of the first endeavors in this area. Empirically, we evaluate 11 fine-tuning methods across 10 pre-trained models and 14 datasets, analyzing the fine-tuned models' performance and generalization using tools such as loss landscapes and spectral analysis. Despite the sensitivity of fine-tuned models to the pre-trained model, datasets, and other factors, our study suggests that restricting LoRA-based PEFT methods' adaptation to a sparse, structured column space remains competitive across tasks with their parameter-matched baselines while reducing up to 10% training time and peak GPU memory up to 15%, even with a naïve, non-optimized, sparse implementation. Our theoretical and empirical generalization measures provide a more consistent and principled approach to their cost-effective adaptation than commonly used analytical tools. Overview and code are available at: https://elicaden.github.io/Beyond_LoRA/.

</details>


### [122] [Diffusion Policy Optimization without Drifting Apart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13795)
*Haozhe Jiang,Haiwen Feng,Pieter Abbeel,Jiantao Jiao,Angjoo Kanazawa,Nika Haghtalab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散策略梯度方法训练不稳定问题，作者识别出"双重漂移"现象——变分代理优化导致ELBO与真实对数似然分离，使代理策略梯度与真实策略梯度失准。提出DiPOD框架，通过自蒸馏与策略梯度更新交织，并用on-policy ELBO正则化来维持紧界行为，显著提升训练稳定性与最终奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散策略梯度方法（如基于变分推断的策略优化）训练不稳定，难以实现可靠的策略改进。作者旨在诊断根本原因并提出解决方案，使扩散策略的后训练既稳定又高效。

Method: 首先诊断出"双重漂移"现象：优化变分代理目标会使ELBO偏离真实对数似然，导致代理策略梯度与真实期望回报梯度不对齐。基于此提出DiPOD框架：在每次扩散策略梯度更新中，加入一个on-policy ELBO正则项，并将自蒸馏（self-distillation）与策略改进梯度更新交替进行，从而在整个训练过程中维持紧界（tight-bound）行为。

Result: 在扩散语言模型后训练和连续控制扩散策略两个任务上，DiPOD显著稳定了训练过程，并获得了比先前方法更高的奖励。

Conclusion: DiPOD通过ELBO正则化有效解决了双重漂移问题，为扩散策略优化提供了一种简单、实用且可靠的算法框架，在多个领域均表现优越。

Abstract: RL post-training has become increasingly pivotal for improving diffusion policies, but existing diffusion policy-gradient methods are often unstable and cannot achieve reliable policy improvement. We identify the cause as the double-drift phenomenon: optimizing a variational surrogate can let the ELBO separate from the true log-likelihood, which then makes the resulting proxy policy gradient misaligned with the true policy gradient of expected return. We propose \textbf{DiPOD}, a diffusion policy optimization framework that maintains tight-bound behavior throughout training by interleaving self-distillation with policy-improving gradient updates. This leads to a simple and practical algorithm: augmenting each diffusion policy-gradient update with an on-policy ELBO regularizer. Across diffusion language model post-training and continuous-control diffusion policies, DiPOD substantially stabilizes training and reaches higher rewards than previous methods.

</details>


### [123] [Neural Variability Enhances Artificial Network Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13801)
*Robin Preble,Praveen Venkatesh,Stefan Mihalas,Kameron Decker Harris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了人工神经网络中相关噪声对鲁棒性的影响，发现结构化噪声能显著提升网络对对抗攻击和自然图像扰动的鲁棒性，且这种基于局部信息的机制具有生物合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑皮层神经元对重复刺激表现出显著的试次间变异性，而外周感觉神经元则高度一致，这引发了对神经随机性是否携带信息的思考。现有研究大多关注噪声对判别任务的优化，但忽略了噪声相关性的作用。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究结构化相关噪声是否能提升人工神经网络的鲁棒性。

Method: 作者利用人工神经网络在干净输入与扰动输入下激活值的协方差结构，分析结构化噪声对网络鲁棒性的影响。分别考察了自然图像扰动和对抗攻击两种场景下的噪声结构效应，并检验了不同扰动类型之间噪声结构的迁移能力。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 结构化噪声能显著提升网络鲁棒性；(2) 自然图像扰动下的鲁棒性最受益于噪声结构，但该结构在不同扰动类型间迁移性差；(3) 对抗攻击产生的噪声结构可泛化至其他攻击类型。

Conclusion: ANN激活中的结构化噪声能普遍提升鲁棒性，这为构建仅依赖局部信息的鲁棒人工神经网络提供了一种生物合理的策略。

Abstract: Neural responses in cortex exhibit substantial trial-to-trial variability in response to repeated stimuli, while peripheral sensory neurons respond far more consistently, leading many to wonder whether stochasticity may carry meaning. Existing work has argued that noise and signal correlations may be optimized for discrimination in animals, whereas artificial neural network (ANN) studies have shown similar benefits of noise in machine learning tasks, although most ANN work has neglected the effects of correlations. Here we investigate whether correlated noise improves the robustness of artificial neural networks to adversarial attacks and naturalistic image modifications. Using the covariance of activations under modified versus clean inputs, we find that structured noise may significantly improve network robustness. Robustness to naturalistic image modifications benefits most from structure, but this structure transfers poorly across modification types. In contrast, noise structure from adversarial attacks can generalize to other kinds of attacks. These results suggest that structured noise in ANN activations generally improves robustness, establishing a biologically plausible strategy for creating robust artificial neural networks that only relies on local information.

</details>


### [124] [Neural Slack Variables for Shape Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13803)
*Ruben Wiedemann,Antoine Jacquier,Lukas Gonon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"神经松弛变量"（neural slack variables），一种将函数不等式约束（如单调性、凸性）强制执行转化为回归问题的原生深度学习方法，通过耦合主网络与辅助网络实现可行性与正则性，在稠密网格测试中实现零违反，并成功应用于金融波动率曲面的无套利学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业与科学应用中，神经网络中单调性、凸性等函数不等式约束的强制执行是一个根本性挑战。传统单边惩罚方法和基于互补松弛条件的原始-对偶方法仅在违反位置提供约束梯度，导致约束满足脆弱；而通过构造保证可行性的架构大多局限于基本情形并引入额外归纳偏置。因此需要一种既能严格满足约束又不引入过度偏置的新方法。

Method: 提出"神经松弛变量"方法，属于深度学习原生的原始侧（primal-side）方法。核心思路是：联合训练一个主网络和一个辅助网络，辅助网络为约束量提供有效的目标值（即"松弛变量"），将约束强制执行转化为回归问题。具体而言，辅助网络学习输出满足约束的有效目标，主网络则通过回归这些目标来间接满足约束，从而诱导可行性和正则性，无需仅在违反点施加惩罚。

Result: 在稠密网格的单调性和凸性测试用例上，神经松弛变量实现了零测量违反（zero measured violations），而传统惩罚方法和原始-对偶基线方法均留有残余违反。此外，该方法成功实现了波动率曲面的无套利学习，解决了量化金融中的一个开放性工业挑战。

Conclusion: 神经松弛变量作为一种将约束强制执行转化为回归问题的原生方法，克服了传统惩罚和原始-对偶方法约束满足脆弱的缺陷，在严格性和实用性之间取得了突破，对需要硬约束的深度学习工业应用（如量化金融）具有重要价值。

Abstract: Enforcing functional inequality constraints such as monotonicity and convexity in neural networks is a fundamental challenge in many industrial and scientific applications. Classical one-sided penalty methods, along with primal-dual methods gated by complementary slackness, provide constraint gradients only at violated locations, resulting in fragile satisfaction. Architectures that guarantee feasibility by construction, on the other hand, remain largely limited to elementary cases and impose additional inductive biases. We introduce neural slack variables, a deep learning native primal-side approach that converts constraint enforcement into a regression problem by coupling the primary network with a jointly learned auxiliary network. The auxiliary network serves as a valid target for the primary network's constraint quantities, inducing feasibility and regularity. Neural slack variables achieve zero measured violations on dense-grid monotonicity and convexity test cases, where penalty and primal-dual baselines leave residual violations, and enable arbitrage-free learning of volatility surfaces, an open industrial challenge in quantitative finance.

</details>


### [125] [Uncertainty Estimation and Generalization Bounds for Modern Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13818)
*Luis A. Ortega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从贝叶斯视角统一了深度学习的泛化与不确定性量化研究，提出DVIP、VaLLA、FMGP三种方法，并基于PAC-贝叶斯与大偏差理论构建了连接多样性、平滑性和随机性三大泛化机制的概率框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络虽预测性能卓越，但其泛化能力和不确定性量化的理论基础仍不完善。现有的贝叶斯方法、函数空间建模与大偏差理论各自为战，缺乏统一的概率视角来同时解释并改进泛化与不确定性估计。

Method: 方法论层面：提出深度变分隐式过程(DVIP)，将隐式过程扩展至深度架构实现可扩展贝叶斯推断；提出变分线性化拉普拉斯近似(VaLLA)与固定均值高斯过程(FMGP)两种后处理方法，为预训练确定性网络赋予校准后的不确定性估计。理论层面：构建统一的PAC-贝叶斯与大偏差理论框架，形式化连接多样性、平滑性与随机性三大泛化机制。

Result: 方法论成果为深度学习提供了可扩展的贝叶斯推断工具及实用的后处理不确定性校准方案；理论成果揭示了过参数化网络泛化能力的概率本质，将三大泛化机制纳入同一数学语言。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯原理能够有效统一深度学习中泛化理论与不确定性量化两大核心问题，函数空间视角与PAC-贝叶斯/大偏差理论的结合为理解现代深度学习系统提供了深刻且实用的分析框架。

Abstract: This thesis investigates how Bayesian principles can deepen our understanding of modern deep learning systems. While neural networks achieve remarkable predictive performance, their ability to generalize and to quantify uncertainty remains only partly understood. This thesis approaches this challenge from both methodological and theoretical angles: unifying Bayesian inference, function-space modeling, and large-deviation theory under a common probabilistic perspective.
  On the methodological side, the thesis introduces the Deep Variational Implicit Process (DVIP), a scalable Bayesian framework that extends implicit processes to deep architectures. Complementing this, two post-hoc methods -- the Variational Linearized Laplace Approximation (VaLLA) and the Fixed-Mean Gaussian Process (FMGP) -- are proposed to equip pretrained deterministic networks with calibrated uncertainty estimates.
  The theoretical contributions focus on one of the central open questions in modern machine learning: why do large, over-parameterized neural networks generalize so well? To address this, the thesis develops a unified probabilistic framework that connects three key mechanisms -- diversity, smoothness, and stochasticity -- within the language of PAC-Bayesian and large-deviation theory.

</details>


### [126] [A Stationarity-and-Coupling Criterion for Training-Free Time-Lagged Spectral Embeddings of Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13823)
*Siddharth Pal,Viktoria Rojkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种无需训练的固定长度多元时间序列描述符 D(τ)，并提出了一个可证伪的适用性判据。该判据基于平稳高斯VAR(1)模型，指出当信号近似平稳且类别信息存在于跨通道时序耦合（而非边际通道功率）中时，D(τ) 能有效分类。作者给出了一个由ADF平稳性检验和功率基线饱和检验组成的"预飞测试"，在四个满足条件的基准上表现优异，在三个违反条件的场景中按预期失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多元时间序列分类方法通常依赖于大量训练参数，作者希望探究是否存在一种完全无需训练的描述符，并更重要的是，建立一个可事先判断该描述符何时有效的可证伪判据，从而在部署前即可预测其适用性。

Method: 描述符 D(τ) 的构建流程：(1) 对多元时间序列计算时滞相关矩阵（时间滞后 τ）；(2) 按Marchenko-Pastur边界截断特征值，仅保留信号承载的特征值；(3) 将截断后的矩阵与类别质心进行余弦相似度比较进行分类（零学习参数）。理论分析基于平稳高斯VAR(1)模型，推导出三个推论：可区分性条件、静态协方差（τ=0）退化为随机水平的原因、以及平稳但功率可区分范式为何使描述符失效。基于此设计了预飞测试：增强迪基-富勒平稳性检验 + 功率基线饱和检验。

Result: 在满足判据的四个数据集上：Sleep-EDF（88.5±4.5%，20人留一交叉验证，单CPU线程）、BCI-IV-2a、MIT-BIH、ESC-50上，D(τ) 以极低计算成本达到与强基线可竞争的性能。在三个违反判据的数据集上：非平稳ERP、金融波动率、可穿戴应激数据，描述符如预飞测试所预测的那样失败。负面结果被视为更具信息量的贡献。

Conclusion: D(τ) 并非最准确的表示方法，其价值在于它是一个紧凑、无需训练的嵌入，且其有效域可事先知晓。核心贡献是首次为无训练描述符建立了一个可操作的、可证伪的适用性判据，使从业者能在投入训练资源之前判断该方法是否值得尝试。

Abstract: We study training-free fixed-length descriptors for multivariate time series and ask not merely whether such a descriptor performs well, but when it can be expected to work at all. Our object of study is $D(τ)$, built from a time-lagged correlation matrix truncated at the Marchenko-Pastur edge so that only signal-bearing eigenvalues survive and classified by cosine similarity to class centroids with zero learned parameters. The central contribution is not the descriptor but a falsifiable applicability criterion for it. Working from a stationary Gaussian VAR(1) model, we argue that $D(τ)$ separates two classes when the signals are approximately stationary and the class information lives in their cross-channel temporal coupling rather than in marginal per-channel power. We derive, semi-formally, three consequences: a distinguishability condition, why the static ($τ=0$) covariance collapses to chance, and why a stationary but power-discriminated paradigm defeats the descriptor. The criterion is operational: a two-part pre-flight test -- an augmented Dickey-Fuller stationarity check and a power-baseline saturation check -- predicts applicability before any training. We validate both halves on a mixed assortment. On four paradigms that satisfy the criterion (Sleep-EDF, BCI-IV-2a, MIT-BIH, ESC-50) the descriptor is competitive with strong baselines at a fraction of their cost, reaching $88.5\pm4.5\%$ under 20-subject leave-one-subject-out on Sleep-EDF on a single CPU thread. On three that violate it -- non-stationary ERPs, and financial-volatility and wearable-stress regimes that are power-discriminated -- it fails exactly as the pre-flight predicts, and these negatives are the more informative half. We are explicit that $D(τ)$ is not the most accurate representation; its value is a compact, training-free embedding whose domain of validity is known in advance.

</details>


### [127] [Attention-Based Estimation of the Individual Treatment Benefit Probability under Dose Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13821)
*Lev V. Utkin,Andrei V. Konstantinov,Stanislav K. Kogan,Natalya M. Verbova,Maksim I. Goriunov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dose-AIPTB框架，用于离散剂量分配下有序结局的个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）估计。该方法将问题转化为对未观测个体治疗效应符号的二分类任务，通过协变量相似的成对比较构建伪标签，并利用注意力机制或Nadaraya-Watson核回归进行聚合，从而支持多离散剂量水平，突破传统二值治疗限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IPTB估计方法主要局限于二值治疗（治疗vs对照）场景，而临床实践中普遍存在多剂量干预。需要一种通用框架，能够在离散剂量分配下估计有序结局的个体化治疗获益概率，为个性化剂量选择提供依据。

Method: 提出Dose-AIPTB框架：（1）将IPTB估计重新表述为对未观测个体治疗效应符号的二分类问题；（2）利用协变量相似的成对比较构建伪标签；（3）通过注意力机制或Nadaraya-Watson核回归对伪标签进行聚合；（4）框架自然适应多离散剂量水平，超越传统二值治疗范式。

Result: 在真实数据和合成数据上，涵盖协变量偏移、不同样本量和异质性结局条件下的数值实验表明，基于注意力机制的聚合方法一致优于核回归替代方案。

Conclusion: Dose-AIPTB为基于个体层面获益概率的个性化剂量选择提供了理论基础，注意力聚合策略在性能上优于核方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Estimating the probability that a treatment outperforms a control for an individual patient, called the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB), offers a clinically intuitive alternative to population-average metrics. However, existing methods for IPTB estimation are largely confined to binary treatment settings, despite the prevalence of dose-varying interventions in clinical practice. We propose a general framework for IPTB estimation with ordinal outcomes under discrete dose assignments, called Dose-AIPTB (Dose Attention-based IPTB). Our approach recasts the problem as binary classification over the unobserved sign of the individual treatment effect, constructing pseudo-labels from covariate-similar pairwise comparisons and aggregating them via attention mechanisms or Nadaraya-Watson kernel regression. This formulation naturally accommodates multiple discrete dose levels, extending beyond the binary treatment paradigm. Through numerical experiments on real-world and synthetic data under covariate shift, varying sample sizes, and heterogeneous outcomes, we demonstrate that attention-based aggregation consistently outperforms kernel alternatives. The framework provides a foundation for personalized dose selection grounded in individual-level benefit probabilities. Codes implementing the model are publicly available at https://github.com/NTAILab/AIPTBDose.

</details>


### [128] [DTVEM-RE: A Hierarchical Random-Effects Extension of the Differential Time-Varying Effect Model for Person-Specific Multi-Lag Estimation in Intensive Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14116)
*Amartya Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DTVEM-RE将原始DTVEM扩展为允许每个人拥有各自滞后系数的个体特异性版本，结合贝叶斯分层VAR和连续时间Ornstein-Uhlenbeck模型，实现了首个个体特异性的DTVEM式滞后检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始DTVEM假设所有人共享相同的滞后结构，这与现代临床研究中"个体存在差异"的基本前提相矛盾。原作者也将解决这一问题列为未来工作。因此，需要一种允许个体间滞后系数异质性的方法。

Method: 提出DTVEM-RE扩展，包含两种验证步骤版本：(1) Stan中的离散时间分层贝叶斯向量自回归(Bayesian VAR)，跨个体池化并给出校准的不确定性；(2) ctsem中的连续时间逐人Ornstein-Uhlenbeck模型，直接处理非等间距测量。还开发了多滞后版本以检测不同滞后阶数的个体差异。

Result: 四项结果：(1) 模拟显示贝叶斯版本恢复个体间离散度τ_a的偏差低于0.01，覆盖率达90-93%。(2) 在Fisher等人(2017)的EMA数据集(N=40)上，个体特异性滞后1效应在三个情绪项目上跨量级变化，贝叶斯与GAMM估计高度一致(r=0.87-0.92)。(3) DTVEM-RE在四种离散时间方法中给出最佳的一步前瞻预测。(4) 多滞后版本显示所有九个τ_k值的可信区间均排除零，且个体差异最大的滞后阶数因项目而异，这是仅滞后1的方法(如mlVAR)无法检测的。两种版本在个体特异性滞后1估计上几乎完全一致(r≥0.995)。

Conclusion: DTVEM-RE是首个实现个体特异性DTVEM式滞后检测的方法，并将标准DTVEM作为其特例包含在内，为密集纵向数据的个体差异研究提供了更灵活、更精确的建模框架。

Abstract: The Differential Time-Varying Effect Model (DTVEM) of Jacobson et al. (2019) is a popular tool for finding the best time lag in intensive longitudinal data, but it assumes everyone shares the same lag structure. The original authors named fixing this as future work, and it clashes with the premise of modern clinical research, which is that people differ. We present DTVEM-RE, an extension that lets each person have their own lag coefficients, with two versions of the confirmatory step: a discrete-time hierarchical Bayesian VAR in Stan, which pools across people and gives calibrated uncertainty, and a continuous-time per-person Ornstein-Uhlenbeck model in ctsem, which handles unevenly spaced beeps directly. We report four results. A simulation shows the Bayesian version recovers the between-person spread tau_a with bias below 0.01 and coverage of 90 to 93 percent. On the Fisher et al. (2017) EMA dataset (N=40), person-specific lag-1 effects vary by an order of magnitude across three mood items, the Bayesian and GAMM estimates agree closely (r=0.87 to 0.92), and DTVEM-RE gives the best one-step-ahead prediction among four discrete-time methods. A multi-lag version shows all nine tau_k values have credible intervals excluding zero, and the lag where people differ most changes across items, something lag-1-only methods like mlVAR cannot detect. Finally, the two versions agree almost exactly on person-specific lag-1 estimates (r >= 0.995), differing only as shrinkage predicts. DTVEM-RE is, to our knowledge, the first person-specific implementation of DTVEM-style lag detection, and it contains standard DTVEM as a special case.

</details>


### [129] [Lyapunov-Based Sample Complexity Analysis for Weakly-Coupled MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14095)
*Tianhao Wu,Matthew Zurek,Weina Wang,Qiaomin Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究弱耦合MDP和Restless Bandit在生成模型下的样本复杂度，通过Lyapunov分析框架和LP扰动分析，证明多项式样本复杂度即可学到近最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将弱耦合MDP直接归约为表格型MDP会导致状态-动作空间随臂数N指数增长，样本复杂度极高。作者希望利用弱耦合结构，以多项式复杂度学到近最优策略。

Method: 采用plug-in方法：从数据估计经验模型后应用高效规划算法。核心技术创新是Lyapunov分析框架，用显式构造的Lyapunov函数替代难以控制的bias函数，并通过漂移转移技术在真实模型与经验模型之间建立联系。另一关键步骤是对底层线性规划松弛的细粒度扰动分析。

Result: 对完全异质WCMDP，首次建立多项式复杂度的有限样本PAC保证，最优性间隙为O(1/√N)；对同质Restless Bandit，在温和结构假设下可实现更小的最优性间隙。

Conclusion: 利用弱耦合结构可显著降低学习复杂度；Lyapunov分析框架和LP扰动分析为弱耦合系统提供了通用的分析工具。

Abstract: We study the sample complexity of learning in average-reward weakly-coupled Markov decision processes (WCMDPs) and Restless Bandits (RBs) under a generative model. Naive reduction to a tabular MDP leads to high complexity bounds as the state-action space is exponentially large in the number of arms $N$. By exploiting the weakly coupled structure, we show that near-optimal policies can be learned with sample and computational complexities that are polynomial in $N$. Specifically, we analyze the plug-in approach, which applies an efficient planning algorithm to an empirical model estimated from data. For fully heterogeneous WCMDPs, we establish the first finite-sample PAC guarantee with polynomial complexity and an $O(1/\sqrt{N})$ optimality gap. For homogeneous RBs, we further prove that a smaller optimality gap is achievable under mild structural assumptions. A primary technical contribution of our work is a novel Lyapunov-based analysis framework. Unlike classical approaches that rely on the difficult-to-control bias function, our framework uses an explicitly constructed Lyapunov function along with a drift transfer technique between the true and empirical models. A key step of independent interest in our framework is a fine-grained perturbation analysis for the underlying linear programming (LP) relaxation, which provides a general tool for analyzing LP-based policies and weakly-coupled systems.

</details>


### [130] [SuperThoughts: Reasoning Tokens in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13862)
*Zheyang Xiong,Shivam Garg,Max Yu,Vaishnavi Shrivastava,Haoyu Zhao,Anastasios Kyrillidis,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SuperThoughts 将连续的两个 CoT token 压缩为单个潜在表示，并通过轻量级多 token 预测模块在一步内解码两个 token，在保持离散监督的同时将推理吞吐量翻倍，实现约 20-30% 的 CoT 长度缩减，准确率仅轻微下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维（CoT）推理虽能提升 LLM 的问题求解能力，但顺序生成 token 带来高昂计算开销。现有连续潜在空间推理方法存在训练不稳定、缺乏监督信号、难以扩展到复杂长程任务等问题。

Method: 提出 SuperThoughts：将连续两个 CoT token 压缩为单个潜在表示，通过轻量级 Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) 模块一步解码两个 token。训练时保留离散 token 监督信号，推理时采用基于置信度的自适应机制，在不确定时回退到标准解码。

Result: 在 MATH500、AMC、OlympiadBench 和 GPQA-Diamond 上，基于 Qwen2.5-Math 系列（1.5B/7B/14B）微调评估，实现约 20-30% 的 CoT 长度缩减，大多数任务上准确率仅下降 1-2 个百分点。

Conclusion: SuperThoughts 在保持离散监督训练稳定性的同时，有效压缩 CoT 推理长度并提升吞吐量，为长链推理的高效化提供了一条可行的折中方案。

Abstract: Long Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning improves LLM problem-solving but is computationally expensive due to sequential token generation. While recent works explore reasoning in continuous latent spaces to bypass discrete token generation, they often struggle with training stability and fail to scale to complex, long-horizon tasks due to lack of supervision signal. We propose SuperThoughts, which compresses pairs of consecutive CoT tokens into single latent representations and decodes two tokens per step via a lightweight Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) module. This preserves discrete token supervision at training time while doubling throughput at inference time. We finetune Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-Math-14B-Instruct, and evaluate on MATH500, AMC, OlympiadBench, and GPQA-Diamond. With a confidence-based adaptive mechanism that falls back to standard decoding when uncertain, SuperThoughts achieves $\sim$20--30\% CoT length reduction while maintaining accuracy with minimal degradation (1-2 points accuracy drop on most tasks).

</details>


### [131] [Federated Learning for Feature Generalization with Convex Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14416)
*Dongwon Kim,Donghee Kim,Sung Kuk Shyn,Kwangsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCONST 提出一种基于全局模型参数强度的自适应更新幅度调节方法，通过线性凸约束保持训练稳定性，并利用梯度信噪比(GSNR)分析增强特征可迁移性，从而缓解联邦学习中的过拟合问题，提升跨异构数据的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端数据异构导致两个核心问题：(1) 本地模型容易过拟合本地数据分布；(2) 即使特征具有可迁移性，在聚合过程中也可能被扭曲。现有方法未能有效解决聚合阶段对已学好参数的过度强调和对欠学习参数的忽视。

Method: 提出 FedCONST 框架，核心机制包括：(1) 基于全局模型参数强度自适应调节各参数的更新幅度——抑制已充分学习参数的更新，增强欠学习参数的更新；(2) 引入线性凸约束，在聚合过程中保持训练稳定性并保留本地学到的泛化能力；(3) 采用梯度信噪比(GSNR)分析，从理论上验证方法对特征可迁移性和鲁棒性的提升效果。

Result: FedCONST 在多种异构联邦学习场景下取得了最先进的性能，有效对齐了本地与全局优化目标，显著缓解了过拟合，提升了模型的泛化能力。GSNR 分析进一步从理论上证实了该方法在增强特征可迁移性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: FedCONST 通过自适应调节更新幅度并结合凸约束聚合，成功弥合了本地训练与全局聚合之间的目标偏差，为解决数据异构联邦学习中的过拟合与泛化难题提供了有效方案，达到了最先进水平。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) often struggles with generalization due to heterogeneous client data. Local models are prone to overfitting their local data distributions, and even transferable features can be distorted during aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose FedCONST, an approach that adaptively modulates update magnitudes based on the parameter strength of the global model. This prevents over-emphasizing well-learned parameters while reinforcing underdeveloped ones. Specifically, FedCONST employs linear convex constraints to ensure training stability and preserve locally learned generalization capabilities during aggregation. A Gradient Signal to Noise Ratio (GSNR) analysis further validates the effectiveness of FedCONST in enhancing feature transferability and robustness. As a result, FedCONST effectively aligns local and global objectives, mitigating overfitting and promoting stronger generalization across diverse FL environments, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [132] [Muon$^p$: Muon with Fractional Spectral Powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13867)
*Yihe Dong,Will Sawin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon优化器通过将梯度$G=USV^\top$替换为其极因子$UV^\top$来展平奇异谱，但完全展平丢弃了可能有用的奇异值信息。本文提出Muon$^p$，采用分数谱幂更新$US^pV^\top$（$p\in(0,1)$有理数），在Muon与梯度下降之间插值。作者证明了分数谱幂无法通过固定单变量多项式迭代计算，并推导出低阶奇次双变量递推公式，仅用矩阵乘法即可近似$US^pV^\top$。Muon$^p$在Schatten $q$-范数下最大化损失的线性改善。实验表明Muon$^p$在十亿级模型微调中显著改善验证困惑度和下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器虽广受欢迎，但其将梯度完全极分解（$UV^\top$）的做法丢弃了奇异值信息，这些信息可能对模型自适应至关重要。作者希望设计一种在保留Muon计算优势的同时，能部分保留奇异谱信息的新优化器。

Method: 核心方法分为三部分：(1) 提出Muon$^p$，用$US^pV^\top$替代$UV^\top$，其中$p\in(0,1)$为有理数，实现Muon与梯度下降的平滑插值；(2) 理论证明分数谱幂无法由固定单变量多项式迭代计算，进而推导出低阶奇次双变量矩阵递推公式来近似$US^pV^\top$，保持纯矩阵乘法结构；(3) 理论分析表明Muon$^p$在Schatten $q$-范数（$q=1+1/p$）下最大化损失的线性改善，并从谱几何角度分析其适用条件。

Result: 在十亿级规模模型的微调实验中，Muon$^p$在验证困惑度和下游任务性能上均优于现有方法。理论分析揭示了保留奇异谱信息可带来显著增益的条件，并通过谱几何视角阐明了Muon$^p$不适用的情况。

Conclusion: Muon$^p$成功在Muon和梯度下降之间建立了有原则的插值方案，证明了部分保留奇异值信息对优化有重要意义。通过巧妙的矩阵递推近似，该方法在不增加计算复杂度的前提下实现了性能提升，为自适应优化器设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Muon is an increasingly widely used optimizer that replaces a gradient $G=USV^\top$ with its polar factor $UV^\top$, thereby flattening the singular spectrum. However, full flattening discards singular-value information that may matter for adaptation. We introduce Muon$^p$, a Muon-style optimizer that instead uses fractional spectral-power updates $US^pV^\top$ for rational $p\in(0,1)$, interpolating between Muon and gradient descent. To make it practical, we prove that fractional spectral powers cannot be computed by any fixed univariate polynomial iteration, and furthermore derive low-degree odd bivariate recurrences that approximate $US^pV^\top$ using only matrix multiplications, preserving Muon's matrix-multiplication-only structure and compute complexity. We show that Muon$^p$ maximizes the linear improvement in loss under the Schatten $q$-norm for $q=1+\frac{1}{p}$. Empirically, Muon$^p$ is especially effective for finetuning: on billion-scale models, Muon$^p$ improves validation perplexity and downstream task performance. We further analyze when Muon$^p$ is less suitable, through the lens of spectral geometry. Our results reveal important insights on when preserving the singular spectrum can bring significant gains, and introduce a principled way to achieve them.

</details>


### [133] [Natively Unlearnable Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13873)
*Gaurav R. Ghosal,Pratyush Maini,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NULLs（Natively Unlearnable LLMs）提出一种原生支持数据源级别遗忘的大语言模型架构：通过共享骨干神经元与稀疏激活的"汇"（sinks）协同训练，使源特定信息自然聚集在汇中，部署时仅需禁用对应汇即可实现遗忘，无需梯度更新或保留数据。该方法在Wikipedia 600万文章上验证有效，并在哈利波特案例中展示了对对抗提取和再学习的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器遗忘（unlearning）面临两难困境：数据源贡献在模型参数中相互纠缠，使其难以隔离移除；而将不同数据源的贡献分配到不相交的参数上虽便于移除，却又阻碍了跨数据源的联合学习。作者旨在设计一种能同时满足"隔离源特定贡献"与"跨源联合学习"这两个对立目标的模型架构。

Method: 提出NULLs架构：训练一组共享的骨干（backbone）神经元和一个稀疏激活的汇（sinks）池。每个数据源在训练中被分配若干专属汇神经元。通过稀疏激活机制，源特定信息自然流向对应汇中，而跨源共享信息则积累在骨干中。遗忘时仅需在部署阶段禁用目标数据源对应的汇，无需任何梯度更新或访问保留数据集。

Result: 在Wikipedia约600万篇文章上验证：单篇文章遗忘可移除其特定知识，同时保留与语义相关文章共享的事实，效果接近从头重新训练。哈利波特书籍遗忘案例中，NULLs能抵抗对抗性知识提取和遗忘后重新学习的攻击。同时在下游基准测试中保持与标准Transformer相当的通用语言能力。

Conclusion: 数据源级别的遗忘不应是事后补救方案，而可以原生嵌入LLM训练过程中，同时保留共享表征学习的优势。

Abstract: Unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data sources, but this has proved challenging because the contributions of different sources are entangled within the model. Isolating source contributions to disjoint parameters makes removal easier, though it obstructs joint learning across sources. We propose NULLs (Natively Unlearnable LLMs), a model class that satisfies the two opposing goals of isolating source-specific contributions and learning jointly across sources, by training a set of shared backbone neurons alongside a pool of sparsely activated sinks. During training, information specific to a source naturally concentrates in its sinks while information shared across sources accumulates in the backbone. A source is then unlearned at deployment by disabling its corresponding sinks, with no gradient updates and no access to the retained data. We show that NULLs scales to Wikipedia's ~6M articles, isolating each as an independent source. Unlearning a single article removes knowledge specific to it while preserving facts shared with semantically related articles, closely matching retraining from scratch. We note that unlearning with NULLs is also robust: in a case study of unlearning the Harry Potter books, NULLs resists both adversarial extraction and relearning that reverses post-hoc unlearning. Finally, NULLs preserves general language capabilities, matching a standard transformer on downstream benchmarks. Together, these results suggest that source-level unlearning need not be an afterthought. It can be built natively into LLM training while retaining the benefits of shared representation learning.

</details>


### [134] [Optimal Hidden-Target Learning for Online Inventory Optimization on General Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14679)
*Anthony Pineci,Yunzong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在线库存优化（OIO）的通用最优方法：维护一个在线学习器选择的隐藏目标，并将其投影到当前可行的order-up-to集合上。通过引入"范数对齐原理"，将状态依赖归约为标量队列控制，在任意有界凸容量集上获得了改进的遗憾界，并通过合成和真实数据实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OIO是具有物理记忆的在线凸优化，库存结转使得可行动作集依赖于过去。现有方法仅在单一线性容量约束下有效，亟需一种能够在任意有界凸容量集上实现最优遗憾界、并适用于强凸损失和动态遗憾设置的通用方法。

Method: 核心方法基于隐藏目标投影原则：在线学习器维护一个隐藏目标，每步将其投影到当前可行的order-up-to集合上执行。基础学习器采用在线梯度下降（OGD）。关键分析工具是"范数对齐原理"：选择从隐藏目标到可行集的距离作为状态变量，并以与投影相同的范数度量。在该范数对齐下，该距离沿路径演化为一个标量队列，目标移动视为到达，共同需求视为服务，从而将多维状态依赖归约为一维队列控制。

Result: 理论上：（1）在任意有界凸容量集上，遗憾界对共同需求概率的依赖从逆线性的改进为逆平方根，并证明匹配下界；（2）对强凸损失首次给出多对数遗憾界；（3）首次给出适应欧几里得路径变化的动态遗憾界。实验上，合成数据和真实库存数据验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 隐藏目标投影原则是OIO在任意有界凸容量集上的最优方法。范数对齐原理通过将多维状态依赖简化为标量队列控制，突破了以往按产品分别处理方法的局限，统一并推广了OIO的理论保证。该框架为在线库存优化提供了简洁、通用且理论完备的解决方案。

Abstract: Online inventory optimization (OIO) is online convex optimization with physical memory: inventory carryover makes the feasible action set depend on the past. A natural principle, used in stochastic inventory learning and recently in OIO under a single linear capacity constraint, is to maintain a hidden target chosen by an online learner and implement its projection onto the currently feasible order-up-to set. We prove that this simple principle is optimal for OIO on arbitrary bounded convex capacity sets. With online gradient descent as the base learner, the method improves the best known regret guarantee for OIO on general convex sets from inverse to inverse-square-root dependence on the common-demand probability, and we prove a matching lower bound. The same principle gives the first polylogarithmic regret guarantee for strongly convex losses and the first dynamic regret guarantee adapting to Euclidean path variation on general convex capacity sets.
  The analysis introduces a norm alignment principle: the right state variable is the distance from the hidden target to the feasible set, measured in the same norm as the projection. Under norm alignment, this distance evolves pathwise as a scalar queue, with target movement as arrival and common demand as service. This reduction to one-dimensional queue control resolves the state dependence and extends the guarantees to general convex capacity sets, beyond the reach of prior productwise approaches. Experiments on synthetic and real-world inventory data corroborate the theory.

</details>


### [135] [A Longitudinal Attribute-Conditioned Neural Network for Modeling Health-State Transition Probabilities in Temporally Irregular Data: The LANTERN Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13880)
*Bright Kwaku Manu,Beckett Sterner,Petar Jevtic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对不规则纵向健康数据，提出了一种校准良好的多状态转移概率估计器，可输出四种健康状态（健康、轻度失能、重度失能、死亡）的概率分布，并按年龄组聚合为转移矩阵。基于HRS数据与多项基准对比，所提方法在重度失能判别、校准性和转移矩阵误差方面均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统精算多状态模型（Markov、semi-Markov、比例风险）对不规则纵向健康数据中非线性衰老模式和异质协变量历史的建模能力受限，需要一个既能灵活学习个体健康史、又能产出可靠群体转移矩阵的估计方法。

Method: 提出结构化机器学习估计器：从个体健康史和观测间隔中学习，以人口和社会经济属性为条件，输出下一观测状态的概率分布（四状态）。个体概率按年龄组和起始状态聚合，形成可直接用于精算队列预测的转移矩阵。

Result: 在HRS数据的测试集上，所提方法相比逻辑回归、梯度提升树、循环神经网络和末态持续基准：(1) 提升了重度失能判别能力；(2) 保持了良好的校准性；(3) 在所有模型中取得最低的转移矩阵误差。

Conclusion: 结构化机器学习估计器可同时满足判别力、校准性和投影保真度的要求，支持长期护理转移建模，为失能保险定价、准备金和偿付能力评估提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of long-term care transition probabilities is central to disability insurance pricing, reserving, and solvency assessment. Classical actuarial multi-state models commonly rely on Markov, semi-Markov, or proportional-hazard specifications, which provide a direct connection to cohort projection but may be restrictive for irregular longitudinal health data with nonlinear aging patterns and heterogeneous covariate histories. This paper develops a well-calibrated estimator of multi-state transition probabilities for irregular longitudinal health data. The model learns from individual health history, incorporates the time elapsed between observations, and conditions transition probabilities on demographic and socioeconomic attributes. It produces a valid probability distribution over the next observed health state, with four possible states: healthy, mild disability, severe disability, and death. Individual probabilities are aggregated by age group and origin state to form transition matrices compatible with actuarial cohort projection. Using longitudinal data from the Health and Retirement Study, we compare the proposed estimator with logistic regression, gradient-boosted trees, a recurrent neural network, and a last-state persistence benchmark. The evaluation considers probabilistic accuracy, endpoint discrimination and calibration for severe disability and death, risk concentration, and transition matrix error after aggregation. The proposed estimator improves severe disability discrimination relative to logistic regression and gradient-boosted tree benchmarks, maintains strong calibration, and yields the lowest transition matrix error among the evaluated models in the held-out test analysis. Results show that a structured machine learning estimator can support long-term care transition modeling when judged by calibration and projection fidelity, beyond discrimination.

</details>


### [136] [Gefen: Optimized Stochastic Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13894)
*Nadav Benedek,Tomer Koren,Ohad Fried*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Gefen 提出一种内存高效优化器，通过学习码本量化一阶矩并在参数块间共享二阶矩估计，将 AdamW 的内存占用降低约 8 倍（每十亿参数减少 6.5 GiB），同时保持相同性能。其理论依据是大型混合 Hessian 项约束平方梯度比趋近于 1，使得 Hessian 对齐的参数天然适合共享二阶矩统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdamW 作为现代深度学习的默认优化器，其一阶和二阶矩状态需要额外存储约两倍于模型参数大小的缓冲区，在大规模训练中造成显著的内存瓶颈。本文旨在在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅削减优化器状态的内存开销。

Method: Gefen 包含两个核心技术：(1) 基于理论分析，发现大混合 Hessian 项迫使 Hessian 对齐参数的平方梯度比趋于 1，因此自动依据初始平方梯度推断参数块结构，在块内共享二阶矩估计，无需架构特定元数据或额外超参数；(2) 采用精确的直方图动态规划量化方法学习码本，对一阶矩进行量化压缩，并复用相同的块结构进行一阶矩缩放。两种策略协同实现约 8 倍内存压缩。

Result: 在多种实验中，Gefen 在所有对比的类 AdamW 方法中实现了最低的峰值优化器内存占用，同时保持 AdamW 级别的性能。在 FSDP 和 DDP 训练中，降低的内存占用允许更大的 microbatch，相比 AdamW 显著提升了吞吐量。

Conclusion: Gefen 可作为 AdamW 的即插即用替代方案，以更低的内存占用实现同等性能，从而支持训练更大模型或使用更大批次规模，具有实际部署价值。代码已开源。

Abstract: AdamW is a default optimizer for modern deep learning, but its first and second moment states add roughly two parameter-sized buffers to training memory. We propose Gefen, a memory-efficient optimizer that automatically shares second-moment estimates across parameter blocks and quantizes the first moment using a learned codebook, thereby reducing AdamW's memory footprint by ~8x while maintaining the same performance, corresponding to a reduction of 6.5 GiB per billion parameters. The method is motivated by a theoretical result showing that large mixed Hessian entries constrain the ratio of squared gradients toward one, suggesting that Hessian-aligned parameters are natural candidates for sharing second-moment statistics. Since computing Hessians is impractical at scale, Gefen infers block structure from the initial squared gradients, requiring no architecture-specific metadata or hyperparameters beyond AdamW defaults. Gefen learns an exact histogram-based dynamic-programming quantization codebook and reuses the same blocks for first-moment scaling. Across diverse experiments, Gefen achieves the lowest peak optimizer memory among the compared AdamW-like methods while maintaining AdamW-level performance. In FSDP and DDP training, the reduced memory footprint enables larger microbatches and improves throughput significantly over AdamW, providing a practical drop-in replacement with lower memory usage that can increase throughput and enable training larger models or using larger batch sizes. We provide the complete Python implementation, including fused CUDA kernels at https://github.com/ndvbd/Gefen

</details>


### [137] [SpikF-GO: Spiking Fourier Graph Operators for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13901)
*Jafar Bakhshaliyev,Niels Landwehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpikF-GO 将图神经网络与脉冲神经网络结合，用于多变量时间序列预测。它通过超变量图（hypervariate graph）将每个标量观测转化为图节点，并引入 Hard Concrete 频率门和 Complex LIF 门在傅里叶域进行稀疏、事件驱动的谱处理，首次将基于图的多元建模引入脉冲域，并在统一协议下取得最优平均排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN时间序列预测方法独立处理各变量，缺乏对多变量间依赖关系的显式建模能力，这在变量间相关性蕴含重要预测信息的多元场景中构成关键瓶颈。同时，缺乏统一的实验协议来公平比较各类SNN预测架构。

Method: 1）超变量图构建：将每个标量观测定义为图节点，形成跨变量连通图；2）Hard Concrete 频率门：在傅里叶域实现可学习的稀疏频率选择；3）Complex LIF 门：对实部和虚部分别施加独立的脉冲神经元，保持整个谱域的二值化、事件驱动计算；4）变体引入 Central Pattern Generator 位置编码以增强长程时序建模；5）在8个基准数据集上建立统一实验协议进行公平比较。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上，SpikF-GO 在所有SNN方法中取得最佳平均排名，性能优于其ANN对应模型 FourierGNN，且能耗更低。在显著压缩嵌入维度的情况下仍保持有竞争力的精度，从而实现显著节能。

Conclusion: SpikF-GO 是首批将基于图的多元建模引入SNN时间序列预测的工作之一，也是首个在统一实验协议下对SNN预测架构进行全面比较的工作。结果表明脉冲驱动的图谱处理在多变量时序预测中兼具高精度与高能效。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have emerged as an energy-efficient alternative to conventional neural networks, demonstrating strong performance in computer vision and robotics. More recently, SNNs have been applied to time series forecasting (TSF), with methods exploring spiking temporal backbones, spike-compatible positional encodings, Fourier-domain processing, and redesigned neuron dynamics. However, existing SNN forecasting approaches process variables independently, lacking explicit mechanisms for modeling inter-variable dependencies. This is a critical limitation in multivariate settings, where cross-variable correlations carry substantial predictive information. We propose Spiking Fourier Graph Operators (SpikF-GO), which addresses this gap by combining a hypervariate graph formulation in which every scalar observation becomes a graph node with spike-driven spectral processing. SpikF-GO introduces a Hard Concrete frequency gate for learnable sparse frequency selection and a Complex LIF gate that applies independent spiking neurons to real and imaginary Fourier components, preserving binary, event-driven computation throughout the spectral domain. We further present a variant incorporating Central Pattern Generator-based positional encodings for stronger long-range temporal modeling. Evaluated on eight benchmarks under a unified experimental protocol, SpikF-GO achieves the best average rank among all SNN methods and outperforms its ANN counterpart, FourierGNN, at reduced energy cost. SpikF-GO maintains competitive accuracy even at substantially smaller embedding dimensions, thereby achieving significant energy reductions. To our knowledge, this is among the first works to bring graph-based multivariate modeling into the spiking domain for TSF and the first to provide a unified comparison across SNN forecasting architectures under a common experimental protocol.

</details>


### [138] [Smoothing Dark Areas in Molecular Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13955)
*Xi Wang,Jiahan Li,Yuxuan Xia,Yingcheng Wu,Shaoyi Zheng,Shengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TopVAE 是一种拓扑优化的分子变分自编码器，通过在训练阶段让解码器内化结构与化学约束，消除潜在空间中的"暗区"（解码产生无效分子的区域），从而大幅提升3D分子生成中扩散模型的离后验鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子VAE基于重建目标训练，无法保证潜在空间在训练分布之外仍能解码为有效分子，导致扩散采样时落入"暗区"——即潜在空间中可被采样到但解码出断裂或化学无效分子的区域。由于分子生成对结构与化学精度要求极高，这一问题尤为严重。

Method: 提出TopVAE，在训练期间让解码器内部化结构与化学约束（而非依赖测试时校正），通过拓扑优化减少潜在空间中的暗区。训练完成后与标准DiT扩散模型配对进行分子生成。

Result: 在QM9数据集上FCD-3D降低77%，达到最高的V&C指标；在GEOM-Drugs上FCD-3D降低52%；在零样本骨架补全（scaffold inpainting）任务上生成分子的稳定性与连接性提升1.29倍。

Conclusion: 通过让解码器内化约束而非后处理校正，TopVAE构建了更平滑、有效且可导航的潜在空间，显著提升了潜在扩散模型在3D分子生成中的性能与可靠性。

Abstract: Latent diffusion is a promising framework for scalable 3D molecular generation, but it requires a latent space that remains smooth, valid, and navigable beyond posterior samples. Existing molecular VAEs, however, are typically learned through reconstruction-based objectives, which do not guarantee such a latent space. We show that this leads to dark areas: regions of latent space that are reachable during diffusion sampling but decode to disconnected or chemically invalid molecules.
