### [175] [Can Deep Neural Networks Improve Compression of Very Large Scientific Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14353)
*Muhannad Alhumaidi,Guozhong Li,Spiros Skiadopoulos,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将现代机器学习预测模型（气象基础模型）集成到误差有界有损压缩管线中的效果。通过在1.7 TB ERA5气候数据上对比三种ML预测器与经典压缩器SZ3.1，发现尽管ML预测器能显著提升预测精度和单变量压缩比，但由于残差的空间结构不利于熵编码，数据集级别的整体压缩比并未改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的有损压缩器遵循"预测-残差"范式，压缩效果高度依赖预测器质量。一个自然的问题是：现代ML模型能否作为更优的预测器来提升科学数据压缩？由于自行开发压缩专用ML预测器成本高昂，作者利用气候领域已有高精度天气预报基础模型作为理想测试平台来回答这一问题。

Method: 提出一个将时空深度学习模型集成到传统误差有界压缩管线的框架，支持自回归预测模型并避免误差累积。以ERA5气候数据（约1.7 TB）为测试集，在相同的量化和熵编码后端下，对比三种ML预测器——VAEformer编解码器(CRA5)、图神经网络预测器(GraphCast)和视觉Transformer预测器(Aurora)——与当前最佳压缩器SZ3.1的性能。

Result: ML预测器能生成更精确的预测，对高度可预测变量最高可提升91%的重建质量，压缩比最高达9.6倍；但在数据集整体层面，ML预测器并未改善压缩比。预测精度并非唯一决定因素：残差的空间结构对熵编码效率起决定性作用。

Conclusion: 仅凭提升预测精度不足以改善科学数据压缩的整体效果；残差的空间结构特征是影响熵编码效率的关键因素，这为未来面向压缩的ML预测器设计指明了新方向。

Abstract: Error-bounded lossy compression is a fundamental technique for managing the rapidly growing volumes of scientific data produced by modern simulations and observational instruments. Most state-of-the-art-compressors follow a prediction-residual paradigm, where compression effectiveness depends on the quality of the predictor: more accurate predictions generate smaller residuals that are easier to compress. This observation raises a question: can modern machine learning models serve as superior predictors for scientific data compression? Answering this question directly is challenging because developing compression-specific ML predictors requires substantial resources. Instead, we leverage the climate domain where highly accurate pretrained weather forecasting foundation models already exist, making them an ideal testbed. We present a framework that integrates spatial and temporal deep learning models into a conventional error-bounded compression pipeline. The framework supports auto-regressive forecasting models and avoids error accumulation. Using ERA5 climate data as a representative large-scale scientific dataset, we evaluate three distinct ML predictors: a VAEformer-based codec (CRA5), a graph neural network forecaster (GraphCast), and a vision-transformer forecaster (Aurora), against the state-of-the-art compressor SZ3.1 under identical quantization and entropy-coding backends. Our evaluation over approximately 1.7 TB of data reveals a surprising result: although ML predictors generate more accurate predictions and can improve reconstruction quality by up to 91% while achieving up to 9.6x higher compression ratios for highly predictable variables, they do not improve overall dataset-level compression ratio. We show that prediction accuracy alone is insufficient: the spatial structure of the resulting residuals plays a decisive role in entropy coding efficiency.

</details>


### [176] [MUFFLe: Efficient Model Update Compression via Generalized Deduplication for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14354)
*Xiaobo Zhao,Daniel E. Lucani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MUFFLe 将广义去重（GD）集成到 FedAvg 流程中，在 IID MNIST 20 客户端场景下，以 38 MB 累计上行通信量达到 92.93% 目标精度，显著优于 8-bit 量化、Top-k 稀疏化和无压缩 FedAvg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在边缘环境中应用广泛，但模型更新上传的通信成本是主要瓶颈，现有压缩方法在通信效率上仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出 MUFFLe 方案，将广义去重（Generalized Deduplication, GD）集成到 FedAvg 流水线中，通过去重更新向量中的重复模式，实现固定速率、可变计数的压缩。

Result: 在 IID MNIST、20 个客户端的实验中，MUFFLe 仅需 38 MB 累计上行通信即达到 92.93% 目标精度，而 8-bit 量化为 75 MB，Top-k 稀疏化为 86 MB，无压缩 FedAvg 高达 310 MB。

Conclusion: 广义去重应用于通信高效的联邦学习具有可行性，MUFFLe 在压缩效率上展现出明显优势。

Abstract: Federated learning is well suited to edge environments but is often limited by the uplink cost of transmitting model updates. This Work-in-Progress paper presents MUFFLe, a communication-efficient update compression scheme that integrates generalized deduplication (GD) into the FedAvg pipeline. MUFFLe deduplicates repeated patterns across the update vector, yielding a fixed-rate, variable-count compression scheme. Preliminary experiments on IID MNIST with 20 clients show that MUFFLe reaches the target accuracy of $92.93\%$ with 38~MB cumulative uplink communication, compared with 75~MB for 8-bit quantization, 86~MB for Top-$k$ sparsification, and 310~MB for uncompressed FedAvg. These results demonstrate the feasibility of applying GD to communication-efficient federated learning.

</details>


### [177] [Flood and Harvest: The Provable Necessity of Trivia for Generating Valuable Mathematics via the Lens of Language Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14688)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Dai Shi,Zheng Gao,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究AI与证明助手耦合生成形式数学的极限。核心模型将有价值数学的生成建模为嵌套语言生成：外层是可通过证明检查器访问的形式语言F，内层是未知的有价值语言H，仅通过密度为α的核心集C暴露。作者解决了四个问题：(1) 验证器不等于品味，能广度生成的集合恰为无预言机模型中的Angluin条件刻画者；(2) 验证器提供可靠覆盖——能覆盖所有未见的H中陈述且仅断言有效陈述，这在无验证器时不可能；(3) 核心二分律：生成有限多个平凡项可达最优覆盖α/2，任何无限平凡项（即使速率趋于零）将最优值跃升至1-α/2；(4) 两种机制均在数学压缩模型中实例化。结论：完美验证器无法替代品味，无限但渐进可忽略的认证平凡项流是覆盖未记录有价值数学的必然代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI与证明助手耦合虽能大规模生成形式数学，但核心瓶颈已从"能否验证"转向"验证器能通过的内容与数学家认为有价值的内容之间的鸿沟"。本文旨在从理论上对"有价值数学的生成"建立形式模型，揭示验证器(verifier)与品味(taste)之间的根本性界限。

Method: 论文将生成问题建模为极限中的嵌套语言生成：形式语言F可经由成员预言机（证明检查器）访问，F中包含一个未知的有价值语言H∈ℋ（属于某个假设类），H仅通过对立枚举(adversarial enumeration)其核心子集C⊆H（密度恰好为α）来揭示——C代表已发表的文献。生成器的输出分为三类：有价值(∈H)、平凡(∈F\H)、幻觉(∉F)。在此框架下，使用Angluin条件、预言机模型、覆盖分析等理论计算机科学工具，分析验证器在覆盖未见有价值陈述时的作用，并证明关于平凡项数量与覆盖率之间的尖锐二分律。

Result: 四个核心结论：(1) 验证器非品味：能实现广度生成(breadth generation)的集合类恰与无预言机模型相同，由Angluin条件逐纤维(fiber-wise)刻画。(2) 验证器确实提供可靠覆盖(sound coverage)：在验证器帮助下，可在仅断言有效陈述的前提下覆盖所有未见的H中陈述；无验证器则不可能，验证器将不可避免的错误从假(false)转移至平凡(trivial)。(3) 核心二分律(tight family上)：生成有限多个平凡项的生成器可达最优覆盖α/2；而任何允许无限平凡项（即使速率趋于零）的生成器，其最优覆盖跃升至1-α/2；两者皆为紧界，且存在一个生成器可同时达到两端。转变取决于平凡项的计数而非速率；间隙1-α恰为未记录的有价值数学的质量(unrecorded mass)。(4) 两种机制均在数学压缩模型中实例化。

Conclusion: 完美验证器不能替代品味：无限但正确却无价值的陈述流并非工程偶然，而是可证明的必然——因为覆盖未记录的有价值数学必然需要产生一个无限但渐进可忽略的认证平凡项流。这一结论从根本上揭示了AI辅助数学研究中自动化验证与人类价值判断之间的不可消解的张力。

Abstract: AI systems coupled to proof assistants now generate formal mathematics at scale, and the gap between what a checker can verify and what a mathematician would value has become the binding constraint. We model the generation of valuable mathematics as nested language generation in the limit: a verifiable formal language $F$, accessed through a membership oracle (the proof checker), contains an unknown valuable language $H \in \mathcal{H}$ revealed only through an adversarial enumeration of a core $C \subseteq H$ of exact density $α$ (the literature). Every output is valuable ($\in H$), trivial ($\in F \setminus H$), or a hallucination ($\notin F$). We settle four questions. First, the verifier is not taste: the collections admitting generation with breadth are exactly those of the oracle-free model, characterized fiber-wise by Angluin's condition. Second, the verifier does buy sound coverage, covering all unseen valuable statements while asserting only valid ones: possible with it, impossible without it; it relocates unavoidable errors from false to trivial. Third, and centrally, a sharp dichotomy on the tight family: generators emitting finitely many trivia achieve optimal coverage $α/2$, while any infinite trivia allowance, even at vanishing rate, jumps the optimum to $1-α/2$ (both tight, for cores presented as the candidate intersection), and one generator attains both ends. The transition is in trivia count, not rate; the gap $1-α$ is the unrecorded mass. Fourth, both regimes instantiate in a compression model of mathematics. A perfect verifier cannot substitute for taste: the unbounded stream of correct-but-worthless statements is not an engineering accident but a provable necessity, since covering unrecorded valuable mathematics requires an infinite, but asymptotically negligible, stream of certified trivia.

</details>


### [178] [SemPiper: Interactive Code Synthesis for Semantic Operators in Machine Learning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14361)
*Olga Ovcharenko,Luciano Duarte,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SemPipes 提出一种新型编程模型，通过声明式、LLM 驱动的语义数据算子扩展 ML 流水线，使开发者能用自然语言描述数据操作，同时无缝融合标准 Python 代码，并在流水线训练时根据数据集特征自动合成专用实现，通过进化搜索进行迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 ML 流水线开发需要大量数据准备、特征工程及跨异构源集成，过程繁琐且易出错。LLM 虽展现出辅助编程的潜力，但基于聊天的界面缺乏对流水线行为的精确控制，生成的代码难以优化或集成到生产系统。

Method: SemPipes 编程模型：(1) 提供声明式语义数据算子，开发者以高层自然语言指令指定数据操作；(2) 语义算子与任意标准数据科学库的 Python 代码无缝结合；(3) 在流水线训练时，根据数据集特征与流水线上下文，为语义算子合成专用实现；(4) 引入进化搜索过程对合成实现进行迭代优化；(5) 构建 SemPiper 交互界面，可视化流水线计算图、合成算子实现及优化轨迹。

Result: 通过 SemPiper 交互界面展示了三个端到端场景，参与者可修改流水线、检查生成的代码，并观察语义算子如何被合成与迭代优化。

Conclusion: 声明式语义算子实现了 LLM 在 ML 流水线开发中可控、可优化且实用的集成方式，为 ML 流水线开发提供了一种更高效、更可靠的编程范式。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) pipelines require extensive data preparation, feature engineering, and integration across heterogeneous sources, making them tedious and error-prone to develop. While large language models (LLMs) have recently shown promise for assisting programming tasks, chat-based interfaces provide limited control over pipeline behavior and often produce code that is difficult to optimize or integrate into production systems. We demonstrate SemPipes, a novel programming model that extends ML pipelines with declarative, LLM-powered semantic data operators. SemPipes allows developers to specify high-level natural language instructions for data-centric operations, while seamlessly combining these operators with arbitrary Python code from standard data science libraries. For the semantic operators, it synthesizes specialized implementations at pipeline training time, conditioned on dataset characteristics and pipeline context, enabling the flexible yet controlled integration of LLM capabilities. We demonstrate SemPipes through SemPiper, an interactive interface that visualizes computational graphs of the pipelines, synthesized operator implementations, and optimization trajectories produced by an evolutionary search procedure. Attendees can explore three end-to-end scenarios, modify pipelines, inspect generated code, and observe how semantic operators are synthesized and iteratively optimized. The demonstration highlights how declarative semantic operators enable controllable, optimizable, and practical integration of LLMs into ML pipeline development.

</details>


### [179] [Persona-Pruner: Sculpting Lightweight Models for Role-Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14695)
*Jinsu Kim,Jihoon Tack,Noah Lee,Jongheon Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Persona-Pruner 框架，通过从单一角色描述中分离出 persona 特定子网络，将通用大模型裁剪为轻量级角色扮演模型，在 RoleBench 上相较最强基线将性能损失降低 93.8%，同时保留通用 LLM 能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有角色扮演对话系统为每个角色部署完整通用大模型，计算成本过高；作者假设单一角色身份仅依赖模型总容量的一小部分，但朴素剪枝会不加区分地删除冗余知识与关键角色特征，严重损害角色扮演性能。

Method: 提出 Persona-Pruner 框架：从单一角色描述出发，通过定位和隔离 persona 特定子网络，精准裁剪模型中以角色身份为核心的部分，保留角色一致性与风格化交互能力，同时维持通用语言能力。

Result: 在 RoleBench 上，以 LLM-as-a-judge 评分为准，Persona-Pruner 将相较于稠密模型的性能损失降低达 93.8%（对比最强基线），且显著优于现有 SOTA LLM 剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 为单一角色部署完整通用模型是不必要的；通过精准定位 persona 特定子网络进行裁剪，可在大幅降低计算成本的同时保留角色扮演质量，验证了该方法在现实多 NPC 场景中的实用潜力。

Abstract: Language Models (LMs) have shown remarkable potential as role-playing chatbots, delivering consistent, stylized interactions when given a specification of a character or user persona. However, applying these capabilities to real-world applications (e.g., ecosystems with numerous NPCs interacting simultaneously) exposes a critical inefficiency due to the excessive computational cost. In this paper, we question the necessity of dedicating a full, generalist model to a single persona, hypothesizing that a specific character identity relies on only a fraction of the model's total capacity. We observe that naively pruning LMs often severely degrades the role-playing performance for a specific persona; it does not distinguish between redundant knowledge and essential character traits. We propose Persona-Pruner, a framework that sculpts a lightweight role-playing model by isolating persona-specific sub-networks from a single description. Our experiments consistently show that Persona-Pruner preserves role-playing performance substantially more effectively than existing state-of-the-art LLM pruning techniques, reducing the performance drop from the dense model by up to 93.8% over the strongest baseline on RoleBench in LLM-as-a-judge score, while still maintaining general LLM capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/jsu-kim/Persona-Pruner.

</details>


### [180] [Discovery under Hypothesis Redundancy: A Geometric Theory of Discovery Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14386)
*Li Xia,Baoxun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 科学发现饱和并非因假设空间名义上缩小，而是新假设不再提供独立信息。本文提出搜索压缩假说，形式化三个几何条件（谱压缩、正交逃逸、残差对齐），证明非局部探索仅在同时满足三者时有效，并在合成环境、A股因子挖掘和符号回归基准上验证了该机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM驱动的科学发现系统倾向于追求新颖性，但新颖性本身不足以保证发现效率提升。作者观察到科学发现饱和的本质是新假设不再提供独立信息，因此需要建立一套理论框架来诊断何时非局部探索（如LLM生成提议）真正有助于突破局部搜索瓶颈。

Method: 提出搜索压缩假说（Search Compression Hypothesis），将混合发现系统的有效条件形式化为三个几何条件：(1) 谱压缩——假设空间的已探索子空间特征谱快速衰减；(2) 正交逃逸——非局部提议需正交于已探索子空间；(3) 残差对齐——提议方向需与目标信号的残差分量对齐。通过信号植入实验、有向vs随机跳跃对比、压缩扫描、真实因子库分析和LLM-SRBench任务进行验证。

Result: 信号植入和有向vs随机实验表明，仅做随机正交跳跃虽能扩展覆盖范围，但缺乏预测对齐时无法提升产出。跨压缩扫描、真实A股因子档案和LLM-SRBench任务中，混合增益集中于弱表示但承载目标信号的方向，当假设空间接近满秩时增益消失。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM引导的发现从通用的新颖性搜索转变为一种诊断程序：通过检验三个几何条件是否同时满足，来决定何时进行有向非局部探索是必要的。这为科学发现中的LLM资源分配提供了可操作的检验标准。

Abstract: Scientific discovery saturates when new hypotheses cease to provide independent information, even if the nominal hypothesis space remains large. We study hybrid discovery systems that combine structured local search with LLM-generated non-local proposals and pose the Search Compression Hypothesis: non-local exploration helps only when three geometric conditions co-occur: spectral compression, orthogonal escape from the explored span, and residual signal alignment with the target. We formalize these conditions, derive necessary conditions for hybrid advantage, and test the mechanism in controlled synthetic environments, large-scale A-share factor discovery, and symbolic-regression benchmarks; a public tabular operational sanity check tests the associated budget-allocation implication. Signal-planting and directed-versus-random experiments show that novelty alone is insufficient: random orthogonal jumps expand coverage but do not improve yield without predictive alignment. Across compression sweeps, real factor archives, and LLM-SRBench tasks, hybrid gains concentrate in weakly represented but target-bearing directions and vanish as the hypothesis space approaches full rank. The framework turns LLM-guided discovery from generic novelty search into a diagnostic procedure for deciding when directed non-local exploration is warranted.

</details>


### [181] [A Low-Rank Subspace Analysis of LLM Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14388)
*Angira Sharma,Christian Schroeder de Witt,Philip Torr,Anisoara Calinescu,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种诊断框架，将LLM中的行为建模为激活空间中的低秩子空间，研究不同行为（如拒绝、越狱、谄媚）之间的干预交互效应，发现行为间存在共享表征和不对称的干预传播，其效应可通过子空间重叠度和与决策子空间的夹角来量化预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前针对LLM特定行为（如拒绝回答、谄媚）的干预措施常产生非预期的副作用，导致其他行为也被改变。这种缺乏精准控制的问题严重影响了可靠安全控制的实现，亟需理解行为间交互效应的内在机制。

Method: 提出诊断框架：将不同行为建模为激活空间中的低秩子空间，通过两个几何量分析干预的影响——(1) 行为子空间之间的重叠度（用主角度平均平方余弦衡量）；(2) 每个行为子空间与决策子空间（捕捉模型最终决策，如拒绝vs.服从）之间的夹角。在多个指令微调模型（7B-70B）上，覆盖拒绝、越狱、谄媚等场景进行实证验证。

Result: 发现不同行为共享内部表征，对某一行为的干预会以不对称方式改变其他行为。部分行为充当"上游控制点"，干预效应广泛传播；另一些行为则相对孤立。干预的副作用强度与两个几何量相关：子空间重叠度越高、源行为子空间与决策子空间夹角越小，对其他行为的影响越大。

Conclusion: 行为难以独立修改，干预会通过共享表征和不对称交互传播，这对LLM的精准行为控制构成根本性挑战。研究揭示了实现可靠安全控制的深层困难，并提供了理解行为交互的几何框架。

Abstract: Interventions designed to modify a particular behavior in LLMs, such as refusal or sycophancy, often produce unintended changes in other behaviors. This lack of targeted control makes it difficult to design and implement reliable safety controls. To understand these side-effects, we introduce a diagnostic framework for analyzing interacting behaviors in LLMs. We model behaviors as low-rank subspaces in activation space, and study how interventions influence across behaviors. Across multiple instruction-tuned models (7B-70B) and across refusal, jailbreak, and sycophancy settings, we find that different behaviors share internal representations, and intervening on one behavior alters others in asymmetric ways. Some behaviors act as upstream control points whose interventions propagate broadly across other behaviors, while others remain more isolated. We relate these effects to two geometric quantities: (i) the overlap between behavior subspaces, measured as the average squared cosine of principal angles, and (ii) the angle between each behavior subspace and the decision subspace (capturing the model's final decision e.g., refuse vs. comply). Empirically, intervention effects on other behaviors tend to be larger for behavior pairs with higher subspace overlap, and for source behaviors whose subspaces lie closer (smaller angle) to the decision subspace. These findings highlight a challenge for targeted behavior control: behaviors are difficult to modify independently, as interventions can propagate through shared representations and asymmetric interactions.

</details>


### [182] [Running the Gauntlet: Re-evaluating the Capabilities of Agents Beyond Familiar Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14397)
*Mykola Vysotskyi,Runqi Lin,Grzegorz Biziel,Michal Zakrzewski,Sebastian Montagna,Damian Rynczak,Shreyansh Padarha,Kumail Alhamoud,Zihao Fu,William Lugoloobi,Kai Rawal,Hanna Yershova,Xander Davies,Taras Rumezhak,Guohao Li,Fazl Barez,Baoyuan Wu,Arkadiusz Drohomirecki,Yarin Gal,Chris Russell,Christopher Summerfield,Adam Mahdi,Volodymyr Karpiv,Philip Torr,Adel Bibi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GauntletBench是一个面向网页环境的智能体基准测试，聚焦于时间感知、图形理解和3D推理三种被忽视的能力，覆盖五个专业应用场景共100个视觉密集型任务。实验表明，当前最先进的智能体成功率仅为19.1%，而人类非专家标注者超过80%，揭示出前沿智能体在复杂真实场景中仍存在巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体评测基准普遍基于简单任务和热门应用，能力覆盖面窄，导致现有智能体性能饱和，无法有效探测其局限。需要构建更具挑战性、覆盖更广维度的基准来忠实评估智能体在真实复杂场景中的泛化能力。

Method: 构建GauntletBench，包括：(1)兼容开源与闭源智能体框架的模块化环境；(2)五个受控的网页应用（视频编辑器、工作流构建器、3D建模器、飞行分析器、电路设计器）；(3)每个应用20个视觉密集型任务，共100个任务；(4)配备多种指标的自动化评估引擎。重点考察时间感知、图形理解、3D推理三项能力。

Result: 最先进的智能体在GauntletBench上仅取得19.1%的成功率，而人类非专家标注者在同样可行但具挑战性的任务上成功率超过80%。

Conclusion: 前沿智能体系统远未达到人类水平，在被忽视的时间感知、图形理解和3D推理等能力维度上存在显著局限，与复杂真实场景需求之间存在实质性差距。GauntletBench为评估智能体泛化能力提供了有效的测试平台。

Abstract: As agentic systems continue to evolve and are widely deployed in real-world scenarios, there is a growing demand to faithfully evaluate their capabilities. However, current benchmarks are typically built on popular applications with relatively simple tasks and focus on a narrow set of capabilities while overlooking broader dimensions, resulting in saturated performance on modern agents and failing to probe their limitations. To this end, we introduce GauntletBench, a web-based benchmark for evaluating agent generalisation in challenging scenarios, focusing on three underexplored capabilities (temporal perception, graphical understanding, and 3D reasoning), across five less-covered professional applications (Video Editor, Workflow Builder, 3D Modeller, Flight Analyser, and Circuit Designer), each with 20 vision-intensive tasks (100 in total). Our benchmark provides a modular pipeline that comprises an environment compatible with both open- and closed-source agent frameworks, a controlled web-based application, a well-structured task suite, and an automated evaluation engine with diverse metrics. Contrary to widespread expectations, our empirical results reveal that frontier agentic systems remain far from achieving human-level performance. Even the state-of-the-art agent achieves only a 19.1% success rate on our GauntletBench, highlighting the limitations in these overlooked capabilities and generalisation. By comparison, non-expert human annotators achieve over 80% success on our challenging yet feasible tasks, revealing the substantial gap between current agent capabilities and those required for complex real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [183] [A theoretical model for task routing in mixture-of-expert transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14398)
*Yongli Xiang,Vinoth Nandakumar,Yunzhi Yao,Peike Li,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过离散语言模型（句法模板和键值字典），从理论上证明单层MoE Transformer可实现任务-专家特化，查询被路由到与任务复杂度相匹配的专用专家，为MoE中局部化知识回路提供理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对MoE任务-专家特化的理论分析采用连续混合模型，无法有效建模自然语言。作者旨在用离散语言模型填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过句法模板（syntactic templates）和有限键值字典（finite key-value dictionaries）表示结构化知识，构造性地证明单层MoE Transformer能编码知识并实现专家任务特化。

Result: 证明查询被唯一路由到特定任务专家，专家规模仅取决于任务的固有复杂度（句法模板与事实字典的合并大小）；实验在不同MoE损失函数下验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 该构造为MoE模型中局部化知识回路的实证研究提供了理论支持，揭示了任务特化可在离散语言建模框架下被严格解释。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) layers enable the scaling of transformer models while keeping the inference compute fixed. While task-expert specialization has been observed in empirical studies of frontier MoE transformer models, existing theoretical work analyzes this using continuous mixture models that cannot be used to model natural language effectively. An important open question is to \textit{theoretically explain task-expert specialization in transformer MoE models using discrete models of language}. To address this, we represent structured knowledge via syntactic templates and finite key-value dictionaries, and prove formally that a single-layer MoE transformer can encode knowledge by using experts that specialize in the corresponding tasks. Our construction shows how queries are routed to unique, task-specific experts whose size depends solely on the intrinsic complexity of the given task (i.e. the combined size of its syntactic templates and factual dictionary). Our construction provides a theoretical support for empirical results on localized knowledge circuits in MoE models. We support our theoretical findings with experiments evaluating model performance under varying MoE loss functions.

</details>


### [184] [EM-NeSy: Expectation Maximization for Neurosymbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14463)
*Annegret Seibt,Luc De Raedt,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EM-NeSy 将概率神经符号学习表述为期望最大化（EM）算法，使得符号组件无需可微分，并能自然扩展到近似推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的神经符号模型要求符号组件以可微分方式表达，这给近似推理的使用带来了困难。作者希望解除这一限制，使神经符号学习能灵活运用近似推理。

Method: 提出 EM-NeSy 框架，将概率神经符号学习转化为 EM 算法实例：E 步通过概率推理计算给定标签下神经预测符号的后验分布；M 步基于此后验，仅通过神经网络部分进行梯度下降来更新参数。该方案在精确推理下可退化为标准端到端梯度方法。

Result: 实验结果表明 EM-NeSy 具有良好的可扩展性和计算效率。

Conclusion: EM-NeSy 释放了 EM 算法在神经符号学习中的全部潜力，无需对符号组件施加可微分约束即可支持近似推理，同时兼容精确推理下的标准范式。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic (NeSy) models integrate neural networks and symbolic reasoning for robust and interpretable AI. State-of-the-art NeSy models require that the symbolic component is expressed in a differentiable way, often complicating the use of approximate inference. We propose EM-NeSy which casts probabilistic NeSy learning as an instance of the Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm. In the expectation step, we compute the posterior over the neurally predicted symbols conditioned on the label via probabilistic inference. In the maximization step, we update the neural parameters based on this posterior using gradient descent only through the neural component. This formulation unlocks the full potential of the EM algorithm for NeSy learning. It allows NeSy to extend naturally to approximate reasoning without any additional modifications or differentiability requirements of the symbolic component. Furthermore, it recovers the standard end-to-end gradient-based NeSy setting under exact inference. Our experimental results demonstrate the scalability and computational efficiency of EM-NeSy.

</details>


### [185] [Recipe-Controlled Decoder Audit for Structural Knowledge-Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14492)
*Xihang Shan,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种配方控制下的解码器审计方法（RCDA），用于结构化直推式知识图谱补全。核心问题是：在相同训练配方下，仅更换解码器会带来什么变化？通过在七个基准上以ComplEx/DistMult为主控对进行实验，发现标准KG上解码器差异温和但一致，小规模KG上解码器选择成为主要诊断因素，且解码器选择与编码器深度存在交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱补全领域在归因性能提升时，常混淆编码器改进与训练配方（如优化器、正则化、负采样等）的贡献。现有研究缺乏一个简洁的审计协议来隔离解码器选择的影响，导致难以判断性能增益究竟来自何处。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出配方控制解码器审计（RCDA）框架：固定同一训练配方（优化器、学习率、正则化系数、负采样数等超参数），仅系统性替换解码器（以ComplEx与DistMult为主要对照对，辅以RotatE/TransE抽查），在七个基准数据集上测量MRR差异。特别关注编码器深度与解码器选择的交互效应，并区分小规模KG的种子敏感性与来源变体影响。

Result: 在五个标准KG上，ComplEx-vs-DistMult差异温和但一致（+0.005至+0.012 MRR）；CompGCN风格编码器效应因数据集而异。小规模KG上解码器效应成为主要诊断因素：Kinship上ComplEx稳定领先+0.143 MRR（6种子），UMLS在干净6种子服务器重跑中ComplEx领先+0.022 MRR，但在早前来源变体中结果反转。WN18RR上解码器选择与编码器深度存在交互。在配方下，YAGO3-10上L=0 ComplEx以d=128达到0.6971±0.0048 MRR。

Conclusion: 提出紧凑审计协议：报告匹配的解码器行、记录小规模KG的来源信息、在做出编码器层面声明前先扫描解码器×深度的组合。该协议有助于更严谨地归因知识图谱补全中的性能提升来源。

Abstract: We present a recipe-controlled decoder audit (RCDA) for structural transductive knowledge-graph completion (KGC). The audit asks a simple reporting question: before attributing gains to an encoder or training recipe, what changes when the decoder is swapped under the same recipe? Using ComplEx and DistMult as the primary controlled pair, with targeted RotatE/TransE spot-checks, we evaluate seven benchmarks. On five standard KGs, ComplEx-vs-DistMult differences are modest but consistent under our recipe (+0.005 to +0.012 MRR), whereas CompGCN-style encoder effects vary more by dataset. On small KGs, decoder effects become the main diagnostic: Kinship shows a stable ComplEx advantage of +0.143 MRR (6 seeds), while UMLS favours ComplEx by +0.022 MRR in a clean 6-seed server rerun but reverses in an earlier provenance variant. We therefore treat small-KG decoder choice as recipe- and provenance-sensitive rather than as a fixed dataset winner. We further show that decoder choice interacts with encoder depth on WN18RR, and that under our recipe L=0 ComplEx on YAGO3-10 reaches 0.6971 +/- 0.0048 MRR at d=128. The result is a compact audit protocol: report matched decoder rows, log small-KG provenance, and sweep decoder x depth before making encoder-level claims.

</details>


### [186] [PepALD: Macrocyclic Peptide Generation via Autoregressive Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14510)
*Junming Zhang,Siyu Yi,Wei Ju,Zhonghui Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PepALD 是一种基于自回归潜在扩散（ALD）的大环肽从头生成基础模型，使用化学信息嵌入表示单体，通过上下文条件潜在扩散生成残基，在自回归过程中预测R基感知的环闭合，并采用赢家保护的扩散适配偏好优化来对齐亲和力奖励，生成质量和奖励优化性能优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大环肽生成模型（基于SMILES或HELM字符串）要么在冗长的原子级序列空间操作，要么将单体视为缺乏化学基础的符号化令牌。设计大环肽需同时控制非天然单体化学、环拓扑、膜通透性和靶标结合，这对生成模型提出了严峻挑战。

Method: PepALD采用自回归潜在扩散架构：(1) 用结构化化学嵌入表示HELM单体；(2) 通过上下文条件扩散在化学信息潜在空间中逐残基生成；(3) 自回归生成过程中预测R基感知的环闭合；(4) 使用赢家保护的扩散适配偏好优化（winner-protected diffusion-adapted preference optimization）将去噪器与亲和力奖励对齐。

Result: 计算机模拟实验表明，PepALD在生成质量和奖励优化性能方面均优于代表性的肽生成基线模型。

Conclusion: PepALD成功将化学信息嵌入与自回归潜在扩散相结合，实现了对非天然单体化学、环拓扑和靶标亲和力的联合控制，为大环肽药物的从头设计提供了有前景的基础模型。

Abstract: Macrocyclic peptides are promising therapeutic candidates for intracellular targets, but their design requires simultaneous control over non-natural monomer chemistry, ring topology, membrane permeability, and target binding. Existing SMILES- or HELM-string generative models either operate in long atom-level sequence spaces or treat monomers as symbolic tokens with limited chemical grounding. We introduce PepALD, an Autoregressive Latent Diffusion (ALD) foundation model for \textit{de novo} macrocyclic peptide generation. The model represents HELM monomers with structured chemical embeddings, generates each residue through context-conditioned diffusion in chemically informed latent space, predicts R-group-aware ring closures during autoregressive generation, and aligns the denoiser to affinity rewards using winner-protected diffusion-adapted preference optimization. In silico experiments demonstrate PepALD's generation quality and reward-optimization performance against representative peptide generation baselines.

</details>


### [187] [Behavioral Audit of Machine Unlearning Has a Privacy Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14518)
*Liou Tang,James Joshi,Ashish Kundu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了机器学习遗忘（MU）审计中一个根本性的隐私-审计权衡：在模型所有者不诚实、审计者诚实但好奇的场景下，仅依赖查询模型行为信号的通用审计方案，无法在不泄露保留集成员信息的前提下识别出遗忘不充分的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MU审计工作要么聚焦于不诚实的模型所有者伪造遗忘证据，要么关注好奇的审计者推断隐私属性，但双方互不信任场景下的审计问题尚未被探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，从信息论角度分析该场景下的根本性限制。

Method: 作者采用信息论方法，针对凸机器学习模型，在模型所有者不诚实、审计者诚实但好奇的威胁模型下，证明仅通过查询模型获取行为信号的通用审计方案必然面临隐私-审计权衡。理论证明之外，还在凸模型和非凸模型上进行了实证验证，实验代码已开源。

Result: 理论证明：对于凸ML模型，任何仅依赖行为信号查询的通用审计方案，无法在识别遗忘不充分模型的同时避免泄露保留集成员信息。实证结果：凸模型实验强烈支持该理论结果，非凸模型实验表明该隐私-审计张力同样持续存在。

Conclusion: 在现实审计者威胁模型下，隐私-审计张力需要被更审慎地对待。该工作为设计MU管线的隐私保护审计方案提供了审视基础，呼吁后续研究重视这一根本性权衡。

Abstract: The removal of learned data from Machine Learning models through Machine Unlearning (MU) has been widely studied; however, there has yet to be an agreed-upon scheme for auditing MU. Existing work has shown that a dishonest model owner can falsify evidence to avoid executing MU, while curious auditors (and adversaries) can infer the privacy-sensitive properties of the model and its training data even with limited access. Yet auditing of MU under mutual distrust between the model owner and the auditor remains unexplored. We provide an information-theoretic proof for this scenario: for convex ML models, a generic audit scheme that relies solely on querying the model for \textit{behavioral} signals cannot identify insufficiently unlearned models without revealing membership information of the retained set. Therefore, auditing MU under the assumption of a dishonest model owner and an honest-but-curious auditor faces an inherent privacy-audit tradeoff. Our empirical results on convex models strongly supports this result, while further experiments demonstrate that this privacy-audit tension persists in non-convex models. Our results call for a more careful consideration of the privacy-audit tension under a realistic auditor threat model, and serve as a foundation for more scrutiny of designs of privacy-preserving audit schemes for the MU pipeline. We also release our code implementation at https://github.com/LiouTang/Behavioral-Unlearn-Audit.

</details>


### [188] [Code Correctness Signals in LLM Hidden States: Pre-Generation Probing and Repair Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14530)
*Carlo Di Cicco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过对Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507模型隐藏状态的分析，研究发现代码正确性可在生成前从隐藏状态中线性解码（AUC 0.931），但修复阶段的隐藏状态偏移在控制修复上下文协变量后不再显著，表明该偏移仅是修复成功的相关因素而非独立的"修复理解"特征。论文贡献兼具方法论与实证价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在代码生成与修复过程中，隐藏状态是否蕴含关于代码正确性的可解读信息，尤其是区分"预生成阶段的可解码性"与"修复阶段的对比方向"是否真正反映模型的修复理解能力。

Method: 以Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507为对象，在444个LiveCodeBench任务上进行分析。核心方法工具是残差化（residualization），用于控制混杂因素。第一项研究：从prompt结束时的隐藏状态训练线性探针来解码首次生成代码的正确性，并在控制prompt长度效应后重新评估。第二项研究：在236个修复案例上，计算失败尝试到修复尝试的隐藏状态偏移，检验其对比方向，并通过条件残差化控制修复上下文协变量。所有探针层选择均使用嵌套交叉验证。

Result: （1）首次生成代码正确性可从隐藏状态线性解码，AUC达0.931±0.008；剔除prompt长度线性效应后AUC仍为0.911±0.010，远高于prompt长度基线0.754±0.014。（2）修复阶段隐藏状态偏移在幅度检验和split-half检验上均显著，但经修复上下文协变量条件残差化后该方向不再显著，表明该信号由修复上下文驱动，并非独立的"修复理解"特征。

Conclusion: 代码正确性在生成前即已编码于隐藏状态中，且该信号不能仅归因于prompt长度；而修复阶段的隐藏状态偏移虽表面显著，但经残差化控制后消失，揭示了此前解读为"修复理解"信号实为修复上下文驱动的相关性。论文的核心贡献在于展示了残差化作为诊断工具的价值——既能巩固正面结果，也能诚实地揭示负面结果。

Abstract: Large language models encode rich information in their hidden states. This work asks whether code correctness is legible in the hidden states of Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, before it generates and as it repairs a failed attempt, studied on 444 LiveCodeBench tasks. It reports two findings connected by a single confound-control tool: residualization. First, the correctness of the model's first-attempt code is linearly decodable from the prompt-final hidden state, with a leakage-free held-out AUC of 0.931 +/- 0.008 across 50 outer splits. After the linear effect of prompt length is removed from each hidden state dimension, the probe still reaches 0.911 +/- 0.010, well above a prompt-length baseline of 0.754 +/- 0.014. Second, on 236 cleaned cases where the model attempts to repair a failed first attempt, the hidden state shift from the failing attempt to its repair carries a statistically detectable contrastive direction, significant on both a magnitude and a split-half test against label-shuffled nulls. This direction does not survive a conditional residualization against repair-context covariates that differ between successful and failed repairs, marking it as a correlate of repair success driven by the repair context rather than an isolated repair-comprehension feature. The probe layer is selected by nested cross-validation, and the same residualization approach that upholds the pre-generation correctness result overturns the repair-direction interpretation. The contribution is as much methodological as empirical: a diagnostic honest enough to report a negative result alongside a positive one.

</details>


### [189] [The Risk Shadow of Principal Component Analysis: When 99.9999% Variance Preservation Causes Catastrophic Decision Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14533)
*Hamidou Tembine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了主成分分析(PCA)在罕见灾难性事件检测中的根本缺陷——"风险阴影"现象，即PCA可保留99.9999%以上的方差却完全抹除尾部风险信号，并提出Expectile PCA和Tail-Preserving PCA两种重加权方法来破解这一困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCA以方差最大化为目标，但方差保留并不等价于保留检测罕见高影响事件所需的信息。在高风险决策场景（如欺诈检测、灾难预警）中，PCA可能系统性抹除尾部信号，导致下游分类器退化为常数预测器，这一根本性缺陷缺乏理论分析和针对性解决方案。

Method: 首先从理论上证明"风险阴影"的存在性：PCA保留近乎全部方差时仍可完全消除罕见事件信号，使得最优分类器退化为常数预测。随后提出两种方法：(1) Expectile PCA (ExPCA)，利用期望分位数回归对数据协方差进行重加权，使低概率高影响事件获得更高权重；(2) Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA)，通过尾部保留机制矫正方差最大化的偏差。理论上证明了ExPCA在保留罕见事件信息方面严格优于标准PCA。

Result: 理论上严格证明了风险阴影的存在性。实验方面，在合成数据上验证了风险阴影现象及所提方法的有效性；在真实信用卡欺诈检测基准数据集上，ExPCA和TP-PCA在保留罕见事件信息方面显著优于标准PCA。

Conclusion: 基于方差的降维方法在高风险决策中存在根本性缺陷，需要从根本上重新审视。ExPCA和TP-PCA通过重加权机制有效保留了尾部风险信息，为高风险场景下的降维提供了新范式。

Abstract: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) preserves variance, not the information needed to detect rare catastrophic events. This paper proves the existence of a {\it Risk Shadow}: PCA can retain over 99.9999 percent of total variance while completely erasing all signal about rare, high-impact failures. When this happens, even the best possible classifier operating on the PCA representation reduces to a constant predictor. The root cause is a fundamental mismatch between variance maximization and tail risk awareness. To break the shadow, we introduce Expectile PCA (ExPCA) and Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA), two methods that reweight the data covariance toward high-impact events. We prove theoretically that ExPCA strictly outperforms PCA in retaining rare-event information, and we validate our claims on synthetic data and a real-world credit card fraud detection benchmark. Our results call for a fundamental rethinking of variance-based dimensionality reduction in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [190] [Provably Safe, Yet Scalable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14536)
*Kai S. Yun,Zeyang Li,Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PS2-RL是一个两阶段框架，通过学习的备份策略在线生成隐式控制不变集，实现可证明安全且可扩展的强化学习，在高达10维状态空间的机器人控制任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全RL方法存在两难：软约束方法缺乏形式化安全保证，而具有严格保证的方法依赖显式不变集构造，在高维状态空间中扩展性差且行为过于保守。需要一种兼具可证明安全性和可扩展性的新方法。

Method: 提出PS2-RL两阶段框架：第一阶段使用安全到达值函数训练备份策略，最大化隐式控制不变集体积；第二阶段通过可微分投影层端到端训练RL策略，该投影层严格强制执行由备份策略诱导的安全保证。

Result: PS2-RL在状态维度高达10的机器人控制任务上进行了评估，该维度下先前的可证明安全RL方法难以适用或不切实际，PS2-RL表现出良好的性能和可扩展性。

Conclusion: PS2-RL成功克服了以往可证明安全RL方法的关键瓶颈，无需显式计算不变集，可与任意现有RL算法即插即用集成，兼具可证明安全性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn policies that optimize rewards while satisfying constraints. Predominant approaches rely on soft-constrained policy optimization, which has achieved empirical success but does not provide formal safety guarantees for the learned policy. In contrast, methods with strict guarantees typically rely on explicit certificate functions, whose construction requires the direct synthesis and verification of control-invariant sets, a process that scales poorly with state dimension and often yields overly conservative behavior. In this paper, we present the Provably Safe, yet Scalable RL (PS2-RL) framework, a novel two-phase architecture for learning provably safe policies in a scalable manner, designed to overcome the key bottlenecks of prior methods. Rather than explicitly computing invariant sets, PS2-RL leverages a learned backup policy to forward-integrate the system dynamics, generating an implicit control-invariant set online. In the first phase, the backup policy is trained with our proposed safe-arrival value function, which characterizes the optimal backup policy for invariant-set construction. In the second phase, an RL policy is trained end-to-end through a differentiable projection layer that strictly enforces the safety guarantees induced by the learned backup policy. By maximizing the volume of the implicit control-invariant set in the first phase, the resulting PS2 policy from the second phase is performant and scalable, while maintaining provable safety. Crucially, PS2-RL imposes no restrictions on the underlying RL algorithm and can be plugged into any existing training pipeline. We establish theoretical guarantees for the proposed framework and evaluate it on robotic control tasks with state dimensions up to 10, a regime in which prior provably safe RL methods struggle or become impractical.

</details>


### [191] [CARE: Controlling LLM-Generated Policies through Auditable Review of Evidence in Scientific Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14581)
*Guanyu Liu,Weiyi Kong,Zeyu Wang,Boer Zhang,Baiqing Li,Peiyu Zhang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE 提出一种可审计的控制器，在高通量实验优化中保留非LLM优化器作为默认路径，仅让LLM修订挑战者排序策略，并通过公开证据门控机制决定是否采纳挑战者方案。该方法在 Minerva/Olympus 和 ChemLex 基准上显著优于其他方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接让LLM控制成本高昂且不可逆的科学实验会导致不安全的探索和不稳定的性能，但完全摒弃LLM的创造力又会牺牲巨大的优化潜力。因此需要一种既能利用LLM创造力又能保证安全性和可审计性的折中方案。

Method: CARE框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 保留非LLM的现有优化器作为默认动作路径；(2) 利用LLM来修订挑战者排序策略；(3) 在每次实验结果揭晓前，通过公开证据干预门对挑战者与现有方案进行比较——仅当实验前的可用证据支持变更时才授权挑战者的选择，并将决策记录在审计日志中。

Result: 在 Minerva/Olympus 基准上，最终最佳指标从 80.0 提升至 88.5；在 ChemLex 基准上，从 83.9 提升至 92.1，均以公开的现有优化器为参照基线。CARE 在所有评估方法中表现最优。

Conclusion: LLM的自我进化在可审计控制器的约束下——即扩展提案空间而非直接选择实验——更加可靠。CARE 成功实现了LLM创造力与实验安全性的有效平衡。

Abstract: Granting LLMs direct control over costly, irreversible scientific experiments leads to unsafe exploration and unstable performance, but discarding LLM creativity entirely sacrifices significant optimization potential. We introduce CARE (Controlling LLM-Generated Policies through Auditable Review of Evidence in Scientific Experimentation), an auditable controller for high-throughput experimentation (HTE) optimization that keeps a non-LLM incumbent optimizer as the default action path while using LLMs to revise challenger ranking policies. Before each outcome is revealed, a public-evidence intervention gate compares the challenger with the incumbent. It authorizes the challenger's selection only when the evidence available before selection supports the change, with the decision recorded in the audit log. CARE outperforms all other evaluated methods on Minerva/Olympus and ChemLex benchmarks, with final-best improving from 80.0 to 88.5 on Minerva/Olympus and from 83.9 to 92.1 on ChemLex, relative to the public incumbent. Our experiments indicate that LLM self-evolution is more reliable when it expands the proposal space under an auditable controller, rather than directly choosing experiments.

</details>


### [192] [Zero-shot generalization of transformer neural operators to larger domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14597)
*Armand de Villeroché,Sibo Cheng,Vincent Le Guen,Marc Bocquet,Rem-Sophia Mouradi,Patrick Armand,Alban Farchi,Patrick Massin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可分解偏置注意力机制和旋转位置嵌入的Transformer神经算子，实现了在训练域之外的更大空间域上进行零样本推理，显著提升了PDE求解的域扩展泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer神经算子在PDE求解中隐式假设固定域大小，无法在推理时泛化到更大的空间域。这一问题本质上是缺乏空间局部性和平移等变性所致。

Method: 通过在注意力logits计算中引入可分解偏置（decomposable bias）实现精细可控的局部性，同时保持与query-key内积的完全可分解性和优化注意力核的兼容性；结合旋转位置嵌入（RoPE），在不改变Transformer架构的前提下实现可控空间支持的表达性嵌入。

Result: 在两个PDE基准测试和一个3D工业大气流应用中，该方法显著改善了向更大域进行零样本泛化的性能。

Conclusion: 通过可分解偏置注意力与旋转位置嵌入的结合，能够在不修改Transformer架构的条件下赋予神经算子空间局部性和平移等变性，是实现域扩展零样本推理的有效方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based neural operators have shown remarkable performance for approximating solution operators of partial differential equations on complex geometries. However, existing approaches implicitly assume a fixed domain size, which limits their ability to generalize at inference. In this work, we investigate domain extension, namely zero-shot inference on spatial domains that are significantly larger than those encountered during training. We argue that this setting fundamentally requires spatial locality and translation equivariance. We propose to implement this locality via a decomposable bias in the attention logits computation, enabling finely controllable locality while remaining fully decomposable into query-key inner products and directly compatible with optimized attention kernels. Combined with rotary positional embeddings, it enables expressive embeddings with controllable spatial support without altering the transformer architecture. We empirically show that our approach substantially improves zero-shot generalization to larger domains across two PDE benchmarks and a 3D industrial atmospheric flow application. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/cerea-daml/domain-extension.

</details>


### [193] [Realizing Native INT8 Compute for Diffusion Transformers on Consumer GPUs: A Fused INT8 GEMM Kernel for Ideogram 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14598)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Ampere消费级GPU上INT8量化推理反而比FP8/NF4慢的根本原因——现有"INT8"实现实际上是伪量化，权重和激活被量化后立即反量化回bf16进行矩阵乘法，未利用INT8 Tensor Core。作者通过一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM内核解决了该问题，在RTX 3090上使INT8从最慢变体变为最快，1024px图像生成用时156.5秒，实现了单卡可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级Ampere GPU（如RTX 3090）上，INT8量化扩散Transformer推理速度反而慢于FP8和NF4，这一反常现象的根本原因未被充分诊断，且缺乏真正利用INT8 Tensor Core的高效实现来扭转局面。

Method: 作者首先诊断出生产环境中INT8推理慢的原因是"伪量化"——权重和激活被量化后立即反量化回bf16，实际执行bf16矩阵乘法，未调用INT8 Tensor Core。解决方案是一个Triton编写的融合INT8 GEMM内核：在Ampere Tensor Core上执行int8×int8→int32累加，将逐token×逐通道反量化与偏置融合进epilogue，并按GEMM形状进行自动调优。该内核直接替换Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer线性层中的反量化到bf16路径。

Result: 内核层面：int8×int8→int32累加与torch._int_mm位精确一致，反量化输出与参考输出的余弦相似度为1.0，无NaN，单GEMM速度比bf16快2.8-4.2倍。端到端：768px分辨率下加速约1.1倍（9-10%），1024px下RTX 3090单卡生成图像仅需156.5秒，优于NF4（164.5秒）和FP8（172.9秒）基线，且PickScore/CLIPScore无质量损失。INT8从最慢变体变为最快，成功超越FP8约9.5%，与NF4的约4.9%差距在运行间方差范围内。

Conclusion: 该优化使INT8在消费级Ampere GPU上成为最快选项，使1024px单卡推理变得可行。但作者诚实指出，这一优势仅限于消费级Ampere架构；在A100和B200上，该内核不敌这些卡原生的快速bf16/FP8路径。

Abstract: Post-training INT8 (W8A8) quantization of diffusion transformers is widely deployed as a speed optimization, yet on consumer Ampere GPUs it is frequently slower than the FP8 and NF4 alternatives it is meant to beat. We trace this to a software artifact: the production "INT8" forward quantizes weights and activations only to immediately dequantize them back to bf16 and run a bf16 matrix multiply, never engaging the GPU's INT8 tensor cores, so the hardware's compute advantage is left entirely unrealized. We close this gap with a single fused Triton INT8 GEMM (int8xint8->int32 on Ampere tensor cores, with per-token x per-channel dequantization and bias folded into the epilogue, autotuned per GEMM shape) dropped into the Ideogram 4.0 diffusion transformer's linear layers in place of the dequantize-to-bf16 path. In the kernel, the int8xint8->int32 accumulation is bit-exact against torch._int_mm and the dequantized output matches the reference at cosine similarity 1.0 with no NaNs, running 2.8-4.2x faster than bf16 per GEMM. End to end it delivers a ~1.1x (~9-10%) speedup at 768px, and at 1024px it generates an image in 156.5 s on a single RTX 3090, faster than the single-card NF4 (164.5 s) and FP8 (172.9 s) baselines, at no measurable quality cost on these point estimates (PickScore/CLIPScore). INT8 thus goes from the slowest variant to the fastest, and 1024px becomes single-GPU feasible. The primary speed criterion (beat FP8, by ~9.5%) is comfortably met; the NF4 margin (~4.9%, single-run n=4) is within run-to-run variance we did not quantify and is best read as consistent with meeting the stretch target. We close with an honest deployment map: the win is specific to consumer Ampere, and on A100 and B200 the same kernel loses to those cards' fast native bf16/FP8 paths.

</details>


### [194] [A Statistical and Machine Learning Framework for Operational Threshold Detection and Deployable Dispatch Controller Development in Hydrogen Multi-Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14601)
*Shadi Heenatigala,Hasanika Samarasinghe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用一年高分辨率运行数据，结合统计分析与机器学习方法对氢基多能源系统(H-MES)进行表征。统计方法揭示了可再生能源盈余驱动的二元运行模式；机器学习方法（随机森林、序列模型、强化学习）则捕捉到参数方法无法识别的非线性动态，并实现了运行预测与优化调度。核心贡献在于证明了统计与机器学习方法在H-MES建模与控制中是互补的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢基多能源系统(H-MES)运行机制复杂，传统单一分析方法难以全面揭示其动态特性。需要一种综合框架，将统计方法的可解释性与机器学习的非线性建模能力相结合，以深入理解系统运行规律并实现有效控制。

Method: 采用统计分析与机器学习互补框架：(1) 统计层面——使用方差分析和多元回归，量化太阳辐照度对氢产量排名方差的解释率(45.7%)、电力需求的抑制效应(ε²=0.126)，以及电解槽功率作为主导线性预测变量；(2) 机器学习层面——随机森林揭示风力输出的非线性预测重要性（尽管双变量r仅0.167），序列模型利用24小时自相关(r=0.845)进行运行预测，强化学习智能体优化氢能收益调度。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 系统呈二元运行模式，仅在高辐照条件下电解槽才有意义地启动；(2) 太阳辐照度是氢产量方差的最大解释变量(45.7%)，电力需求呈弱抑制效应；(3) 随机森林中风电排名预测重要性第一，尽管其与氢产量的双变量相关系数仅为0.167，揭示了参数方法不可见的非线性动态；(4) 序列模型和强化学习分别实现了有效的运行预测和收益优化调度。

Conclusion: 统计方法与机器学习方法在H-MES建模与控制中具有互补性：统计方法提供可解释的效应量化和线性关系验证，机器学习方法则发掘隐藏的非线性动态并赋能预测与优化控制。这一互补框架为复杂多能源系统的运行分析与智能控制提供了方法论参考。

Abstract: This study presents a statistical and machine learning framework for characterizing a hydrogen-based multi-energy system (H-MES) using one year of high-resolution operational data. Statistical analysis revealed a binary operation driven by renewable surplus, with solar irradiance explaining 45.7% of rank-based variance in hydrogen production, a large effect by conventional standards. Only high-irradiance periods triggered meaningful electrolyzer engagement, while electricity demand exerted a weaker inverse suppression effect ($ε^2 = 0.126$). Multiple regression confirmed electrolyzer power as the dominant linear predictor, with a synergistic solar-wind interaction. Notably, Random Forest analysis ranked wind output first in predictive importance despite its weak bivariate correlation (r = 0.167), revealing non-linear dynamics invisible to parametric methods. A sequence model exploited strong 24-hour autocorrelation (r = 0.845) for operational forecasting, while a reinforcement learning agent optimized hydrogen revenue dispatch. The core contribution is demonstrating that statistical and machine learning approaches are complementary for H-MES modeling and control.

</details>


### [195] [A Comparative Study of Deep Learning Architectures for Multi-Horizon Behavioural Forecasting for Mobile Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14604)
*Pavlos Nicolaou,Kleanthis Malialis,Artemis Kontou,Panayiotis Kolios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地在可穿戴设备行为预测任务上，对六种深度学习架构、两种零样本基础模型和统计基线进行了基准测试，涵盖800+参与者、三种行为特征（步数、屏幕时间、睡眠时长）及1-8天预测范围。核心发现：PatchTST表现最优但无单一架构全面领先；TimesFM基础模型在零样本场景尤其是低数据量下表现匹敌甚至超越训练模型；参与者级微调可将RMSE降低16-60%，睡眠获益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可穿戴设备和智能手机产生了丰富的行为时间序列数据，可用于主动健康干预，但目前缺乏对这些数据在现代预测架构上的系统性对比研究。尤其不清楚模型如何跨人群泛化、不同架构对个体级微调的响应、以及预测精度如何随多天跨度衰减。

Method: 在三个公开数据集（800+参与者）上，对六种深度学习架构（PatchTST、TCN、MLP、Transformer等）、两种零样本基础模型（TimesFM等）和统计基线进行基准测试，报告步数、屏幕时间和睡眠时长的1-8天预测范围的逐特征指标。进一步开展所有六种架构的逐特征个性化研究，并评估基础模型在不同数据集规模和时序粒度下的迁移能力。

Result: （1）无单一架构全面领先，PatchTST在训练模型中表现最佳，TCN、MLP、Transformer三者无显著性能差异；（2）基础模型TimesFM在零样本条件下匹敌甚至超越训练模型，尤其在低数据场景优势明显；（3）参与者级微调使逐特征RMSE降低16-60%，睡眠时长获益最大，步数获益最小。

Conclusion: 该研究为移动健康预测提供了关于架构选择、基础模型适用性及个性化策略的实用指导，是首个联合评估现代深度学习、基础模型与个性化在可穿戴设备多跨度行为预测中的研究。

Abstract: Wearable devices and smartphones generate rich behavioural time series that can support proactive health interventions, yet systematic comparisons of modern forecasting architectures for these data are lacking. In particular, it remains unclear how models generalise across populations, how different architectures respond to participant-level fine-tuning and how forecasting accuracy degrades across multi-day horizons. We benchmark six deep learning architectures, two zero-shot Foundation Models (FM) and statistical baselines on three public datasets encompassing over 800 participants, reporting per-feature metrics for step counts, screen time and sleep duration across 1-8 day horizons. We further conduct a per-feature personalisation study across all six architectures and assess FM transferability across dataset sizes and temporal granularities. Our key findings are: (i) no single architecture dominates, PatchTST leads among trained models while the three runners-up (TCN, MLP, Transformer) show no meaningful performance difference; (ii) the FM TimesFM matches or exceeds trained models zero-shot, especially in low-data regimes and (iii) participant-level fine-tuning reduces per-feature RMSE by 16-60\%, with sleep benefiting most and step counts least. These results provide practical guidance on architecture selection, FM applicability and personalisation strategies for mobile health forecasting. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to jointly evaluate modern deep learning, FMs and personalisation for multi-horizon behavioural forecasting from wearables.

</details>


### [196] [Expert-Driven Survival Machines: Improving Stratification and Interpretability in Multiple Clinical Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14608)
*Farica Zhuang,Zixuan Wen,Christos Davatzikos,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaCSM 是一种结合混合专家（MoE）框架的自适应深度聚类生存分析模型，通过动态路由机制将患者分配给专业化的风险预测器，在保留生存预测和亚型聚类目标的同时，实现异质性生存模式的个性化建模，并在多个真实临床队列上验证了其优越的预测性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生存模型通常为所有患者学习一个共享的特征表示，掩盖了不同患者亚群之间的重要差异。需要一种能够捕捉异质性生存模式、实现个体化风险分层的建模框架。

Method: 提出 AdaCSM 框架，核心是在参数化生存建模框架中引入基于路由的专家机制（Mixture-of-Experts）。模型通过动态路由将患者分配给不同的专业化风险预测器，同时联合优化生存预测目标和亚型聚类目标，实现条件特化的异质性生存模式建模。

Result: 在多个真实世界纵向临床队列（涵盖不同疾病领域）上，与最先进的生存分析模型和深度聚类模型相比，AdaCSM 展现了更优的预测性能，并产生了可解释的分析结果。

Conclusion: AdaCSM 通过 MoE 架构有效捕捉患者异质性生存模式，兼顾预测精度与可解释性，为临床风险分层和个性化患者管理提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Survival prediction plays a central role for healthcare providers and clinical researchers. Accurate risk stratification enables early intervention and improved patient management. Most existing deep survival models learn one common feature representation for all patients, which may hide important differences between patient subgroups. In contrast, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework allows different parts of the model to focus on different patient patterns, leading to more individualized representations. Therefore, in this work, we propose a mixture-of-experts enhanced adaptive deep clustering survival framework (AdaCSM) for modeling such heterogeneous survival patterns. We introduce a routing-based expert mechanism that enables conditional specialization within a parametric survival modeling framework. The proposed architecture allocates patients to specialized risk predictors dynamically while preserving the patient survival and subtype clustering objectives. We compare our method with state-of-the-art survival and deep clustering models on multiple real-world longitudinal clinical cohorts spanning diverse disease domains. The proposed method demonstrates improved predictive performance and leads to interpretable results in survival analysis.

</details>


### [197] [Neither Parallel Nor Sequential: How DiffusionGemma Actually Commits Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14620)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对 DiffusionGemma 26B（基于 Gemma 4 的掩码离散扩散 MoE 模型）的实际解码顺序进行了系统测量，发现其并非真正并行或块自回归，而是呈现部分从左到右的提交偏置，其"块大小"是分析粒度的产物。该方法学贡献在于揭示了测量解码顺序时需处理的五个关键混杂因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放扩散语言模型被宣称是并行的非自回归解码器，但实际部署中模型提交 token 的顺序几乎从未被实证测量过。本文旨在填补这一空白，检验扩散模型解码到底是否真正并行。

Method: 作者对 DiffusionGemma 26B 进行插桩（hook），在采样器的 accept 步骤记录每个 canvas 位置的提交时间、提交顺序及置信度。在 686 个提示、六种解码机制（regime）的探测套件上进行分析，并系统性地考虑了尾部 EOS 填充、机制内混杂、提交非单调性、块大小敏感性及大批次平局等五种方法学挑战。

Result: 解码既非并行也非块自回归，而是呈现部分从左到右的提交偏置；该偏置在逐 token 粒度上很弱，随分析粗化而平滑增强，"块大小"本质上是测量尺度的伪影。模型以大批次同时提交，批次内顺序基本不可定义。行为具有机制依赖性：结构化 JSON 几乎任意顺序提交；在数学推理上提交置信度与正确性相关，但在事实回忆上无信号。提交过程积极激进，在步数预算内的后期短爆发中完成，任务准确率与自回归 Gemma-4 兄弟模型相当。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型的实际解码顺序远非表面宣称的并行或块自回归。本文的核心贡献在于方法论层面：诚实地测量解码顺序必须处理五种关键混杂因素，否则容易制造出虚假的解码顺序结论。

Abstract: Open diffusion language models are marketed as parallel, non-autoregressive decoders, yet the order in which a shipped checkpoint actually commits its tokens is almost never measured. We instrument DiffusionGemma 26B, a masked discrete-diffusion mixture-of-experts model built on Gemma 4, hooking its sampler's accept step to record which canvas positions commit, when, and at what confidence. Across a 686-prompt, six-regime probe suite we find that its decoding is neither parallel nor block-autoregressive: it follows a partial left-to-right commit bias whose apparent strength depends almost entirely on the granularity at which you look. Order is weak token by token and strengthens smoothly as the analysis is coarsened, so the model's "block size" turns out to be an artifact of the measuring ruler rather than the architecture. The model commits in large simultaneous batches, leaving much of the within-batch order genuinely undefined rather than merely unobserved. The behaviour is regime-dependent: structured JSON is committed in essentially arbitrary order, and a position's commit confidence tracks correctness on mathematical reasoning but carries no signal on factual recall. Commitment is aggressive, finishing in a short late burst well inside the step budget, while task accuracy matches the model's autoregressive Gemma-4 sibling. Beyond these findings, our central contribution is methodological: measuring decoding order honestly demands handling trailing-EOS padding, within-regime confounding, commit non-monotonicity, block-size sensitivity, and large commit-batch ties, each of which can otherwise manufacture a decoding-order result that is not really there.

</details>


### [198] [Graph Diffusion Residuals for Control-Function Instrumental Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14636)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie,Defu Lian,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出自适应各向异性工具热流（A-IHF），一种基于图的确定性扩散残差提取器，用于灵活控制函数工具变量估计。它强调控制函数IV估计需要第一阶段的残差而非预测，通过图扩散检测处理变量的跳跃并衰减跨跳跃的电导，从而保留残差信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高容量第一阶段模型可能过度内插处理变量，导致残差信息不足，损害第二阶段结果方程的估计。需要一个能从第一阶段特征图中有效提取残差的方法，而非仅依赖第一阶段预测。

Method: 提出A-IHF：将处理变量视为第一阶段特征图上的信号，利用导频扩散检测处理变量的剧烈跳跃，在这些跳跃处衰减电导，再通过稀疏图预解式（resolvent）计算生成的控制量。模型选择规则仅用(Z,X)，结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可容许性过滤。

Result: 在54个合成基准单元上，经观测选择规则筛选的A-IHF平均结构响应MSE最低；A-IHF家族在32个单元中击败最佳非A-IHF基线。当图能捕捉分段光滑的第一阶段结构时，性能尤为突出。

Conclusion: A-IHF通过显式提取第一阶段残差而非预测，有效解决了高容量第一阶段内插带来的信息不足问题，在合成实验中一致优于树、核、神经网络等传统控制函数方法。

Abstract: Control-function instrumental variable estimators need a first-stage residual, not merely a first-stage prediction. High-capacity first stages can interpolate treatment and leave too little residual information for the outcome equation. We study Adaptive Anisotropic Instrumental Heat Flow (A-IHF), a deterministic graph-diffusion residual extractor for flexible control functions. A-IHF treats treatment as a signal on a graph of first-stage features, uses pilot diffusion to detect large treatment jumps, attenuates conductance across those jumps, and computes the generated control with a sparse graph resolvent. Its observational selection rule uses only $(Z,X)$, combining graph generalized cross-validation, roughness, residualized-treatment relevance, and graph-admissibility filtering. The analysis decomposes error into structural leakage, residual attenuation, and residualized treatment variation, yielding finite-sample bounds, graph-admissibility rates under latent piecewise-smooth geometry, and finite-path selection calibration. Across 54 synthetic benchmark cells with tuned graph, kernel, tree, boosting, series, and neural control-function baselines, guarded observational A-IHF has the lowest average structural-response MSE; the A-IHF family beats the best non-A-IHF baseline in 32 cells. Performance is strongest when the graph captures piecewise-smooth first-stage structure.

</details>


### [199] [Online Convex Optimization with Sublinear Noisy Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14640)
*Simone Di Gregorio,Anupam Gupta,Stefano Leonardi,Matteo Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线凸优化中的“探测”模型：学习者在每轮可选择查询两个点的损失比较（带噪声），预算为 k ≤ T。主要结果表明，即使在完全反馈设定下，次线性且带噪声的探测预算也能可证明地改善最坏情况遗憾。遗憾界在 T、k 和噪声参数 δ 上（对数因子内）是紧的，且当应用于有限专家设置时在所有参数上完全紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合两条近期工作线——专家设置中的次线性最佳专家查询（sublinear best-expert queries）和 OCO 中每轮可用的成对比较反馈——作者提出统一的探测模型，旨在量化少量带噪声的成对探测如何改善在线凸优化的遗憾界。

Method: 引入统一探测框架，学习者有 k ≤ T 次成对探测预算；在探测轮次可查询两点并获知哪一点损失更小（可能含 δ 噪声）。分析上，通过将探测的收益量化为方差削减效应，并结合连续指数权重（Continuous Exponential Weights）的二阶（基于方差的）分析，给出遗憾上界。下界证明采用标准随机化论证，表明上界在 T、k、δ（对数因子内）紧，在有限 K 情形完全紧。

Result: 获得遗憾界 Reg_T ≤ O(min{√(dT ln T), (dT ln T)/(k|1-2δ|)})，在 T、k 和 δ 上（对数因子内）紧。遗憾保证随噪声 δ 接近 1/2（即 oracle 响应趋近抛硬币）而平滑退化。对有限 K 的 d 专家预测设置，得到的速率在所有参数（包括 d）上完全紧。

Conclusion: 即使是次线性且带噪声的成对探测预算也能可证明地改善完全反馈 OCO 中的最坏情况遗憾。统一分析框架通过方差削减效应优雅地刻画了探测的收益，并为在线凸优化中利用比较反馈提供了紧的刻画。

Abstract: We study Online Convex Optimization (OCO) over a convex set $K\subseteq \mathbb R^d$, where in each round $t$ the learner selects $x_t\in K$ and then observes a convex loss $f_t:K\to[0,1]$, with the goal of minimizing regret to the best fixed decision in hindsight. We introduce a unified probing model that generalizes two recent lines of work: sublinear best-expert queries in the experts setting, and pairwise (comparison-based) feedback available every round in OCO. In our framework, the learner has a budget of $k\le T$ pairwise probes; on a probed round it may query two points and learn which one has smaller loss.
  Our main result shows that even a sublinear and noisy probe budget can provably improve worst-case regret in the full feedback OCO regime. With $k$ $δ$-noisy pairwise probes, we obtain: $ \text{Reg}_T \le O\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{dT\ln T},\; \frac{dT\ln T}{k|1-2δ|}\right\}\right) $, which is tight (up to logarithmic factors in $T$) across $T$, $k$ and $δ$. Specifically regarding the noise parameter $δ\in [0,1]$, the regret guarantee smoothly degrades as the oracle response approaches a coin flip, i.e., $δ$ is close to $\frac{1}{2}$. When applying the same techniques to a finite $K$ for the prediction with $d$ experts setting, the resulting rates are instead completely tight in all parameters, including $d$.
  Our analysis gives a streamlined treatment of pairwise probing in OCO by quantifying the benefit of probing via a variance reduction effect, combined with a second-order (variance-based) analysis of Continuous Exponential Weights.

</details>


### [200] [Which Directions Matter? Sparse Design for Affine Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14648)
*Pedro Chumpitaz-Flores,My Duong,Juan S. Borrero,Kaixun Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究鲁棒优化中基于有限字典和预算约束的原子不确定性集构建问题，提出一种数据驱动的子集选择规则，利用覆盖目标在评估方向上选取不确定性方向，证明该目标具有单调次模性，支持贪心算法及(1-1/e)近似保证，并提供损失界证书和半径校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒机器学习和优化的性能高度依赖于不确定性模型的选择。现有方法大多基于预定义的范数球等简单不确定性集，缺乏数据驱动的、有理论保证的不确定性方向选择机制，因此需要研究如何从有限字典中选取最具代表性的不确定性方向子集。

Method: 作者将不确定性集建模为由有限字典和预算约束定义的原子不确定性集，其支撑函数具有闭式形式，针对仿射目标可得到易于求解的鲁棒规划。核心方法包括：提出基于覆盖目标的数据驱动选择规则，覆盖方向涵盖梯度、对抗扰动及留出数据上的分布偏移；证明该覆盖目标是单调次模函数，从而采用贪心算法进行子集选择，获得(1-1/e)近似保证及匹配的困难性下界；此外给出所选子集损失的证书界和具有样本外控制的半径校准规则。

Result: 理论结果：覆盖目标的单调次模性证明，(1-1/e)贪心近似保证及其匹配的硬度障碍。实用工具：损失界证书和半径校准规则，可实现样本外控制。

Conclusion: 通过数据驱动方式从有限字典中选择不确定性方向子集来构建原子不确定性集，是一种兼具理论保证和计算可处理性的方法，为鲁棒优化中不确定性模型的构建提供了新的理论基础和实践工具。

Abstract: Robust machine learning and optimization rely on the uncertainty model choice. We investigate which uncertainty directions a model must cover when defined by a finite dictionary and a budget constraint. Selecting a subset forms an atomic uncertainty set with a closed form support function, yielding tractable robust programs for affine objectives. We propose a data driven selection rule based on a coverage objective over evaluation directions, including gradients, adversarial perturbations, or shifts observed on held out data. We prove this objective is monotone and submodular, supporting a greedy method with a $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantee and a matching hardness barrier. We also provide a certificate bounding the loss from the selected subset and a radius calibration rule with out of sample control.

</details>


### [201] [Graph Structured Combinatorial Semi-Bandit with Nonlinear Reward Associations through Separable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14650)
*Christoph Bauschmann,Setareh Maghsudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类通用自适应策略，结合基于图的因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法以及函数过程的Taylor近似，用于在非线性依赖关系下高效识别大规模互联数据中的最优结构。理论上实现了时间次线性、数据量线性的性能保证，并验证了对噪声、模型收敛和求解空间失配的鲁棒性。在合成数据集和真实交通数据集上的数值实验证明了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模互联数据中的最优结构识别面临巨大采样与计算开销，而非线性统计关系的普遍存在进一步加剧了这一复杂性。现有方法难以同时兼顾效率与预测能力，亟需能学习并利用底层信号依赖关系的通用且高效的自适应策略。

Method: 提出通用自适应策略框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于图的因果奖励建模；(2) 解析再生核方法（analytic reproducing kernel methods）；(3) 函数过程的Taylor近似。该框架仅依赖极简的条件集或先验估计，并提供多种针对特定或扩展场景的改进方案。

Result: 理论上，建立了时间次线性（sublinear in time）、数据量线性（linear in data volume over time）的性能保证；分析涵盖了对噪声干扰、模型渐进收敛及求解空间失配等多种不确定性来源的鲁棒性。数值实验在基准合成数据集和真实交通数据集上均验证了方法的实用有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架以极简的假设条件实现了通用性，在理论和实践层面均展现出优越性能，为大规模非线性互联数据中的最优结构识别提供了一种高效、鲁棒且适应性强的解决方案。

Abstract: The identification of optimal structures within vast arrays of interconnected data necessitates significant sampling- and computational effort. Learning and leveraging underlying signal dependencies can improve efficiency and predictive capabilities considerably, but the ubiquity of nonlinear statistical relations amplifies the complexity of such undertakings. In this paper, we develop novel generic and adaptive strategies equipped with routines for graph-based causal reward modeling, analytic reproducing kernel methods, and Taylor approximation of functional processes. We establish theoretical performance guarantees sublinear in time and linear in data volume over time. Our analyses cover robustness to a multitude of uncertainties arising from noise interference, gradual model convergence, and solution space mismatch. The framework's general appeal is substantiated by a minimalistic set of conditions or reliance on prior estimates, while various outlined modifications address specific or extended settings. To demonstrate practical effectiveness, we conduct numerical experiments using both benchmarked synthetic and real-world transportation datasets.

</details>


### [202] [Beyond task performance: Decoding bioacoustic embeddings with speech features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14662)
*Ines Nolasco,Jules Cauzinille,Marius Miron,Gagan Narula,Milad Alizadeh,Emmanuel Fernandez,Matthieu Geist,Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon,Olivier Pietquin,Emmanuel Chemla,Sara Keen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过线性与非线性回归探针，量化了六种分类群上预训练音频嵌入对88个eGeMAPS声学特征的可恢复性，揭示了不同模型编码的声学特征存在互补性，拼接嵌入性能最优，并基于特征可恢复性与物种特征显着性（NMI）提出了数据驱动的模型选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练音频嵌入在生物声学中广泛使用，但缺乏对其编码了哪些声学特征、以及哪些特征对特定任务有用的理解，这限制了模型透明度和向稀有物种或数据稀缺领域的扩展能力。

Method: 采用88个eGeMAPS语音特征，覆盖六个分类群；应用线性回归和非线性回归探针（包括MLP等）量化不同预训练模型嵌入对各声学特征的可恢复性（以R²衡量）；进一步将特征可恢复性与每种物种的特征显着性（NMI）进行交叉参照，形成模型选择建议。

Result: 确认"没有免费午餐"模式：没有单一模型能覆盖完整特征空间；拼接嵌入在所有模型中性能最高，表明模型间声学空间覆盖互补；响度（Loudness）特征最易恢复（R²=0.76），基频（F0）最难恢复（R²=0.33）。

Conclusion: 不同预训练模型编码的声学特征互补而非重叠，通过特征可恢复性分析与物种特征显着性交叉参照，可以为特定生物声学任务提供数据驱动的模型选择依据。

Abstract: Pretrained audio embeddings are standard in bioacoustics, yet little is known about which acoustic features these models encode, nor which are useful for a given task. This hinders transparency and limits extension to rare species or data-scarce domains. Here we reveal which speech-like features are encoded in bioacoustic representations. Using the 88~eGeMAPS features across six taxonomic groups, we apply linear and nonlinear regression probes to quantify which acoustic properties each model captures. Results confirm a ``no free lunch'' pattern: no single model captures the full feature space. A concatenated embedding achieves the highest performance, suggesting complementary acoustic space coverage across models. Loudness features are best encoded ($R^2 = 0.76$) while F0 is hardest to recover ($R^2 = 0.33$). By cross-referencing recoverability with per-species feature salience (NMI), we derive data-driven model selection guidance for bioacoustics.

</details>


### [203] [When to Write and When to Suppress: Route-Specialized Dual Adapters for Memory-Assisted Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14668)
*Yining Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种路由特化的双适配器知识编辑方法，通过相关性路由器决定何时注入编辑记忆、何时抑制编辑，将编辑注入与离路由抑制分离，在三个基准上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑系统主要关注"如何写入编辑"，但忽略了"何时抑制编辑"这一关键问题。在记忆辅助的推理场景中，无关提示不应被编辑记忆干扰，需要一个机制来区分需要编辑的提示与需要保持原始行为的提示。

Method: 提出双适配器架构：(1) 相关性路由器（lexical neural router 或 BGE embedding router）判断提示是否应接收编辑记忆；(2) 被路由的提示使用编辑适配器，训练为偏好新对象而非原始对象；(3) 未被路由的非直接提示使用局部性适配器，训练为保持或恢复原始对象偏好。两个适配器均采用参数高效的方式（LoRA等）。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，方法在三个1000样本基准上均取得最佳概率偏好准确率：CounterFact 0.8180、ZsRE 0.8946、MQuAKE 0.9922。Qwen3-8B上趋势一致。路由器消融实验表明不同数据集的最优路由边界不同：CounterFact上词汇神经路由器最安全，ZsRE和MQuAKE上BGE嵌入路由更优。组件消融显示性能增益主要来自编辑注入与离路由抑制的分离，而非单纯增加LoRA容量。

Conclusion: 知识编辑系统的核心设计问题不仅是"如何写"，更是"何时抑制"。通过路由特化的双适配器将编辑与抑制解耦，在保持编辑精度的同时有效保护了无关行为的局部性，为记忆辅助知识编辑提供了新范式。

Abstract: Knowledge editing systems must update selected facts while preserving nearby but irrelevant behavior. This paper studies this problem in a memory-assisted setting where an edit memory is retrieved at inference time and a parameter-efficient adapter corrects the model's object preference. We argue that the central design question is not only how to write an edit, but also when to suppress it. We introduce \method{}, a route-specialized dual-adapter editor. A relevance router first decides whether a prompt should receive an edit memory. Routed prompts use an edit adapter trained to prefer the new object over the original object; unrouted non-direct prompts use a separate locality adapter trained to preserve or restore the original-object preference. We evaluate \method{} on three 1,000-case protocols, \cf{}, \zsre{}, and \mquake{}, under the same memory protocol and two 7B/8B base models. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, \method{} obtains the best overall probability-preference accuracy on all three benchmarks: 0.8180 on \cf{}, 0.8946 on \zsre{}, and 0.9922 on \mquake{}. The same trend holds on Qwen3-8B. Router ablations show that the relevant memory boundary differs across datasets: a lexical neural router is safest on \cf{}, while BGE embedding routing is better on \zsre{} and \mquake{}. Component and module ablations show that the gain mainly comes from separating edit injection from off-route suppression rather than from simply increasing LoRA capacity.

</details>


### [204] [Compressed Computation is (probably) not Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14673)
*Jai Bhagat,Sara Molas-Medina,Giorgi Giglemiani,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了压缩计算（CC）玩具模型是否为叠加计算的一个实例，发现其性能提升来源于标签中的非预期混合矩阵，而非真正的叠加计算。通过分解训练目标、分析神经元方向以及使用SNMF基线，作者得出结论：CC模型不是叠加计算的合适玩具模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Braun等人（2025）提出的压缩计算模型似乎仅用50个神经元就能计算100个ReLU函数，表面上看像是叠加计算。本文旨在严格检验CC模型是否真的是叠加计算的有效实例，还是其性能增益另有来源。

Method: 1. 将训练目标分解为ReLU项和混合项，分别衡量二者的贡献；2. 分析学习到的神经元方向在混合矩阵特征子空间中的集中程度；3. 构建基于半非负矩阵分解（SNMF）的基线，仅利用混合矩阵复现损失曲线，与训练模型对比。

Result: 性能增益与混合矩阵的规模成正比，移除混合矩阵后增益消失；神经元方向集中在混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间中；SNMF基线能复现定性损失曲线并优于先前基线，但仍不及训练模型。

Conclusion: CC模型的性能提升源于标签中的混合矩阵，而非真正的叠加计算。因此，CC模型不适合作为叠加计算的玩具模型，研究社区应寻找或设计更恰当的模型来研究这一现象。

Abstract: We study whether the Compressed Computation (CC) toy model (Braun et al., 2025) is an instance of computation in superposition. The CC model appears to compute 100 ReLU functions with just 50 neurons, achieving a better loss than expected from only representing 50 ReLU functions. We show that the model mixes inputs via its noisy residual stream, corresponding to an unintended mixing matrix in the labels. Splitting the training objective into the ReLU term and the mixing term, we find that performance gains scale with the magnitude of the mixing matrix and vanish when the matrix is removed. The learned neuron directions concentrate in the subspace associated with the top 50 eigenvalues of the mixing matrix, suggesting that the mixing term governs the solution. Finally, a semi-non-negative matrix factorization (SNMF) baseline derived solely from the mixing matrix reproduces the qualitative loss profile and improves on prior baselines, though it does not match the trained model. These results suggest CC is not a suitable toy model of computation in superposition.

</details>


### [205] [A Complexity Measure for Active Learning in Multi-group Mean Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14690)
*Abdellah Aznag,Rachel Cummings,Adam N. Elmachtoub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多组均值估计的$d$臂老虎机中主动学习的最大风险目标，建立了局部极小极大框架，给出了首个一般下界。下界将难度分解为预算项、异方差指数和方差局部曲率（VLC）三个正交因素。对于光滑类，VLC是方差-Fisher信息的重新参数化。证明引入了损失诱导的$\ell_1$几何和基于表示的例子生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习中，学习者需在预算约束下自适应分配样本以最小化最坏情况的不确定性。现有文献缺乏对最大风险目标的统一理论理解，尤其缺少适用于任意有限方差假设类的一般下界。本文旨在填补这一空白，揭示难度来源并指导最优策略设计。

Method: 作者建立局部极小极大框架，将下界分解为三个正交因素：预算项、异方差指数和方差局部曲率(VLC)。证明引入两个关键技术：(1) 决策空间上由损失诱导的$\ell_1$几何结构；(2) 基于表示的实例生成器，将困难实例构造转化为显式随机矩阵计算。对于光滑类，给出VLC的闭式表达，并与已知最强上界进行对标。

Result: 获得了首个针对最大风险目标的一般下界，适用于任意有限方差假设类。在广泛区域内，下界与最强已知上界匹配至对数因子，证明近最优性；同时精准定位了高度异质实例中的系统性差距。对于常见分布族，VLC具有闭式值。

Conclusion: 该工作为多组老虎机中的最大风险主动学习提供了统一的下界框架，清晰分离了难度来源。结果揭示了现有上界在异质场景下的改进空间，并为未来算法设计指明了方向。

Abstract: We study a \emph{max-risk} objective for active learning in a multi-group mean estimation $d$-armed bandits: a learner adaptively allocates a budget of $T$ samples across $d$ groups to minimize the worst-case uncertainty index $\max_{k\in[d]}σ_k^2/n_k$, where $σ_k$ is the standard deviation of the distribution of arm $d$, and $n_k$ is the number of times arm $d$ is sampled. We develop a local minimax framework and prove the first general lower bound for this objective, valid for any finite-variance hypothesis class. The bound separates difficulty into three orthogonal factors: a \emph{budget} term, a \emph{heteroscedasticity} index measuring how unevenly the uncertainty is spread across arms, and a model-dependent complexity measure, the \emph{Variance Local Curvature} ($\mathrm{VLC}$), which captures how much information a local change of variance creates inside the hypothesis class. For smooth classes, the $\mathrm{VLC}$ is a reparametrization of a variance--Fisher information, with closed-form values for common families. Benchmarking against the strongest available upper bound shows near-optimality up to logarithmic factors in broad regimes, and pinpoints a systematic gap in highly heterogeneous instances. Our proof introduces two key ingredients: a loss-induced $\ell_1$ geometry on the decision space, and a representation-based instance generator that reduces hard-instance construction to an explicit random matrix calculation.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [LoMC: Localized Multidirectional Correction for Refusal Suppression in Routed Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13709)
*Yan Hong,Kedong Xiu,Wei Li,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Shuheng Zhou,Zhongcai Lyu,Weiqiang Wang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: LoMC 提出一种"先定位支持集、后校正"的支撑门控干预框架，在路由式 MoE 和混合 MoE 模型中，以紧凑的干预范围实现受控的拒绝抑制，提升目标响应行为的同时保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两难：基于方向的全局编辑会干扰通用计算，而仅编辑支持专家又因容量不足无法纠正异构的拒绝表征。因此需要一种既能增强校正容量、又不扩大干预范围的新方法。

Method: 提出 Localized Multidirectional Correction (LoMC)，采用 support-then-correction 执行顺序：(1) 先识别紧凑的编辑支持集（edit support）；(2) 将原型校正方向聚合为逐层校正方向；(3) 仅在选定的支持集内施加秩一逐层校正。利用编辑支持集作为结构性门控约束，在不扩大干预范围的前提下增加校正容量。

Result: 在四个路由式骨干模型上，针对纯文本和多模态安全基准的实验表明，LoMC 显著改善了非拒绝目标响应行为，同时在紧凑干预范围下保持了通用能力。

Conclusion: LoMC 通过支撑门控的局部多方向校正，有效解决了路由式 MoE 模型中拒绝抑制的干预容量与范围之间的权衡问题，在安全对齐编辑任务上实现了优越的性能。

Abstract: We study controlled post-training refusal suppression in routed MoE and hybrid-MoE foundation models, aiming to increase non-refusal target-response behavior while preserving general capability under a compact intervention footprint. Existing broad direction-based edits can perturb general-purpose computation, whereas support-only expert edits often lack sufficient capacity to correct heterogeneous refusal representations. To address this limitation, we introduce Localized Multidirectional Correction (LoMC), a support-gated intervention framework that follows a support-then-correction execution order: it first identifies a compact edit support, then aggregates prototype correction directions into layer-wise correction directions, and finally applies rank-one layer-wise correction only within the selected support. By using the edit support as a structural gating constraint, LoMC increases correction capacity without expanding the intervention scope. Experiments on text-only and multimodal safety benchmarks across four routed backbones show that LoMC substantially improves non-refusal target-response behavior while maintaining general capability under a compact intervention footprint.

</details>


### [207] [Recursively Trained Diffusion Models: Limiting Collapse Distribution and Spectral Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13796)
*Naïl B. Khelifa,Richard E. Turner,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了递归训练生成模型时模型崩溃的极限行为：即使完美评分估计和精确采样，反向扩散的早期停止也会导致向唯一极限分布的几何收敛，该极限可闭式表征为数据分布的高斯平滑混合；Hermite谱分解揭示递归训练相当于低通滤波器，高阶模衰减更快；基于此提出退火截断调度可渐近消除递归累积；在离散化和估计误差下极限仍具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型递归训练的理论工作仅限定了有限轮次的误差累积，但两个核心问题悬而未决：递归最终收敛到什么分布？收敛速度多快？作者旨在回答这两个问题，并隔离出一个与不完美学习不同的机制。

Method: 作者将递归训练建模为反向扩散早期停止的迭代过程，利用Hermite谱分解分析每轮递归在函数空间上的算子作用，证明该算子是一个低通滤波器；推导出极限分布的闭式解（无限高斯平滑混合）；提出退火截断调度并证明收敛到零的调度可渐近消除递归累积；进一步分析离散化和评分估计误差下的鲁棒性，给出Wasserstein-2球内的误差界及模态依赖的收缩率。

Result: 1）递归训练以几何速率收敛到唯一极限分布，该极限是数据分布经递增高斯平滑的无限混合；2）Hermite谱分解表明递归训练是低通滤波器，高阶非高斯模态比低阶粗模态衰减更剧烈；3）任何收敛到0的退火截断调度可渐近消除递归累积；4）在有离散化和评分估计误差时，学习分布保持在理想极限的Wasserstein-2球内，且高阶误差收缩快于低阶误差；5）在合成高斯混合和CIFAR-10上验证了理论。

Conclusion: 即使完美学习，扩散模型递归训练中的早期停止机制也会驱动模型崩溃，但该过程收敛到可闭式刻画的唯一极限分布。通过谱分析揭示的低通滤波特性，可设计退火截断调度来消除递归累积。理论框架对实际中的离散化和估计误差具有鲁棒性，为理解和缓解模型崩溃提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recursive training of generative models on their own outputs can lead to model collapse, a compounding drift away from the true data distribution. Existing theoretical works bound finite-round error accumulation in the context of diffusion models, but two questions remain open:~what distribution does the recursion converge to, and how fast? We answer both, isolating a mechanism distinct from imperfect learning: even with perfect score estimation and exact sampling, the early stopping of the reverse diffusion (required for numerical stability) drives a progressive drift away from the data distribution. We prove that this recursion converges geometrically to a unique limiting distribution, which admits a closed-form characterization as an infinite mixture of increasingly Gaussian-smoothed versions of the data distribution. A Hermite spectral decomposition of this limit reveals that recursive training acts as a low-pass filter: higher-order modes, which encode fine non-Gaussian structure, are attenuated much more strongly than coarse modes. This spectral picture motivates annealed truncation schedules that progressively shrink truncation times across retraining rounds; we prove that any schedule converging to $0$ asymptotically eliminates recursive compounding. Finally, we show our idealized characterization is robust: in the presence of discretization and score estimation errors, the learned distribution remains in a Wasserstein-2 ball around the ideal limit, with mode-dependent contraction rates that contract high-order errors faster than low-order ones. We validate the theory on synthetic Gaussian mixtures and CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [208] [Adaptive Nucleus Truncation for Long-Form Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13982)
*Ousmane Amadou Dia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ANTS 是一种自适应采样方法，将 top-nσ 从固定截断规则扩展为熵条件控制的自适应机制，在长文本推理中显著提升性能，尤其在 32K 预算下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有截断采样方法（如 top-p、min-p、固定 top-nσ）依赖固定阈值，无法根据熵变化、任务难度、训练阶段或生成长度预算进行自适应调整，导致长文本推理中采样策略次优。

Method: 提出 ANTS（Adaptive Nucleus Truncation Sampling）：(1) 在温度缩放前，围绕最大 logit 选择标准化邻域；(2) 使用熵条件控制器自适应调整截断宽度；(3) 保留无截断 fallback arm，在截断不安全时稳定训练。该方法将 top-nσ 从固定解码规则升级为自适应 rollout 控制机制。

Result: 在 33B 总参数量/4B 激活参数的稀疏 MoE 推理模型上，ANTS 在 8K、16K、32K 预算下分别提升 +1.9、+3.8、+5.2 个百分点。IFBench 在 32K 下提升超 10 分，AIME 2025 提升 7 分。Codeforces 上 8K 时略逊于基线，但在 16K 和 32K 下显著反超并提升 ELO。

Conclusion: 采样器设计不应仅被视为解码超参数，而应作为稳定和扩展长预算推理的核心组成部分。ANTS 证明了自适应截断在长文本生成中的有效性，且存在预算-任务的交互效应。

Abstract: Sampling plays an important role in long-form language-model reasoning. Over thousands of decoding steps, small changes in the candidate token set can compound into different reasoning trajectories, stability profiles, and final answers. Existing truncation methods such as top-$p$, min-$p$, and fixed top-$nσ$ sampling improve over unrestricted sampling, but they rely on fixed thresholds that cannot adapt to changes in entropy, task difficulty, training stage, or generation budget. We introduce Adaptive Nucleus Truncation Sampling (ANTS), which extends top-\(nσ\) sampling from a fixed decoding rule into an adaptive rollout-control mechanism for long-form generation. ANTS selects standardized neighborhoods around the maximum logit before temperature scaling, adapts the truncation width using an entropy-conditioned controller, and retains a no-truncation fallback arm to stabilize training when truncation becomes unsafe. On a 33B-total / 4B-active sparse Mixture-of-Experts reasoning model, ANTS improves average performance over percentage-based benchmarks by +1.9, +3.8, and +5.2 points at 8K, 16K, and 32K generation budgets, respectively. The strongest gains appear on instruction following and mathematical reasoning, with IFBench improving by more than 10 points at 32K and AIME 2025 improving by 7 points. Code generation reveals an important budget interaction. On Codeforces, ANTS trails the baseline at 8K, but reverses this gap and substantially improves ELO at 16K and 32K. These results suggest that sampler design should be treated not just as a decoding hyperparameter, but as part of how we stabilize and scale long-budget reasoning.

</details>


### [209] [A General Framework for Decision Trees via Bregman Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13984)
*Mathias Bourel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Bregman散度的CART决策树泛化框架，将传统CART中的不纯度准则统一到凸分析与信息几何的框架下，并研究了生成凸函数性质（如强凸性、光滑性）对树的 impurity gain 及估计量稳定性与一致性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CART及其变体对不同统计模型引入不纯度准则时多为ad hoc方式，缺乏统一的理论框架。Bregman散度作为平方欧氏距离的自然推广，涵盖了KL散度、Poisson散度、Itakura-Saito散度及指数族分布对应的多种损失函数，且具有丰富的几何结构。因此，作者希望利用Bregman散度统一推导和解释不同决策树的不纯度准则，从而获得适应不同统计模型和底层几何的更广泛的决策树族。

Method: 作者将CART范式推广到基于Bregman散度的框架：用Bregman散度替代传统平方误差作为分裂准则和不纯度度量，将节点预测值定义为Bregman重心（即条件期望的Bregman推广）。在算法层面，给出了适用于任意Bregman散度的统一分裂准则推导。在理论层面，研究了生成凸函数的强凸性和光滑性等性质如何影响父节点到子节点的impurity gain，以及这些性质对决策树估计量的稳定性和一致性的作用。

Result: 构建了一个统一的Bregman CART框架，使得不同分布假设下的不纯度准则（如KL散度对应泊松或多项分布模型，Itakura-Saito散度对应乘性噪声模型等）可以从共同的凸几何原理中自然导出。理论上证明了生成凸函数的强凸性与光滑性直接影响impurity gain的上下界，进而影响树的分裂行为和估计量的统计性质（稳定性、一致性）。

Conclusion: 基于Bregman散度的决策树泛化不仅统一了现有多种CART变体的不纯度准则，还提供了系统的理论工具来分析树的性质。该框架为根据具体数据分布和几何结构选择或设计合适的决策树提供了原则性指导，拓展了决策树在更广泛统计模型中的应用潜力。
