Abstract: Decision trees are one of the fundamental tools in statistical learning due to their interpretability, flexibility, and their ability to adapt to nonlinear structures. Among them, the Classification and Regression Trees, introduced by Breiman, Friedman, Olshen, and Stone in 1984, became one of the most influential algorithms and remains one of the most widely used methods for classification and regression problems. On the other hand, Bregman divergences, introduced by Lev Bregman in 1967 in the context of convex optimization, provide a broad family of loss functions that naturally generalize the squared Euclidean distance. This family includes, among others, the Kullback-Leibler divergence, the Poisson divergence, and the Itakura-Saito divergence, as well as several losses associated with distributions belonging to the exponential family. Moreover, Bregman divergences possess a rich geometric structure and deep connections with convex analysis and information geometry. In this work, we propose a generalization of the CART paradigm based on Bregman divergences, thereby obtaining a broader family of decision trees adapted to different statistical models and underlying geometries. Although algorithms such as CART or classical implementations such as rpart incorporate different impurity criteria, these are usually introduced in an ad hoc manner for each specific model. In contrast, the Bregman divergence approach provides a unified framework that allows these criteria to be derived and interpreted from common convex and geometric principles. Beyond the algorithmic construction, we also investigate theoretical properties of these trees. In particular, we study how properties of the generating convex function -- such as strong convexity or smoothness -- influence impurity gains between parent and child nodes, as well as stability and consistency properties of the estimator.

</details>


### [210] [Geometric Domain Adaptation via Optimal Transport for Linear Regression in R^2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14023)
*Brian Britos,Mathias Bourel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究了 $\mathbb{R}^2$ 中源域和目标域之间存在旋转、平移或缩放变换的监督域适应问题。理论上证明了当使用 $p \geq 2$ 的 $p$-范数代价时，最优传输映射可以恢复底层几何变换。基于此，提出结合 $K$-means 与最优传输的方法来估计该映射，从而在目标数据稀缺时实现线性回归模型的适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在域适应中，源域和目标域的分布差异往往呈现为简单的几何变换（旋转、平移、缩放）。现有方法多依赖高表达力的深度学习架构，缺乏理论可解释性。本文旨在从经典机器学习视角出发，明确刻画最优传输在恢复此类几何变换中的理论角色，并提供一种概念清晰、可解释的域适应方法。

Method: 1. 理论层面：在 $\mathbb{R}^2$ 中，当源域和目标域存在旋转、平移或缩放关系时，证明采用 $p \geq 2$ 的 $p$-范数代价的最优传输映射能够精确恢复该底层几何变换。2. 方法层面：提出将 $K$-means 聚类与最优传输相结合的方法，首先利用 $K$-means 对数据进行结构化，再通过最优传输估计源域到目标域的几何映射，最终实现线性回归模型的域适应，尤其适用于目标数据稀缺的场景。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所提方法相较于基线方法具有更优的性能。理论结果明确建立了最优传输与旋转、平移、缩放变换之间的内在联系。

Conclusion: 本文在 $\mathbb{R}^2$ 空间内，从理论和实践两个层面证明了最优传输在恢复几何变换（旋转、平移、缩放）中的有效性。所提出的 $K$-means 结合最优传输的方法，为小样本目标域条件下的线性回归模型适应提供了可解释且实用的解决方案，强调了经典模型在域适应任务中的价值。

Abstract: Optimal Transport has become recently a powerful method for domain adaptation by aligning source and target distributions. We study a supervised domain adaptation problem where source and target domains are related by a rotation or a translation or a homothety in $\mathbb{R}^2$. We prove that the optimal transport map recovers the underlying map when using a $p-$norm cost with $p \geq 2$. Based on this insight, we develop a method combining $K-$means and optimal transport to estimate the underlying map, enabling adaptation of linear regression models when target data is scarce. Simulations demonstrate improved performance over baseline methods. Rather than relying on highly expressive deep learning architectures, we focus on classical machine learning models to emphasize interpretability and theoretical insight. This perspective allows us to explicitly characterize the role of optimal transport in recovering geometric transformations such as rotations, translations, and homotheties.
  Our contributions include a theoretical result linking optimal transport and rotations, translations and homothecies in $\mathbb{R}^2$, and a practical method for adaptation in linear regression offering both conceptual clarity and applied value in domain adaptation tasks in this space.

</details>


### [211] [Anytime-Valid Confirmation of Label-Shift Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14028)
*Seungjin Choi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对小批量科学部署中标签偏移难以估计的问题，本文提出将领域知识预设的标签偏移校正转化为一个序贯确认规则：通过逐观测似然比构造条件e值和鞅，利用Ville不等式实现随时有效的假设检验，将常规模型监控转化为源预测与校正预测间累积NLPD差距的检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小批量科学部署场景中，即使有未标记目标输入，标记目标结果往往过于稀缺，无法可靠地估计标签偏移。本文针对这一互补设置：实践者已从领域知识中获得预设的标签偏移校正，需要判断新到达的标记结果是否支持该校正假设。

Method: 将标签偏移校正后的预测与源预测之间的逐观测似然比定义为条件e值（conditional e-value），其累积乘积构成非负鞅；利用Ville不等式构建随时有效的序贯确认规则。对数鞅等价于源预测与校正预测之间的累积负对数预测密度（NLPD）差距，将常规模型监控转化为正式的序贯检验。对于高斯过程源模型和高斯标签偏移比，给出了闭式解。

Result: 高斯过程回归模拟验证了该方法在以下方面的表现：(1) Type I错误控制；(2) 有限样本检验功效；(3) 误校准敏感性；(4) 相较于基于标签重新估计的方法，可靠先验在小批量场景下具有优势。

Conclusion: 该方法为小批量部署中的标签偏移确认提供了一种形式化的序贯检验框架，能够在标记数据稀缺时有效利用领域知识先验，且无需精确估计偏移程度即可判断数据是否支持预设校正。

Abstract: In small-batch scientific deployments, labeled target outcomes may be too scarce for reliable shift estimation even when unlabeled target inputs are available. We address the complementary setting where the practitioner has a pre-specified label-shift correction from domain knowledge and asks whether incoming labeled outcomes support it. We show that the per-observation likelihood ratio between a label-shift-corrected predictive and the source predictive is a conditional e-value, so its running product is a nonnegative martingale and Ville's inequality yields an anytime-valid confirmation rule. The log martingale equals the cumulative negative log-predictive density (NLPD) gap between the source and the corrected predictive, converting routine model monitoring into a formal sequential test. Rejection means the incoming data support the posited correction relative to the source predictive, but it is not a precise estimate of the degree of shift. Closed forms are available for GP sources with Gaussian label-shift ratios. GP regression simulations validate Type I control, finite-sample power, miscalibration sensitivity, and the small-batch advantage of a reliable prior over label-based re-estimation.

</details>


### [212] [Hybrid Uncertainty Sensitivity Analysis Based on the HSIC for High-Dimensional Responses with Aleatory--Epistemic Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14053)
*Shijie Zhong,Jiangfeng Fu,Pengfei Wei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于双空间张量积RKHS框架的全局敏感性分析方法，通过双重Möbius反演正交分解混合不确定性的主效应及交互效应，实现高维多输出系统在偶然与认知不确定性下的敏感性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于HSIC的全局敏感性分析方法多局限于单输出场景，缺乏对异质不确定性源（偶然/认知）及其交互作用的严格分解，无法有效处理高维多输出系统在混合不确定性下的敏感性归因问题。

Method: 1. 构建潜输入空间与多输出空间上的因子化核函数，形成双空间张量积RKHS；2. 推导并发双重Möbius反演，将全局依赖测度正交分解为纯偶然效应、纯认知效应及交互贡献；3. 引入基于逆概率积分变换的辅助变量表示，统一处理层次化不确定性与Copula相关结构；4. 开发全矢量单循环蒙特卡洛实现，避免嵌套模拟；5. 通过置换检验和Bootstrap置信区间量化统计显著性与估计不确定性。

Result: 在改进的多输出Ishigami函数和气动压力场问题上的数值研究表明，所提框架在准确性、可扩展性和实际适用性方面均表现良好，能够有效量化和分解多维输出的混合不确定性敏感性指标。

Conclusion: 该框架突破了传统HSIC方法在混合不确定性和多输出系统中的局限，为高维系统全局敏感性分析提供了严格的分解理论与高效的计算方案。

Abstract: Quantifying the influence of hybrid aleatory and epistemic uncertainties on high-dimensional system responses remains a major challenge in global sensitivity analysis (GSA). Existing Hilbert--Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC)-based approaches are primarily restricted to single-output settings and lack a rigorous decomposition of heterogeneous uncertainty sources and their interactions. To address this limitation, a novel double-space tensor-product RKHS framework is proposed for sensitivity analysis under hybrid uncertainty. By constructing factorized kernels over both the latent input space and the multidimensional output space, a concurrent double Möbius inversion is derived to orthogonally decompose the global dependence measure into pure aleatory effects, pure epistemic effects, and their interaction contributions. The resulting dimension-wise sensitivity indices preserve the uncertainty attribution structure across all output dimensions. To satisfy the independence assumptions required by the decomposition, an auxiliary-variable representation based on the inverse probability integral transform is introduced, enabling the treatment of hierarchical uncertainties and Copula-induced correlations within a unified latent space. A fully vectorized single-loop implementation is further developed to avoid the computational burden of nested Monte Carlo simulation. Statistical significance and estimation uncertainty are quantified through permutation testing and Bootstrap confidence intervals. Numerical studies on a modified multi-output Ishigami function and an aerodynamic pressure-field problem demonstrate the accuracy, scalability, and practical applicability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [213] [Gradient boosting for extremes: sampling theory and application to insurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14268)
*Stéphane Lhaut,Olivier Lopez*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为协变量依赖的广义帕累托分布（GP）梯度提升估计建立了统计学习理论框架。通过对GP似然进行正交重参数化以对角化Fisher信息矩阵，作者将估计问题纳入经验风险最小化（ERM）框架，并推导了提升估计量的非渐近误差界。分析区分了三类误差源：统计波动、二阶正则变差控制的GP模型渐近近似偏差、以及有限提升迭代次数的逼近误差，明确刻画了偏差-方差权衡。仿真表明重参数化显著降低梯度相关性并改善收敛稳定性。该方法应用于德州医疗责任险数据集（超18000条已结赔案），梯度提升对结案成本尾部拟合良好，并揭示结案天数是尾部厚度的主要预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端事件的尾部建模（如保险理赔）常依赖于协变量依赖的GP分布，但现有方法缺乏严格的统计学习理论支撑，尤其在梯度提升框架下。作者旨在填补这一空白，同时解决GP似然参数化导致的梯度相关性和收敛不稳定问题。

Method: 首先对GP似然进行正交重参数化，使其Fisher信息矩阵对角化；然后将估计问题纳入ERM框架，推导梯度提升估计量的非渐近误差界，将总误差分解为统计误差、二阶正则变差控制的渐近近似偏差、以及提升迭代次数的逼近误差三部分；最后通过仿真验证重参数化效果，并在德州医疗责任险真实数据上进行实证应用。

Result: 理论上得到了非渐近误差界，清晰展示了偏差-方差权衡；仿真中正交重参数化显著降低了训练过程中的梯度相关性，提升了收敛稳定性；实证应用中梯度提升方法对结案成本尾部拟合良好，且发现"结案天数"是尾部厚度最重要的预测变量，与准备金文献中的既有发现一致。

Conclusion: 该研究为梯度提升在协变量依赖极值建模中的应用提供了坚实的统计学习理论基础。正交重参数化策略有效缓解了优化中的病态问题，理论与实证结果均支持该方法在保险尾部风险建模中的实用价值。

Abstract: We develop a statistical learning theory for gradient boosting applied to the estimation of covariate-dependent Generalized Pareto (GP) distributions in the context of Peaks-over-Threshold modeling. After an orthogonal reparametrization of the GP likelihood that diagonalizes its Fisher information matrix, we cast the estimation problem within the Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) framework and derive non-asymptotic error bounds for the boosting estimator. Our analysis accounts for three distinct sources of error in the process: statistical fluctuations, the approximation bias inherent to the asymptotic nature of the GP model-controlled under second-order regular variation-and the approximation error associated with the finite number of boosting iterates, making explicit the resulting bias-variance trade-off. We illustrate the practical benefits of the reparametrization through simulations, showing that it significantly reduces gradient correlation during training and improves convergence stability. The methodology is applied to a medical malpractice insurance dataset from the Texas Department of Insurance, comprising over 18 000 closed claims. The gradient boosting approach yields a good fit for the tail of settlement cost distributions and reveals that the number of days to settlement is the dominant predictor of tail heaviness, consistent with earlier findings in the reserving literature.

</details>


### [214] [Nonlocal Bayesian Modeling of Continuous Spatio-Temporal Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14313)
*Jaeyeong Lee,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: NLBST 是一个用于连续时空场的层次化贝叶斯框架，通过空间基展开和神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）学习显式的非局部耦合，结合线性高斯观测模型实现卡尔曼滤波式推理，在非局部和部分观测场景下表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时空预测需同时应对不规则时间点、空间稀疏观测、不确定性量化以及非局部（长程）耦合，而连续时空非局部动力学自然导向无限维积分-微分方程，导致精确贝叶斯推断不可行。

Method: NLBST 采用三层结构：(1) 用坐标基函数展开表示连续空间隐变量场；(2) 将展开系数建模为连续时间 ODE，其线性算子对应非局部 IDE 的 Galerkin 降维，并用 Neural ODE 残差项捕捉非线性；(3) 线性高斯观测模型支持缺失和不规则观测下的卡尔曼序贯更新，空间基表示支持未测量位置的归纳预测。全局参数通过变分推断学习，整体纳入贝叶斯层次以量化不确定性。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，NLBST 展现出强预测能力和空间泛化性能，不确定性校准良好，在强非局部和部分观测场景下相较于基线方法取得显著增益。

Conclusion: NLBST 成功将非局部 IDE 的物理结构、Neural ODE 的灵活性和贝叶斯推断的不确定性量化融合于统一框架，为复杂时空预测提供了一种可解释、可外推且计算可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world spatio-temporal forecasting must handle irregular time points, spatially sparse observations, and the need for uncertainty quantification. This setting is often further compounded by nonlocal interactions (long-range spatial coupling). Modeling continuous-space, continuous-time nonlocal dynamics naturally leads to infinite-dimensional integro-differential equations (IDEs), making principled Bayesian inference intractable. We propose the NonLocal Bayesian Spatio-Temporal model (NLBST), a hierarchical Bayesian framework for continuous spatio-temporal fields that learns explicit nonlocal coupling while retaining tractable inference. NLBST represents the latent field via a coordinate-based spatial basis expansion and models the coefficient process with a continuous-time ODE whose learnable linear operator corresponds to a Galerkin reduction of a nonlocal IDE; a Neural ODE residual captures additional nonlinear dynamics. A linear-Gaussian observation model enables Kalman-style sequential updates under missing and irregular observations, while the spatial basis representation enables inductive prediction at unmeasured locations without retraining. Global parameters are learned via variational inference, and uncertainty is handled through a Bayesian hierarchy. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate strong forecasting and spatial generalization with well-calibrated uncertainty, yielding substantial gains over baselines in strongly nonlocal and partially observed regimes.

</details>


### [215] [Beyond the Training Distribution: Evaluating Predictions Under Distribution Shift and Selection Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14506)
*Annie Ulichney,Amanda Coston*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个双重机器学习方法，用于在协变量偏移和选择性标签同时存在的情况下，对黑箱预测模型进行部署前目标风险评估。研究者推导了基于影响函数的偏差校正估计量，并在eICU电子健康记录数据库上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算法辅助决策的现实场景中，预测模型部署到新环境时性能常因两个问题而退化：(1) 协变量偏移——目标人群的协变量分布与训练数据不同；(2) 选择性标签——结果的观测性依赖于历史决策。现有方法通常只单独处理其中一个问题，缺乏在二者同时存在时的有效评估手段。因此，需一种能在部署前准确估计目标风险的统一框架。

Method: 作者采用双重机器学习（Double Machine Learning）框架：首先在标准假设下证明目标风险估计量的可识别性；然后基于目标风险的影响函数推导出偏差校正估计量，利用交叉拟合和Neyman正交化来消除一阶偏差，使得即使使用任意机器学习模型估计冗余参数，也能得到目标风险的√n一致估计。

Result: 在eICU电子健康记录数据库上的实验表明，所提出的估计量在追踪真实目标风险方面，优于仅处理选择性标签或仅处理协变量偏移的单一方法，也优于结合标准插件方法的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为在协变量偏移和选择性标签共同作用下的部署前模型评估提供了有效的解决方案，能够更准确地估计任意黑箱模型在新环境中的目标风险，有助于在实际部署前识别潜在的性能退化风险。

Abstract: Understanding how a prediction model will perform in a new environment before deployment is essential to preventing harm when algorithms inform decision-making. Two common sources of model performance degradation are (i) covariate shift, where the target covariate distribution differs from the source, and (ii) selective labels, where the observability of outcomes depends on historical decisions. We study pre-deployment model evaluation under the joint presence of covariate shift and labeling of outcomes selectively based on observed features. In particular, we present a double machine learning procedure for estimating the target risk of an arbitrary black-box prediction model under a general loss function. We show identification of this estimand under standard assumptions and derive a bias-corrected estimator based on the influence function of the target risk. Finally, we evaluate our estimator through experiments using the eICU electronic health records database, showing that it tracks the true target risk more accurately than methods that address either selective labels or covariate shift alone, as well as baselines that combine standard plug-in approaches.

</details>


### [216] [Cluster LOCO: Feature Importance For Interpreting Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14592)
*Claire M. He,Genevera I. Allen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 Cluster LOCO，一种基于特征遮挡和聚类泛化能力的模型无关聚类特征重要性评分方法，通过衡量移除某特征后聚类泛化能力的下降程度来量化特征重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据集日益庞大复杂，聚类结果难以解释、审计和复现。可靠使用聚类需要理解哪些特征驱动了所发现的结构，但聚类领域的特征级解释方法远少于监督学习，且现有方法多绑定特定算法和数据假设，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出 Cluster LOCO 方法族：(1) Cluster LOCO-Split：基于数据分割，在训练集上聚类后在测试集上预测标签，通过留一协变量（移除某特征）衡量泛化能力下降；(2) Cluster LOCO-MP：基于 minipatch 集成的扩展版本，适用于大规模数据。两者均为模型无关方法。

Result: 在合成模拟实验和单细胞转录组学细胞类型发现应用中，Cluster LOCO 比现有聚类特征重要性方法更可靠地恢复出有信息的特征。

Conclusion: Cluster LOCO 为聚类分析提供了一种通用、可靠的模型无关特征重要性评估框架，填补了聚类可解释性领域的空白，特别适用于大规模复杂数据的探索性分析场景。

Abstract: Clustering is widely used for exploratory analysis and scientific discovery, driving insights from market segmentation to biological data analysis, but its outputs can be difficult to interpret, audit, and reproduce as modern datasets become increasingly large and complex. Reliable use of clustering requires understanding which features drive the discovered structure, yet feature-level explanations for clustering remain scarce compared with methods in supervised learning. Furthermore, existing clustering feature importance scores are often tied to specific algorithms and data assumptions. To address these challenges, we propose Cluster LOCO (Leave-One-Covariate-Out), a family of model-agnostic feature importance scores for clustering. Cluster LOCO is built on feature occlusion and clustering generalizability, defined as whether cluster labels learned on one subset of the data can be accurately predicted on held-out samples. For any chosen clustering algorithm, Cluster LOCO quantifies a feature's importance by measuring how much its removal degrades generalizability. We first introduce Cluster LOCO-Split, which relies on data splitting, and then extend it to Cluster LOCO-MP, a minipatch ensemble-based version designed for large-scale data. Across synthetic simulations and an application to cell-type discovery in single-cell transcriptomics, we show that Cluster LOCO more reliably recovers informative features than existing clustering feature importance methods.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [Controller-Augmented Hidden Markov Models: A Computational Framework for Constrained Sequential Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13850)
*Lekha Patel,Luis Damiano*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种控制器增强隐马尔可夫模型（CHMM），通过将各类路径约束编译为有限状态控制器来追踪最小充分历史，从而在离散和连续时间中实现精确的约束推断。理论证明了精确性、EM单调上升、线性复杂度及约束误设下的总变差界，并在三个真实场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准隐马尔可夫模型的马尔可夫假设在存在路径约束（如优先关系、访问基数、单调状态递进）时失效，因为这些约束引入的长程依赖使标准动态规划算法不再适用。

Method: 提出控制器增强隐马尔可夫模型框架，将每种约束编译为一个追踪最小充分历史的有限状态控制器，然后在增广链上运行标准前向-后向及维特比递归，实现离散与连续时间（通过均匀化）下的精确约束后验和最大后验路径推断。

Result: 理论层面：建立了约束推断精确性、约束EM单调上升、推断复杂度与控制器基数线性相关以及约束误设下的总变差界四项保证，并提供了覆盖排序、访问、路径、时序四大类共11种约束族的控制器编码目录。实验层面：在果蝇基因结构解码、CASAS智能家居活动识别、可穿戴传感器人类活动识别三个任务上对比6种替代解码器，表明控制器增强在累积约束场景下能唯一恢复全局可行轨迹，而在局部主导场景下与简单方法相当。

Conclusion: 理论与实验共同刻画了何时必须采用精确控制器增强、何时简单方法即足够的边界条件：累积约束场景需要控制器增强，局部主导场景则可用简化方法匹配其有效性。

Abstract: Hidden Markov models are foundational for sequential inference, but their Markovian assumption fails under pathwise constraints such as precedence requirements, visitation cardinalities, or monotonic state progression, which induce long-range dependencies that invalidate standard dynamic programming algorithms. To deal with this, we present Controller-Augmented Hidden Markov Models (CHMMs), a framework that compiles each constraint into a finite-state controller tracking the minimal sufficient history, after which standard forward--backward and Viterbi recursions on the augmented chain compute exact constrained posteriors and maximum a posteriori paths in both discrete and continuous time, the latter through uniformization. We establish four theoretical guarantees: exactness of constrained inference, monotone ascent of constrained EM, inference complexity linear in the controller cardinality, and a total-variation bound under constraint misspecification. A catalog of controller encodings covering 11 constraint families across the ordering, visitation, path, and temporal categories operationalizes the framework. Empirically, we evaluate CHMMs against 6 alternative decoders on 3 real-world sequence-labeling tasks of substantively different character: gene-structure decoding in \emph{Drosophila melanogaster}, free-living activity recognition in CASAS smart-home environments, and protocol-defined human activity recognition from wearable sensors. The results reveal a clean local-versus-cumulative dichotomy in which controller augmentation is uniquely able to recover globally feasible trajectories on cumulative-constraint regimes, whilst simpler decoders are matched in validity on locally-dominated regimes. Together, theory and experiment characterize when exact controller augmentation is necessary and when simpler approaches suffice.

</details>


### [218] [Constraint-based difference graph discovery in a linear setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13947)
*Daria Bystrova,Emilie Devijver*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究跨环境因果差异图推断问题，针对线性结构因果模型，提出基于回归系数等值检验的因果发现方法。核心贡献包括：引入diff-separation图形准则刻画回归系数不变性的条件，提出diff-faithfulness假设，以及设计LDiffPC算法从多环境数据中恢复差异图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较不同总体的因果关系是科学研究的核心需求，但现有因果发现方法多聚焦于单一环境。作者指出回归系数的不变性受比标准d-分离更复杂的图形条件控制，需要新的理论与算法来识别跨环境因果结构的差异。

Method: 1. 理论层面：提出diff-separation图形准则，刻画何时一个条件集能阻断所有导致跨环境回归系数差异的路径；引入diff-faithfulness假设，将图形分离陈述与回归系数等式约束连接。2. 算法层面：设计LDiffPC算法，借鉴PC算法框架，通过回归系数的等值检验（而非条件独立性检验）逐步恢复两个环境间的差异图。

Result: （摘要中未报告具体实验或理论结果的数值数据，主要呈现在方法论贡献。）

Conclusion: 本文为跨环境因果差异发现提供了完整的理论框架和实用算法。Diff-separation和diff-faithfulness构成了回归系数不变性的图形化表征基础，LDiffPC算法则实现了从多环境数据中有效恢复差异图的目标。

Abstract: Comparing causal relationships across populations is essential in many scientific domains. This paper studies the problem of inferring a difference graph between two environments and proposes a causal discovery method for linear structural causal models based on equality tests of regression coefficients. We show that invariance of regression coefficients is governed by graphical conditions that go beyond standard d-separation. Therefore, we introduce diff-separation, a graphical criterion that characterizes when a conditioning set blocks all paths capable of inducing differences in regression coefficients across environments. Building on this criterion, we introduce a corresponding diff-faithfulness assumption, linking graphical diff-separation statements to equality constraints on regression coefficients. Finally, we propose LDiffPC, a PC-style algorithm that uses equality tests of regression coefficients to recover the differences from multi-environment data.

</details>


### [219] [Scan Statistics for Nonhomogeneous Poisson Processes with Extreme-Value Calibration and Application to CNV Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13973)
*Tung-Lung Wu,Asanka R. Duwage*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扫描统计量的两样本非齐次泊松过程局部聚类检测方法，用于测序数据CNV分析；通过经验时间变换将问题标准化，证明扫描统计量收敛于含极值指数的广义极值分布，并提供参数化和置换两种校准方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代测序数据的拷贝数变异分析需要在异质性基线强度下检测局部聚类，现有连续检验方法在非均匀背景下的性能受限，需要一种能够适应复杂基线且具有严格渐近理论的检测框架。

Method: （1）利用对照样本构建经验时间变换，将病例过程变换为[0,1]上的近似均匀过程；（2）定义滑动窗口内变换点最大计数的扫描统计量；（3）证明扫描统计量收敛于GEV分布，极值指数刻画重叠窗口依赖性；（4）采用极大似然估计GEV参数，用超阈值聚类法估计极值指数；（5）同时开发置换检验作为非参数校准方案。

Result: 模拟研究表明置换校准在各设置下维持接近名义水平的I类错误，GEV校准在小窗口下准确；两种方法在异质性基线强度下均展现与连续检验方法竞争性的检验功效。

Conclusion: 所提扫描统计量框架通过渐近校准和置换校准，能够在异质性泊松强度下有效检测局部聚类，在测序数据CNV区域识别中验证了其实际有效性。

Abstract: We develop a scan statistic method for detecting local clusters in a two-sample nonhomogeneous Poisson process (NHPP) framework, motivated by copy number variation (CNV) analysis in next-generation sequencing data. The control sample is used to construct an empirical time transformation, under which the transformed case sample is approximately uniform on [0,1] under the null hypothesis. The scan statistic is defined as the maximum number of transformed points within a moving window.
  We show that the scan statistic converges to a generalized extreme value (GEV) distribution with an extremal index that captures the dependence induced by overlapping windows. The GEV parameters and extremal index are estimated using maximum likelihood and exceedance clustering methods, providing an asymptotic calibration of the test. A permutation procedure is also developed to provide a nonparametric alternative. Simulation studies show that the permutation calibration maintains empirical Type I error close to the nominal level across the considered settings, and the GEV calibration is accurate for smaller windows. Both proposed procedures show competitive power compared with the continuous testing method under heterogeneous baseline intensities. An application to sequencing data illustrates the effectiveness of the proposed approach for detecting CNV regions.

</details>


### [220] [Reliable Panel Regression: A Default Workflow for Slow-Moving, Mismeasured Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14009)
*Andrew S. Rosenberg*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 固定效应系数收缩常被解读为混杂偏误的证据，但本文指出对于缓慢变化且存在测量误差的变量，这一推论不可靠。固定效应会移除大量信号，仅从测量误差主导的组内变异中识别系数，导致估计向零衰减。本文提出了一套面板回归的默认工作流程，结合信度估计、修正估计、部分识别界限和自相关前沿面，帮助研究者区分衰减与混杂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治学者通常将加入固定效应后系数向零收缩解释为混合OLS估计存在混杂偏误。但这一推论在回归变量缓慢变化且存在测量误差时并不可靠——固定效应可能同时消除了真实信号与混杂因素，导致研究者无法区分衰减偏误与混杂偏误。本文旨在揭示这一方法论陷阱，并提供可操作的诊断与补救方案。

Method: 理论推导方面，作者将固定效应估计量的衰减因子分解为两个可识别成分的函数：测量信度（reliability）和经验组内相关系数（intraclass correlation, ICC）。实证工作流程包括：(1) 当可能时估计信度，报告混合估计和固定效应估计，并用组内信度进行修正；(2) 当两估计同号时，使用部分识别界限；(3) 当两估计异号时，将固定效应估计解释为组内估计量；(4) 对于无信度估计的变量，引入"自相关前沿面"直接界定衰减因子的边界。最后将该流程应用于多项已发表研究。

Result: 理论分析表明，衰减程度取决于变量的经验ICC和测量信度：ICC越低（变量越缓慢变化）且信度越低，固定效应下的衰减越严重。在多项已发表研究的应用中，数据往往无法区分衰减与混杂，而本文提出的工作流程能够明确揭示研究者面临的是哪种情形。

Conclusion: 研究者不应自动将固定效应下的系数收缩等同于混杂偏误的消除。本文的工作流程通过信度修正、部分识别和自相关前沿面等工具，使研究者能够更审慎地诊断面板数据中的衰减问题，从而做出更可靠的因果推断。

Abstract: Political scientists often interpret coefficient shrinkage under fixed effects as evidence that pooled associations are confounded. This paper shows why that inference is unreliable for slow-moving, mismeasured regressors. Fixed effects can remove much of the signal and identify coefficients from within-unit variation that is disproportionately measurement error, attenuating estimates toward zero. A lone fixed effects coefficient may therefore be unable to distinguish confounding from measurement error. I show that the attenuation depends on a regressor's empirical intraclass correlation and measurement reliability. I then propose a default workflow for panel regression. Researchers estimate reliability when possible, report pooled and fixed effects estimates with corrected within reliability, use partial identification bounds when the estimates share a sign, and report fixed effects as a within-unit estimate when they do not. For variables with no reliability estimate, I introduce an autocorrelation frontier that bounds the attenuation factor directly. I conclude by applying this workflow to several published results to show that the data often cannot distinguish attenuation from confounding, and the workflow makes clear which case the researcher faces.

</details>


### [221] [Real-order moments, tail representations, and logarithmic means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14019)
*Roberto Vila,Eduardo Nakano*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文建立了任意随机变量实数阶矩的统一框架，给出用累积分布函数和生存函数表示的一般积分表达式，涵盖连续、离散和混合分布，并推广到正、分数及负矩。对离散分布导出基于累积概率的级数表示，应用于zeta和Skellam分布，并得到对数矩表示，连接了对数均值、Laplace变换与Frullani恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典矩的尾部积分恒等式仅适用于非负随机变量，缺乏对定义在全实线上任意分布（连续、离散、混合）的实数阶矩（包括正、分数、负矩）的统一处理框架。

Method: 利用累积分布函数（CDF）和生存函数（SF）构建统一积分表示；对离散分布导出基于累积概率的显式级数表示；通过尾部行为分析矩的存在性判据；将对数矩表示与Laplace变换及经典Frullani恒等式建立联系。

Result: 建立了适用于全实线任意随机变量实数阶矩的统一积分公式；得到离散分布的级数表示及矩存在性的简便判据；成功应用于zeta分布和Skellam分布；导出了对数矩的表示，建立了对数均值、Laplace变换与Frullani恒等式之间的关联。

Conclusion: 该统一框架将经典非负随机变量的尾部积分公式推广到任意分布和任意实数阶矩，揭示了尾部概率、分布函数、Laplace变换与矩存在性之间的深刻联系，为矩表示理论提供了系统化的视角。

Abstract: This paper develops a unified framework for the study of real-order moments of arbitrary random variables. General integral representations are established in terms of cumulative distribution functions and survival functions, covering continuous, discrete, and mixed distributions supported on the whole real line. These formulas extend the classical tail-integral identities for nonnegative random variables and provide a common treatment of positive, fractional, and negative moments.
  For discrete distributions, explicit series representations are derived in terms of cumulative probabilities, yielding simple criteria for the existence of moments. Applications are presented for the zeta and Skellam distributions, illustrating how tail behavior determines moment finiteness and how moments can be represented geometrically through cumulative distribution functions. In addition, a representation for logarithmic moments is obtained, linking logarithmic means, Laplace transforms, and the classical Frullani identity.
  The results provide a unified perspective on moment representations and establish useful connections between tail probabilities, distribution functions, Laplace transforms, and moment existence.

</details>


### [222] [Bias-corrected empirical likelihood-based inference for the tail index under heavy-tailed models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14085)
*Haodi Liang,Natalia Nolde*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合偏差校正与经验似然估计的尾指数估计方法，建立了其渐近理论，并通过模拟和实例验证了其有限样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尾指数在刻画重尾分布尾部衰减特征及极值分析外推中至关重要，但现有估计方法在偏差和效率方面存在不足，需要一种更具吸引力的替代方案。

Method: 将偏差校正技术与经验似然估计方法相结合，构建新的尾指数估计量，并推导其渐近分布理论。

Result: 所提出的估计量在渐近性质上具备良好表现，有限样本模拟研究表明其性能优于部分现有估计量，实际数据应用也验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 偏差校正与经验似然相结合的策略为尾指数估计提供了有竞争力的新方法，兼具理论保障与实用价值。

Abstract: The tail index parameter of heavy-tailed probability models plays a key role in characterizing the tail decay of the underlying distribution function and is often involved in extrapolation procedures for various extreme value analysis questions. In this paper we revisit the question of tail index estimation and combine the ideas of bias-correction and empirical likelihood estimation to propose an estimator that offers an attractive alternative to some of the existing estimators. We develop an asymptotic theory for the proposed estimator and conduct simulation studies to demonstrate its performance in finite sample situations. The method is also applied to a data example for illustration.

</details>


### [223] [Cauchy Aggregation of Ridge-Regularized Hotelling Tests for High-Dimensional Change-Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14092)
*Ping Zhao,Le Zhou,Long Feng*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对RHT变点检验中岭参数选择困难的问题，本文提出在有限确定性网格上计算固定岭p值，并用Cauchy组合规则进行聚合，从而避免选择单一岭参数。论文建立了岭过程的有限网格联合弱收敛，证明了联合极限校准下的固定水平有效性和解析Cauchy p值的小尾有效性。蒙特卡洛实验表明该方法具有稳健的size表现，且功效接近最优固定岭选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RHT变点检验依赖于岭参数λ，但功效最优的λ取决于未知的协方差结构和未知的均值偏移，实践中难以选定单一最优值。现有方法需要选择单个岭参数，存在主观性和非鲁棒性问题，因此需要一种无需选择岭参数且能保持高功效的聚合策略。

Method: 在标准随机矩阵条件下，对一个有限确定性网格上的固定岭值分别计算p值，然后采用Cauchy组合规则（Cauchy combination rule）将这些p值聚合为一个综合检验统计量。作者进一步建立了固定岭RHT统计量在有限网格上的联合弱收敛理论，从而保证联合极限校准下的水平控制和Cauchy聚合p值的渐近有效性。

Result: 理论上证明了有限网格联合弱收敛，得到了联合极限校准下的固定水平有效性和解析Cauchy p值的小尾有效性。蒙特卡洛模拟显示，确定性网格Cauchy聚合在不同协方差结构和信号配置下均具有稳定的size行为，功效接近该范围内最稳定且最优的固定岭选择。

Conclusion: 确定性网格Cauchy聚合方法成功规避了RHT检验中岭参数的选择难题，在保持水平控制的同时实现了接近最优的功效表现，为高维变点检测提供了一种实用且理论可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Ridge-regularized Hotelling-type (RHT) change-point tests depend on a ridge parameter $λ$, but the power-optimal value is determined by the unknown covariance structure and the unknown mean shift. We avoid selecting a single ridge value by computing fixed-ridge p-values on a finite deterministic grid and aggregating them with the Cauchy combination rule. Under the standard random-matrix conditions for fixed-ridge RHT statistics, we establish finite-grid joint weak convergence of the ridge processes. This leads to fixed-level validity under joint-limit calibration and small-tail validity for the analytic Cauchy p-value. Monte Carlo experiments show that deterministic-grid Cauchy aggregation has stable size behavior and achieves power close to the best stable fixed ridge choice across a range of covariance and signal configurations.

</details>


### [224] [A Two-Stage Statistical Framework for Evaluating Associative Interference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14117)
*Achraf Cohen,Andrew Kincaid*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文通过改编内隐联想测验（IAT）并引入两阶段建模方法，分离了LLM的响应合规性与任务一致性，发现不同模型在各领域的联想干扰效应差异显著，表明IAT式联想不对称并非LLM的普遍属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前使用人类心理学范式评估LLM偏见时存在方法论局限，尤其是将"拒绝响应"与"任务表现"混为一谈，导致对模型偏见难以清晰解读。因此需要一种能将响应合规性与任务表现分离的评估框架。

Method: 1. 将内隐联想测验（IAT）改编为受控的强制选择框架；2. 提出两阶段建模方法，将响应合规性（是否按格式作答）与任务一致性（分类判断是否符合IAT预期）分离；3. 在三款当代LLM（Claude Sonnet-4、Gemini 2.5 Pro、GPT-5）上评估联想干扰效应（即不一致条件相比一致条件下任务一致性下降的程度），采用贝叶斯方法估计DeltaP及其95%可信区间。

Result: 三款模型的响应合规性均较高，但干扰效应差异显著：Claude Sonnet-4在性别-职业领域表现出强干扰（DeltaP=0.086，95% CrI [0.026, 0.173]），在性别-科学领域也有较小但可信的效应；Gemini 2.5 Pro干扰效应减弱；GPT-5在各领域几乎未检测到干扰效应。

Conclusion: IAT式联想不对称并非LLM的普遍属性，而是取决于模型自身特性。通过将干扰效应与合规性分离并建模项目级变异，该研究为评估LLM的结构化响应模式提供了原则性框架，并表明联想干扰在现代系统中可被大幅缓解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated for bias using adaptations of human psychological paradigms, yet methodological limitations-particularly the conflation of refusal behavior with task performance-have hindered clear interpretation. Here, we adapt the Implicit Association Test (IAT) to a controlled, forced-choice framework and introduce a two-stage modeling approach that separates response compliance from task-consistent classification. Across three contemporary LLMs (Claude Sonnet-4, Gemini 2.5 Pro, and GPT-5), we evaluate associative interference, defined as reduced task-consistency in incongruent relative to congruent conditions. While compliance with the structured response format was uniformly high, interference effects varied substantially across models and domains. Claude Sonnet-4 exhibited strong interference in the Gender--Career domain (DeltaP = 0.086, 95% CrI [0.026, 0.173]) and smaller but credible effects in Gender--Science. Gemini 2.5 Pro showed attenuated interference, and GPT-5 exhibited minimal or no detectable interference across domains. These findings demonstrate that IAT-style associative asymmetries are not a universal property of LLMs, but instead depend on model-specific characteristics. By isolating interference from compliance and modeling item-level variability, this study provides a principled framework for evaluating structured response patterns in LLMs. The results highlight the importance of model-specific assessment and suggest that associative interference can be substantially mitigated in modern systems.

</details>


### [225] [G-computation for causal effect estimation from observational hierarchical data with unmeasured cluster context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14131)
*Shafayet Khan Shafee,Bishal Sarker,Md. Niamul Islam Sium*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对层次化观测数据的组内g-computation方法，通过在聚类内部进行因果估计来减少未测量簇级混杂和处理效应异质性带来的偏倚，模拟和实证均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化观测数据中，未测量的簇级因素可能同时混杂处理-结果关系，并导致跨簇的处理效应异质性，传统方法难以同时应对这两种挑战，需要新的因果估计策略。

Method: 提出组内g-computation框架：先按观测到的处理率对簇进行分组，然后在各组内分别进行g-computation（结合随机效应模型作为结果模型），最后聚合各组估计量。对比了标准g-computation与组内g-computation在线性模型和随机效应模型下的表现。

Result: 模拟显示：当未测量簇级变量仅为混杂因素时，基于随机效应模型的标准和组内g-computation均能大幅降低偏倚；当该变量同时为混杂和效应修饰因子时，组内随机效应估计量RMSE最低。实证应用（2019年孟加拉国MICS数据）估计青少年怀孕对儿童身高年龄别Z评分的因果效应为-0.12（95% bootstrap CI: [-0.18, -0.06]）。

Conclusion: 组内g-computation为层次化观测研究中未测量簇级混杂和效应异质性提供了一种有效的偏倚减少策略。

Abstract: Observational studies frequently involve hierarchical data structures in which individuals are nested within higher-level units. In such settings, unmeasured cluster-level factors may confound the treatment-outcome relationship and may additionally induce treatment effect heterogeneity across clusters, complicating causal effect estimation. We formalize the use of g-computation for hierarchical observational data by incorporating random-effects models (REM) as outcome models and propose a within-group g-computation strategy designed to reduce bias arising from unmeasured cluster context. The approach groups clusters according to their observed treatment prevalence and performs g-computation within groups before aggregating group-specific estimates. Through extensive Monte Carlo simulations, we compare the standard and within-group implementations of g-computation using both linear models and REM. Results show that both standard and within-group REM-based implementations substantially reduce bias when the unmeasured cluster-level variable acts solely as a confounder, whereas the proposed within-group REM estimator achieves the lowest RMSE when the unmeasured cluster-level factor acts as both a confounder and a source of treatment effect heterogeneity. We apply the proposed within-group REM estimator to estimate the causal effect of adolescent pregnancy on the child height-for-age Z-score using 2019 Bangladesh MICS data, obtaining an estimated effect of -0.12 (95% bootstrap CI: [-0.18, -0.06]). The proposed within-group g-computation framework offers a strategy for reducing bias from unmeasured cluster-level confounding and treatment effect heterogeneity in hierarchical observational studies.

</details>


### [226] [HSCI: Neyman-Orthogonal Causal Inference under High-Dimensional Proportional Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14132)
*Yingying Fan,Lan Gao,Daoji Li,Jinchi Lv*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出高维生存因果推断(HSCI)框架，在高维Cox模型和Logistic倾向得分模型下，通过Neyman近正交得分和交叉拟合实现治疗效应的有效推断，具有双稳健性和根n渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生存分析中，当治疗分配和结局受大量基线协变量混杂时，如何进行有效的治疗效应推断是一个基础性难题。现有方法在高维稀疏设定下存在冗余参数估计偏差，导致推断失效。

Method: 提出HSCI框架：(1)采用稀疏高维Cox比例风险结局模型和高维Logistic倾向得分工作模型；(2)为消除冗余参数估计偏差，构建Neyman近正交得分；(3)采用交叉拟合(cross-fitting)策略实现该得分；(4)在双稳健冗余率条件下，证明估计量的根n渐近正态性和方差一致估计；(5)扩展框架至高维生存协变量效应的推断。

Result: 模拟实验表明，HSCI相比正则化Cox估计量显著降低偏差，在不同维度、删失率和倾向得分模型错误设定下均保持有效的置信区间覆盖。在弥漫性大B细胞淋巴瘤数据上的应用进一步验证了其在高维生物医学生存研究中的价值。

Conclusion: HSCI框架为高维生存数据中的因果推断提供了有效的解决方案，兼具双稳健性、渐近有效性和广泛的适用性，对高维生物医学生存研究具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Valid treatment effect inference in survival studies is fundamental yet challenging when the treatment assignments and outcomes are confounded by many baseline covariates. To this end, in this paper we propose a high-dimensional survival causal inference (HSCI) framework that delivers valid inference under a sparse high-dimensional Cox proportional hazards outcome model and a high-dimensional logistic propensity score working model. To mitigate the nuisance estimation bias, we develop a Neyman near-orthogonal score for the treatment effect and implement it with cross-fitting. Under doubly robust nuisance-rate conditions, we establish the root-n asymptotic normality and consistent variance estimation. We also extend the framework to inference on high-dimensional survival covariate effects. Simulation examples confirm that HSCI reduces sharply the bias relative to the regularized Cox estimators and maintains valid confidence interval coverage across different dimensionality, censoring, and misspecified propensity-model settings. An application to diffuse large-B-cell lymphoma data further showcases its value for high-dimensional biomedical survival studies.

</details>


### [227] [Predictive Concordance for Parameter Optimisation and Mixture Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14382)
*Tobias Adrian,Domenico Giannone,Matteo Luciani,Mike West*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于期望误分类率（EMR）的概率分布一致性度量，重点分析如何通过最大化EMR来优化参数化分布族与参考分布之间的一致性，并将其应用于混合合成和宏观经济情景预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望提供一种具有实际决策理论意义的分布一致性度量方法。传统的度量如KL散度在某些应用场景中缺乏直接的决策解释性，而EMR作为一种有界效用函数下的贝叶斯决策分析工具，能够弥补这一不足，尤其在宏观经济情景预测等政策应用中具有明确的实际意义。

Method: 本文以期望误分类率（EMR）为核心度量指标，研究其在参数化分布族中优化一致性的理论性质。方法上：(1) 建立EMR与KL散度等其他度量的关系；(2) 引入正则化变体以约束优化过程；(3) 将EMR优化解释为合成贝叶斯仿真（synthetic Bayesian emulation）；(4) 在混合合成框架下，将参数化族设定为给定分布的离散混合，并通过直接蒙特卡洛模拟实现数值优化。

Result: 研究揭示了EMR的理论性质及其与KL散度等传统度量的联系；证明了EMR优化等价于具有有界效用函数的贝叶斯决策分析；正则化惩罚项可通过合成贝叶斯仿真框架得到清晰解释；所提方法在宏观经济情景预测的混合合成应用中表现出良好的可操作性和数值效率。

Conclusion: 期望误分类率是一种兼具理论深度和实际决策意义的分布一致性度量。其优化过程可自然地置于贝叶斯决策分析框架下理解，且通过蒙特卡洛模拟易于实现。该方法在宏观经济政策情景预测等应用领域具有重要价值。

Abstract: We discuss probabilistic measures of concordance between two probability distributions based on the expected misclassification rate (EMR). The focus is on comparing a given reference distribution with other distributions in a parametrised class, and optimising concordance by identifying parameter values maximising EMR or a regularised variant. EMR is a practical and decision-theoretically meaningful measure, and its optimisation has direct interpretation as a Bayesian decision analysis with a bounded utility function. We explore theoretical properties of EMR, discuss relationships with other measures including Küllback-Leibler divergence, and recognise that its optimisation has a synthetic Bayesian emulation interpretation that aids understanding and specification of regularisation penalties. A main area of methodology is in mixture synthesis where the parametrised family is a discrete mixture of given distributions. A detailed example comes from scenario forecasting in macroeconomic policy settings, a key applied area motivating the new methodology. Theoretical developments underlie efficient numerical optimisation and analysis is easily implemented using direct Monte Carlo simulation.

</details>


### [228] [Joint Nuclear and $\ell_1$ Regularization for Logistic Matrix Regression with Applications to Brain Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14436)
*Damian Brzyski,Aaron Cohen,Zijian Wang,Mario Dzemidzic,David A. Kareken,Jaroslaw Harezlak*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种新的逻辑矩阵回归凸优化框架，联合使用核范数和ℓ₁范数惩罚，实现系数矩阵的低秩与稀疏结构估计，并通过ADMM算法求解，应用于脑连接矩阵以识别酒精使用障碍家族史特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维矩阵值预测变量（如脑连接矩阵）在二元响应下的回归建模需要同时具备可解释性和统计效率，但现有方法难以同时捕捉低秩和稀疏结构。

Method: 提出logistic scalar-on-matrix回归的凸优化框架，联合施加核范数（低秩）和ℓ₁范数（稀疏）惩罚；设计基于ADMM的自定义算法高效求解；推导解的理论性质。

Result: 数值实验表明方法能有效恢复有意义的预测模式；在脑成像数据中成功识别出与酒精使用障碍家族史相关的功能性脑连接矩阵结构特征。

Conclusion: 联合低秩与稀疏约束的逻辑矩阵回归框架兼具预测能力和可解释性，适用于高维矩阵预测变量的二元分类问题，在神经影像等应用领域具有推广价值。

Abstract: We introduce a new convex optimization framework for logistic scalar-on-matrix regression which incorporates nuclear and $\ell_1$ norm penalties to enforce simultaneously low-rank and sparse structures in the estimated coefficient matrix. The proposed method enables interpretable modeling of high-dimensional matrix-valued predictors in the presence of binary responses. We derive a custom algorithm based on the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) to efficiently solve the resulting convex optimization problem and establish the theoretical properties of the obtained solution. Numerical experiments clearly demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in recovering meaningful predictive patterns. Finally, we apply our method to the brain imaging data to identify structures in functional brain connectivity matrices that are characteristic of subjects with a family history of alcohol use disorders (AUDs).

</details>


### [229] [On the design distribution for predictive Bayesian regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14544)
*Wanyue Sun,Edwin Fong*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了回归问题中预测性贝叶斯推断的预测设计分布的作用，提出了预测可识别性和设计不变性的概念，并构建了一类适用于高维回归的弱形式参数鞅后验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性贝叶斯推断中预测设计分布的选择对推断有效性的影响缺乏正式研究，尤其在高维回归场景下，需要明确其理论性质以确保推断的合理性。

Method: 通过分析参数鞅后验在随机设计回归中的理论性质，定义预测可识别性和设计不变性两个核心概念，并在此基础上构建一类通过正则化自然适应高维场景的新型参数鞅后验。

Result: 理论分析揭示了预测设计分布选择对推断有效性的关键影响；所提出的新型参数鞅后验满足弱形式的预测可识别性和设计不变性要求；通过模拟实验验证了方法的性能。

Conclusion: 预测设计分布的选择对回归推断具有根本性影响；所提出的弱形式参数鞅后验为高维回归提供了理论上合理且实践可行的预测性贝叶斯推断框架。

Abstract: The predictive approach to Bayesian inference accesses the posterior distribution via a sequence of one-step-ahead predictives, enabling inference via predictive resampling without Markov chain Monte Carlo. In the random-design regression setting, an explicit specification of the predictive design distribution is required, yet the impact of this choice has received little formal attention. We study the role of this predictive design distribution in parametric martingale posteriors for regression, and identify predictive notions of identifiability and design invariance that are essential for valid inference, particularly in the high-dimensional regression setting. Building on these foundations, we introduce a novel class of parametric martingale posteriors for regression that satisfies a weak form of these desiderata, and naturally accommodates the high-dimensional setting through regularization. We then illustrate our method through a simulation.

</details>


### [230] [Typical Healthcare Pathways as a Basis for Admixture Modeling of Patient Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14587)
*Maryam Farhadizadeh,Carola S. Heinzel,August Sigle,Harald Binder,Frederik Wenz,Jan Hasenauer,Peter Pfaffelhuber,Nadine Binder*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架，将队列级典型医疗路径识别与患者级推断分离：先通过规则算法构建紧凑的路径图并建模为马尔可夫链，再以混合隶属度模型将每位患者表示为典型路径的概率混合，从而实现稳定、可解释的治疗路径总结与患者亚组刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规医疗轨迹因诊断流程、治疗顺序、事件时间和记录方式的差异而难以直接比较，但队列层面存在重复出现的模式。现有方法难以同时兼顾队列级路径的全局可解释性和患者级轨迹的灵活表征，需要一个能系统分离这两个层次的分析框架。

Method: 框架分为两层：(1) 队列层：采用基于规则的算法从真实世界数据中提取典型医疗路径，构建紧凑的路径图，并将其建模为马尔可夫链；(2) 患者层：使用混合隶属度模型（admixture model），以典型路径作为结构化组件，将每位患者的轨迹表示为多个典型路径的概率混合，而非硬性分配到单一路径，从而获得紧凑的混合权重向量用于亚组分析。此外通过多次训练-测试分割评估路径结构和混合表征的稳定性。

Result: 在多次训练-测试分割中，框架展示出稳定的路径结构和患者混合模式。在前列腺癌根治术患者的常规诊疗数据上，框架识别出可解释的诊疗模式，并有效支持具有相似临床事件模式的患者亚组识别。

Conclusion: 该框架为总结真实世界治疗路径和刻画患者亚组提供了一种可解释且稳定的方法，有助于理解临床实践、支持质量改进。

Abstract: Background: Understanding whether patients follow similar or distinct patterns of care is important for characterizing clinical practice, identifying patient subgroups, and supporting quality improvement. However, routine healthcare trajectories are difficult to compare directly because patients may differ in their diagnostic workup, treatment sequencing, timing of clinical events, and documentation practices. Despite this variation, trajectories often contain recurring patterns at the cohort level. Methods: To address this challenge, we present a framework that explicitly separates cohort-level typical pathway identification from patient-level inference. At the cohort level, we derive an interpretable representation of care processes using a rule-based algorithm to identify typical healthcare pathways, resulting in a compact pathway graph. These pathways are then modeled as Markov chains and used as structured components in an admixture model, allowing each patient to be represented as a probabilistic mixture of typical pathways rather than being assigned to a single pathway component. The resulting admixture weights provide a compact representation of patient trajectories for subgroup characterization. We further assess the stability of the identified pathways and inferred admixture representations across multiple train-test splits. Results: Across train-test splits, the framework demonstrated consistent pathway structures and patient-level mixture patterns. Applied to routine care data from prostate cancer patients undergoing radical prostatectomy, the framework identified interpretable care patterns and supported the identification of patient subgroups with similar clinical event patterns. Conclusion: Overall, the proposed framework provides an interpretable and stable approach for summarizing treatment pathways and characterizing patient subgroups in real-world practice.

</details>


### [231] [Testing Preferential Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14615)
*Isabel Natario,Andreia Monteiro*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单且易于实施的检验方法，用于检测地统计学中的优先采样问题。该方法基于采样点数量与对应测量值之间的依赖关系，通过大规模模拟研究和两个真实数据集验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在地统计学中，当空间过程与采样过程存在随机依赖时，即发生优先采样（preferential sampling）。忽略这一问题会导致估计有偏且不准确，但识别它往往较为复杂。现有方法存在实施和理解上的困难，因此亟需一种简单易行的检验方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于采样点数量与对应测量值之间依赖关系的检验方法。通过大规模模拟研究评估其性能，模拟中考虑了不同程度的优先性、与协变量的关系、不同样本量以及不同检验程序条件。并在两个真实数据集——苔藓铅浓度采样数据和红蓝虾捕捞数据上进行了应用验证。

Result: 模拟研究结果显示，该方法在不同条件下均能取得较高的优先采样正确检测率。在苔藓铅浓度和虾类捕捞数据这两个已知真实数据集上的应用，进一步证实了该检验方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的检验方法简单、易于实现，能够有效检测优先采样问题，具有良好的统计性能，有助于地统计建模中避免因忽略优先采样而导致的偏差估计。

Abstract: Geostatistics aims to infer a spatially continuous phenomenon from observations collected at a finite number of locations, frequently measured with error. Whenever there is stochastic dependence between the spatial and sampling processes, preferential sampling occurs. Ignoring this problem drives to incorrect and biased estimates and, therefore, recognizing it is quite important, but not always simple to execute and understand. In this work, a test for assessing preferential sampling, simple and easy to implement, is presented, overcoming the previous concerns. It is based on the dependence between the number of sampled points and the values of the corresponding measures. The performance of the proposed test id assessed through a large simulation study, which consideres different levels of preferentiability, relation with a covariate, different sample sizes and different test procedure conditions. The results are quite encouraging, with high levels of correct preferential sampling detections, further confirmed by the test application to already known real data sets of lead concentrations in moss samples and red and blue shrimp capture data.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [A Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL)-Based Transformer Method for Solving the Open Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13682)
*Faezeh Ardali,Mwembezi A. Nyelele,Gerald M. Knapp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer编码器-解码器架构的开放车间调度问题（OSSP）求解策略，仅使用加工时间矩阵作为输入，在小规模Taillard基准实例上训练后，无需重新训练即可泛化至40×40到100×100的大规模随机实例，相比传统调度规则展现了良好的竞争力和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放车间调度问题在工业和服务业中广泛存在，但随着作业数和机器数增加，精确方法迅速变得难以计算，传统调度规则和元启发式方法在大规模场景下需要大量调优才能维持解质量。因此，需要一种特征依赖少、无需手动调优且能跨规模泛化的学习型调度策略。

Method: 采用Transformer编码器-解码器架构结合多头注意力机制构建调度策略。模型仅以加工时间矩阵作为输入，在Taillard基准实例（4×4、5×5、7×7、10×10）上进行训练，直接生成可行调度方案。为评估可扩展性，将训练好的策略直接应用于40×40至100×100的随机生成实例，并与SPT、LPT、MWKR、EST等经典调度启发式规则进行对比。

Result: 在训练规模内，模型生成的调度方案makespan与已知最优值的偏差通常在15-30%以内。在大规模实例上，Transformer相对于标准下界的平均偏差为12.89-15.12%。与经典规则对比，Transformer与EST保持竞争力（差距适中），且显著优于SPT和LPT。

Conclusion: 在小规模OSSP实例上训练的Transformer策略能够泛化到远大于训练规模的实例上，为经典调度规则提供了一种特征轻量、基于学习的替代方案，证明了深度学习模型在组合优化问题跨规模泛化方面的潜力。

Abstract: The open shop scheduling problem (OSSP) arises in many industrial and service settings but remains computationally challenging as the number of jobs and machines increases. While exact methods quickly become intractable, classical dispatching rules and metaheuristics may require substantial tuning to maintain solution quality at large scales. This study develops a Transformer-based scheduling policy for OSSP using an encoder-decoder architecture with multi-head attention. The model is trained on Taillard benchmark instances (4x4, 5x5, 7x7, and 10x10) using only the processing-time matrix as input and produces feasible schedules with makespans typically within 15-30% of best-known values. To evaluate scalability, the trained policy is applied without retraining to randomly generated instances from 40x40 to 100x100 and compared against classical dispatching heuristics, including SPT, LPT, MWKR, and EST. Across these large instances, the Transformer achieved average gaps of 12.89-15.12% relative to a standard lower bound. Compared with EST, the Transformer remained competitive, typically within a modest margin, while substantially outperforming SPT and LPT. These results indicate that a Transformer policy trained on small OSSP instances can generalize to substantially larger problems and provide a feature-light, learning-based alternative to classical dispatching rules.

</details>


### [233] [UP-NRPA: User Portrait based Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation for Planning with Large Language Models in Goal-oriented Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13683)
*Hui Wang,Fafa Zhang,Meng Liu,Xiangyu Chen,Chaoxu Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UP-NRPA框架，利用大语言模型和用户画像实现无需离线训练的在线对话策略自适应，在多个对话任务中达到100%成功率，谈判任务中销售比提升56.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话策略规划方法难以动态适应多样化的用户特征，传统方法依赖离线强化学习针对用户群体训练策略模型，缺乏实时个性化适应能力。

Method: 提出基于用户画像的嵌套 rollout 策略自适应（UP-NRPA）在线框架，利用大语言模型结合实时用户反馈，以及从当前用户画像中映射出的个性、偏好和目标，实现对话策略的动态定制，无需离线强化学习即可适应用户特征。

Result: 在协作与非协作对话基准测试中，UP-NRPA在多项对话任务上达到100%成功率；在谈判任务中，销售比（SL）提升56.41%。

Conclusion: UP-NRPA能够在不依赖训练机制的情况下适应多样化用户需求，使对话系统有效适配用户特征，验证了基于用户画像和大语言模型的在线自适应框架的显著优势。

Abstract: To address the challenge that current dialogue policy planning methods struggle to dynamically adapt to diverse user characteristics, this paper proposes a User Portrait based Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation (UP-NRPA) online framework with Large Language Models. In contrast to conventional approaches dependent on model training and require offline reinforcement learning policy models for user groups, UP-NRPA enables dynamic customization of dialogue strategies through an adaptive mechanism. This is achieved by leveraging real-time user feedback alongside personality, preferences, and objectives mapped from the current user portrait, thereby adapting to user characteristics without offline reinforcement learning. In collaborative and non-collaborative dialogue benchmarks, UP-NRPA demonstrated considerable benefits, achieving an impressive 100% success rate in multiple dialogue tasks. Particularly in negotiation tasks, the sale-to-list ratio (SL) increased by 56.41%. This demonstrates that UP-NRPA can adapt to diverse user needs without requiring a training mechanism, enabling the dialogue system to adapt to user characteristics.

</details>


### [234] [History of the Muddy Children Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13703)
*Hans van Ditmarsch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文追溯了"泥孩子谜题"在过去两个世纪中通过逻辑学和文学出版物的起源与演变历程，并介绍了一个涉及自指的新型帽子谜题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泥孩子谜题（Muddy Children Puzzle）对认知逻辑的发展具有重要启发意义，但其确切起源并不清楚。作者试图通过梳理两个世纪以来的逻辑学和文学出版物，追溯该谜题的源头，并展现其后续的变体发展。

Method: 采用历史文献追溯法，系统检索和梳理过去两百年间逻辑学与文学出版物中与泥孩子谜题相关的文本，分析其演变脉络；同时在变体研究的基础上，提出一个全新的涉及自指（self-reference）的帽子谜题。

Result: 实现了对泥孩子谜题起源的系统性追溯，厘清了该谜题在逻辑学和文学中的传播路径；梳理了该谜题启发的多种变体（如涉及数字或彩色帽子的版本）；提出了一个原创的、包含自指要素的帽子谜题。

Conclusion: 泥孩子谜题虽起源不明，但通过历史追溯可揭示其跨学科的演化轨迹；该谜题及其变体持续推动着认知逻辑的发展，而引入自指概念的新谜题有望为该领域带来新的研究视角。

Abstract: The Muddy Children Puzzle is a puzzle about knowledge and ignorance that has been inspiring for the development of epistemic logic. Who came up with it first? This is unclear. We trace the origin of the Muddy Children Puzzle through logical and literary publications over the past two centuries. The puzzle inspired a numerous variations such as involving numbers or coloured hats. We also present a novel hats puzzle involving self-reference.

</details>


### [235] [Orchestra-o1: Omnimodal Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13707)
*Fan Zhang,Vireo Zhang,Shengju Qian,Haoxuan Li,Hao Wu,Jinyang Wu,Donghao Zhou,Zhihong Zhu,Zheng Lian,Xin Wang,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Orchestra-o1 是一个全模态智能体编排框架，通过模态感知的任务分解、在线子智能体特化和并行子任务执行，实现跨文本、图像、音频、视频等多种模态的高效协作。其 DA-GRPO 训练方法在 OmniGAIA 基准上以 10.3% 的优势超越次优方案，Orchestra-o1-8B 在所有开源全模态智能体中达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 多智能体编排框架仅限于处理单一或少量模态，难以泛化到文本、图像、音频、视频等异构模态共存的全模态复杂场景，亟需一种支持多模态统一理解与协调的编排机制。

Method: (1) 统一编排机制：模态感知的任务分解，将全模态任务拆分为子任务；(2) 在线子智能体特化：动态为各子任务分配合适的模态专长智能体；(3) 并行子任务执行：提升效率的可扩展设计；(4) DA-GRPO：决策对齐的组相对策略优化，用于高效训练 Orchestra-o1-8B 的智能体强化学习方法。

Result: 在 OmniGAIA 基准上，Orchestra-o1 以 10.3% 的准确率优势超越次优方案；Orchestra-o1-8B 在所有现有开源全模态智能体中取得 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: Orchestra-o1 通过模态感知编排和 DA-GRPO 训练，有效解决了全模态多智能体协作问题，展现出在全模态复杂现实任务中的强大潜力与领先性能。

Abstract: The recent success of agent swarms has shifted the paradigm of large language model (LLM)-based agents from single-agent workflows to multi-agent systems, highlighting the importance of agent orchestration for task decomposition and collaboration. However, existing orchestration frameworks are limited to a narrow set of modalities and struggle to generalize to more complex settings where heterogeneous modalities coexist and interact. This limitation becomes particularly pronounced in omnimodal scenarios, where tasks require the unified understanding and coordination of diverse inputs such as text, image, audio, and video. In this work, we propose Orchestra-o1, an omnimodal agent orchestration framework designed to support efficient agent collaboration across multiple modalities. Orchestra-o1 introduces a unified orchestration mechanism that enables modality-aware task decomposition, online sub-agent specialization, and parallel sub-task execution. This scalable design allows agent systems to effectively tackle complex real-world tasks involving heterogeneous information sources, surpassing the second-best approach by 10.3% accuracy on the OmniGAIA benchmark. Furthermore, we introduce decision-aligned group relative policy optimization (DA-GRPO), an efficient agentic reinforcement learning approach for training Orchestra-o1-8B, which also achieves state-of-the-art performance against all existing open-source omnimodal agents.

</details>


### [236] [Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution Makes Better Deep Researcher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13710)
*Hongming Piao,Chi Liu,Mengzhuo Chen,Yan Shu,Derek Li,Ying Wei,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HOTE框架，通过混合模式强化学习实现提议者、求解者和评判者三模块的协同进化，使8B模型在深度研究基准上超越最强静态开源模型及SOTA训练方法，且时间开销更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究（静态参数能力受限）与智能体进化（仅在有标准答案的可验证任务上有效）之间存在鸿沟，亟需打通两者，实现开放任务中自主进化的智能体。

Method: 提出HOTE（Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution）框架，采用混合模式强化学习，基于网络规模知识，驱动proposer、solver、judge三个模块协同进化。

Result: 在三个长篇深度研究基准上，HOTE训练的8B模型超越了最强静态开源8-32B模型以及现有SOTA深度研究训练方法，且时间开销更低；消融实验验证了三模块协同进化的必要性。

Conclusion: HOTE框架成功弥合了深度研究与智能体进化之间的鸿沟，为实现开放任务中自主进化智能体提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Deep research and agent evolution serve as de-facto tasks for AI agents in real-world applications toward artificial general intelligence. The former enables autonomous retrieval and integration of information in open-ended environments to tackle open-ended research tasks, yet it is constrained by the static parametric deep research capabilities of agent systems. The latter allows agents to autonomously interact with the environment to gain experiences that evolve model capabilities. However, its effectiveness has been widely validated only on verifiable tasks with standard answers, leaving a gap with open-ended research tasks. To bridge these two critical tasks, we propose the Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution (HOTE) framework, which leverages hybrid-mode reinforcement learning to facilitate the collaborative evolution of a proposer, solver and judge based on web-scale knowledge, moving toward autonomous evolving agents in open-ended tasks and environments. Extensive experiments on three long-form deep research benchmarks demonstrate that the 8B model trained via HOTE surpasses the strongest static open 8-32B models as well as those trained by state-of-the-art deep research training methods with less time overhead, and further verify that the evolution of all three modules in HOTE is indispensable.

</details>


### [237] [WorkBench Revisited: Workplace Agents Two Years On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13715)
*Olly Styles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了WorkBench基准测试从2024年到2026年的演进，最佳智能体从GPT-4（43%任务完成率，26%有害行为率）进步到Claude Opus 4.8（89%完成率，2.5%有害行为率）。研究发现能力与安全性呈正相关而非权衡关系，部分错误类型已被消除但基础性错误仍偶发并可能造成不可逆伤害，同时开源模型大幅降低了达到此前闭源专属性能水平的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 追踪并量化前沿AI智能体在WorkBench基准上两年间的性能与安全性进展，揭示能力与安全之间的关系，并分析开源模型对成本结构的冲击。

Method: 研究者于2026年6月重新运行WorkBench基准测试，使用更新后的数据集和代码，对最新的前沿模型（包括Claude Opus 4.8等）进行评分，并与2024年3月的基线结果（GPT-4等）进行纵向对比分析，涵盖任务完成率、意外有害行为发生率、错误类型分类及成本维度。

Result: Claude Opus 4.8在任务完成率上达到89%（较GPT-4的43%大幅提升），意外有害行为率降至2.5%（此前为26%）。能力与安全性呈正相关：完成任务最多的模型造成的意外损害也最少。多类错误已被根除，但发送错误邮件等基础性错误仍存。开源模型在同等性能水平上显著降低了成本，而前沿模型的成本保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: 两年间前沿AI智能体在WorkBench上取得了显著进展，能力提升与安全改善并行不悖。尽管整体趋势积极，基础性错误导致的不可逆风险仍需警惕。开源模型的崛起正在重塑AI智能体的成本格局，使高性能水平更加普惠。

Abstract: The best agent on WorkBench in March 2024, GPT-4, completed 43% of tasks and took an unintended harmful action, such as emailing the wrong person, on 26% of them. We re-visit the benchmark in June 2026 and find that the best agent to date, Claude Opus 4.8, completes 89% and takes an unintended harmful action on 2.5%. Aside from this considerable progress in frontier agent performance, three things stand out. First, capability and safety go together on WorkBench rather than trade off, so the models that finish the most tasks also do the least unintended damage. Second, while several classes of error have been totally eliminated, frontier models still make some basic mistakes that occasionally result in irreversible harm, such as sending an email to the wrong person. Third, the rise of open-weight models has drastically lowered costs for a performance level that was previously only accessible to proprietary models, while frontier costs have stayed relatively stable. We release an updated version of the benchmark with data and code quality improvements, new model scores, and analysis of agent progress on WorkBench since 2024.

</details>


### [238] [Refusal Beyond a Single Direction: A Preliminary Comparison of Diff-in-Means and INLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13720)
*Elisabetta Rocchetti,Alfio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了基于差异均值（DiM）的安全微调拒绝控制方法与基于迭代零空间投影（INLP）的两种干预手段。研究发现，INLP的反事实翻转在拒绝抑制上与DiM方向消融具有竞争力，而零空间投影效果较弱；且通过限制INLP到子空间的主导方向，可在保持近基线困惑度的同时实现可调节的拒绝控制。几何分析揭示两种INLP干预在激活空间中产生定性不同的变换模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arditi等人（2024）已证明聊天模型中的拒绝行为由残差流中的单一线性方向介导，且可通过有害/无害激活的差异均值（DiM）恢复。本文旨在探究：INLP能否在拒绝控制上匹敌DiM？INLP更丰富的参数化是否带来更可调节的干预手段？

Method: 在五个开源权重聊天模型上，对比两种DiM干预（激活加法、方向消融）与两种INLP干预（零空间投影、反事实翻转）。通过限制INLP到提取子空间的主导方向，控制干预强度。并对激活空间进行几何分析，观察变换后激活的聚类位置。

Result: INLP反事实翻转在拒绝抑制任务上与DiM方向消融表现相当；零空间投影则始终较弱。限制INLP到主导方向可在保持近基线困惑度的同时保留大部分抑制效果，提供可调节能力。几何上：零空间投影使变换后的激活坍缩到有害与无害簇之间，而反事实翻转将激活移动到对立簇中。

Conclusion: INLP反事实翻转是DiM的有效替代方案，且INLP框架通过方向数量调节提供了更精细的控制能力。模型对"概念缺失"与"概念对立"的编码方式不同，这一几何差异值得未来深入研究。

Abstract: Arditi et al. (2024) has shown that refusal in safety fine-tuned chat models is mediated by a single linear direction in the residual stream, recoverable by a difference-in-means (DiM) of harmful and harmless activations. We compare DiM-based interventions (activation addition and directional ablation) with two interventions derived from Iterative Nullspace Projection (INLP) -- nullspace projection and counterfactual flipping -- on five open-weight chat models, asking whether INLP can match DiM at steering refusal and whether its richer parameterisation yields more tweakable interventions. INLP counterfactual flipping is competitive with DiM directional ablation on refusal suppression, while nullspace projection is consistently weaker. Restricting INLP to the leading directions of the extracted subspace preserves most of the suppression effect at near-baseline perplexity, giving a tunable capability. Geometrically, the two INLP interventions land in qualitatively different regions of activation space: nullspace projection collapses transformed activations \emph{between} the harmful and harmless clusters, while counterfactual flipping moves them into the opposite cluster, suggesting that the model encodes the absence of a concept differently from its opposite -- an intriguing distinction that warrants further investigation in future work.

</details>


### [239] [YeasierAgent: Agentic Social Sandbox as a Canvas for Intent-Driven Creation of Platform-Agnostic Symbiotic Agent-Native Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13722)
*Jory He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: YeasierAgent 提出了一种基于共生智能体、叙事世界和场景感知交互的应用构建范式，将应用重新定义为用户、智能体与世界之间的协作空间，实现了跨平台的智能体原生应用快速构建，并统一了情感陪伴与工具执行的体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件与应用紧密耦合于特定设备与固定图形界面，导致应用构建僵化、跨平台成本高，且智能体的情感陪伴与工具执行两种属性长期割裂。本文旨在突破这一局限，探索一种以智能体为中心的、场景化、协作式的新型应用范式。

Method: 提出"共生智能体原生应用"（Symbiotic Agent-Native Applications）这一形式化类别，设计了一套系统架构，核心手段包括：(1) 采用平台无关的交互单元——智能体（agents）、场景（scenes）、对话（dialogue）——取代固定的图形布局，实现快速、跨平台的应用构建；(2) 在统一的体验沙箱中融合自动化生成、用户创建的世界以及空间多智能体协作，将情感陪伴与实用工具执行整合于同一环境。

Result: 该系统架构实现了两大贡献：(1) 基于平台无关的交互单元，支持跨平台智能体原生应用的快速构建；(2) 在单一体验沙箱内统一了智能体的情感陪伴与实用工具执行属性。由此，YeasierAgent 将智能体应用形态从孤立的、工具型聊天机器人转变为具有凝聚力的、社会性嵌入的计算环境。

Conclusion: YeasierAgent 范式标志着应用从"设备耦合的软件工具"向"用户、智能体与世界的协作空间"的根本转变，为构建更具社会性与沉浸感的智能体原生应用提供了可行的系统架构与理论框架。

Abstract: This paper introduces YeasierAgent, an application-building paradigm based on symbiotic agents, narrative worlds, and scene-aware interaction. It challenges the conventional device-coupled model of software by redefining applications as collaborative spaces among users, agents, and worlds. We present a system architecture that achieves two primary contributions: (1) enabling the rapid, cross-platform construction of agent-native applications by utilizing platform-agnostic interactive units (agents, scenes, dialogue) rather than fixed graphical layouts; and (2) unifying the emotional companionship and practical tool execution attributes of intelligent agents within a single experiential sandbox. By integrating automated generation, user-created worlds, and spatial multi-agent collaboration, YeasierAgent formalizes the category of Symbiotic Agent-Native Applications, demonstrating a shift from isolated, tool-specific chatbots toward cohesive, socially embedded computational environments.

</details>


### [240] [TwinBI: An Agentic Digital Twin for Efficient Augmented Interactions with Business Intelligence Dashboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13731)
*Jisoo Jang Wen-Syan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TwinBI 是一个智能体数字孪生框架，通过将LLM代理系统与可执行的BI仪表板状态耦合，统一了对话交互与仪表板操作，以统一交互日志重建共享分析状态，从而在基准测试和可用性研究中显著提升分析准确率并降低超时率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前BI工具在结合仪表板直接操作与LLM自然语言辅助时，多步骤分析中两种模式容易失去同步，难以在过滤器、层级、指标和图表上下文之间保持一致的的分析状态。

Method: 提出TwinBI框架，核心是将LLM代理系统与可执行BI仪表板状态耦合的数字孪生架构。通过统一交互日志重建共享分析状态，实现对话交互、仪表板操作、语义锚定和溯源追踪的统一，并暴露Schema视图、SQL、日志和/insights命令等工件。

Result: 在对照A/B基准测试中，TwinBI将完全匹配准确率从43.3%提升至63.3%，部分匹配准确率从48.3%提升至70.8%，超时率从40.0%降至10.0%。可用性研究中，参与者对状态感知交互机制给予高任务准确率、中等工作负荷和良好评价。

Conclusion: TwinBI通过将可见的仪表板状态转化为更丰富的可操作上下文，既提升了代理层面的分析可靠性，也改善了面向用户的分析支持体验。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) increasingly combines dashboard interaction with LLM-based assistance, but these two modes often fall out of sync during multi-step analysis. As users switch between direct dashboard manipulation and natural-language queries, it becomes difficult to preserve a consistent analytical state across filters, hierarchies, metrics, and chart context. We present TwinBI, an agentic digital-twin framework that couples an LLM-based agent system with an executable BI dashboard state. TwinBI unifies conversational interaction, dashboard manipulation, semantic grounding, and provenance tracking through a shared analytical state reconstructed from a unified interaction log. It also exposes artifacts such as schema views, SQL, logs, and an /insights command for state-grounded analytical summaries. We evaluate TwinBI in two complementary ways. In a controlled A/B benchmark with the same backbone agent, TwinBI improves exact-match accuracy from 43.3% to 63.3%, partial-credit accuracy from 48.3% to 70.8%, and substantially reduces timeout rate from 40.0% to 10.0% relative to Dashboard alone. In a usability study, participants benefited from the integrated dashboard-and-chat workflow, with high task accuracy, moderate workload, and favorable ratings for state-aware interaction mechanisms. These results suggest that TwinBI improves both agent-level analytical reliability and user-facing analytical support by turning visible dashboard state into richer actionable context. Our dataset and source code are available at: https://github.com/simonjisu/TwinBI

</details>


### [241] [When Sample Selection Bias Precipitates Model Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13732)
*Xinbao Qiao,Xianglong Du,Wei Liu,Jingqi Zhang,Peihua Mai,Meng Zhang,Yan Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在低资源验证机制下，基于孤立、碎片化参考分布的数据选择非但不能防止模型崩溃，反而会因偏向本地流形而加速多样性衰退；本文从理论和实证两方面证明了该现象，并提出基于Wasserstein代理参考的跨孤岛协作缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归合成数据训练中，数据选择被广泛视为防止模型崩溃的手段，但其可靠性依赖于验证器所使用的参考分布。在医疗联盟、金融等低资源数据孤岛场景中，各验证器仅能观测到目标流形的局部、碎片化切片，导致选择本身出现偏差——这一关键问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 首先，从理论上证明在孤岛式验证（siloed verification）条件下，基于局部参考的数据选择会优先保留与本地流形对齐的样本，剪除全局相关的尾部模态，从而加速模型崩溃并导致幂律多样性衰减。其次，提出基于Wasserstein距离的代理参考（proxy references）构建方法，允许在不共享原始数据的前提下，融合多个孤岛的分布信息以形成更全面的参考。最后，通过合成数据实验验证理论结论及缓解方案的有效性。

Result: 理论推演表明，孤岛式数据选择将加速崩溃，并引发幂律多样性衰减。实证结果证实：在偏斜分布上，基于本地参考的选择会失效；而采用跨孤岛协作的Wasserstein代理参考能有效缓解多样性退化。

Conclusion: 在真实数据覆盖碎片化或稀缺的情况下，递归合成数据流水线需格外谨慎——孤岛式局部参考的数据选择反成崩溃催化剂，而跨孤岛的协作代理参考是可行的初步缓解方向。

Abstract: The proliferation of recursive training on synthetic data can alleviate data scarcity but risks model collapse, where repeated training erodes distributional tails and homogenizes outputs. Data selection is widely viewed as a remedy, yet its reliability depends critically on the reference distribution used by the verifier. We show that in low-resource verification regimes, where each verifier observes only a small, fragmented, and biased slice of the target manifold, selection itself becomes biased. This situation naturally arises in low-resource data silos such as healthcare consortia or proprietary financial institutions, where raw data cannot be pooled and local references are inherently incomplete. As a result, selection preferentially retains samples aligned with the local manifold while pruning globally relevant tail modes, turning from a safeguard against collapse into a mechanism that precipitates it. We theoretically prove that such siloed selection accelerates collapse and induces power-law diversity decay. As an initial mitigation, we construct Wasserstein proxy references from multiple silos without sharing raw data. Empirical results confirm that local-reference selection fails on skewed distributions, whereas collaborative proxy references mitigate diversity degradation, suggesting that recursive synthetic-data pipelines require particular caution when real-data coverage is fragmented or scarce.

</details>


### [242] [AI Receptivity or AI Adoption Breadth? A Tool-Specific Reanalysis of the Lower-Literacy/Higher-Usage Link](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13734)
*Hristo Inouzhe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文重新分析了 Tully 等人(2025)的研究数据，发现 AI 素养与 AI 使用之间的负相关关系存在显著的工具类型异质性：AI 素养不能显著预测文本类 AI 工具的使用，但能强烈预测非文本类 AI 工具的采用，且后者主要表现为"是否采用"的二元模式，而非使用强度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tully 等人(2025)声称较低的 AI 素养预示着更高的 AI 接受度。本文作者质疑这一笼统结论，认为该结论可能掩盖了不同 AI 工具类型之间的重要异质性，因此利用其公开数据重新检验这一主张。

Method: 使用 Tully 等人(2025)研究 3 的公开数据，该数据测量了五类 AI 工具的历史使用频率（五点量表）。作者首先采用 OLS（参与者层面均值）、二元 Logit、有序 Logit 和多项 Logit 模型复现了 AI 素养与 AI 使用总量之间的负相关关系；随后按工具类型（文本类 vs. 非文本类）进行分拆分析，并在模型中控制人口统计学变量及原文研究 3 的控制变量。

Result: （1）聚合层面复现了负相关。（2）分拆后，在控制人口学变量的主规格中，AI 素养对文本 AI 使用无显著预测作用（有序 Logit β = -0.090, p = .387），但对非文本 AI 工具的采用预测显著（β = -0.377, p < .001），该效应在原文控制变量规格下依然稳健（β = -0.502, p < .001）。（3）二元、有序及多项 Logit 模型表明，非文本工具的关系主要是"采纳/未采纳"模式：人口学调整后，曾使用非文本 AI 工具的 OR 为 0.68。

Conclusion: 在测量自报历史使用行为（而非陈述偏好）的研究中，证据不支持"较低 AI 素养普遍预测更高 AI 接受度"的简单论断，而是指向一个更窄的模式：AI 素养较低者更倾向于采纳渗透率较低的非文本类 AI 工具，且主要表现为采纳决策差异而非使用强度差异。

Abstract: Recent evidence reported by Tully, Longoni, and Appel (2025) suggests that lower artificial intelligence (AI) literacy predicts greater receptivity toward AI. We revisit this claim using the public data from Study 3 of that article, which measures past usage of five AI tool categories on a five-point frequency scale. We first reproduce the negative association between AI literacy and aggregate AI usage using OLS on participant-level averages, binary logit, ordered logit, and multinomial logit specifications. We then show that the aggregate relationship masks substantial heterogeneity by tool type. In our demographic-adjusted primary specification, AI literacy does not significantly predict text AI usage (ordered-logit $β$ = -0.090, p = .387), whereas it remains a strong predictor of non-text AI adoption ($β$ = -0.377, p < .001). The non-text effect is also robust under Tully et al.'s original Study 3 control specification ($β$ = -0.502, p < .001). Binary, ordered-logit, and multinomial specifications suggest that the non-text relationship is primarily an adoption/non-adoption pattern rather than evidence of intensive use: the demographic-adjusted odds ratio of ever having used a non-text AI tool is 0.68. Thus, in the study that measures self-reported past usage rather than stated preferences, the evidence does not support a simple claim that lower AI literacy predicts greater receptivity to AI in general. It points instead to a narrower pattern of broader adoption across lower-penetration, non-text AI tools.

</details>


### [243] [MA-ProofBench: A Two-Tiered Evaluation of LLMs for Theorem Proving in Mathematical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13782)
*Lushi Pu,Weiming Zhang,Xinheng Xie,Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Hongya Lyu,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Yudong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MA-ProofBench 是首个专注于数学分析的形式化定理证明基准，包含200道覆盖6大核心领域的题目，分本科和博士资格考两个难度级别。当前最强模型 GPT-5.5 在 Level I 上仅达16% Pass@8，Level II 上仅5%，暴露出LLM在深度形式化推理上的严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化定理证明基准（如代数、初等数论）数学覆盖面和难度有限，缺乏对数学分析等需要深度推理的子领域的关注。为填补这一空白，作者构建了首个专注于数学分析的形式化证明基准。

Method: 构建了 MA-ProofBench 基准：包含200个形式化定理，覆盖测度与积分论、复分析、泛函分析等6大核心主题和27个子类别；分为 Level I（本科水平，100题）和 Level II（博士资格考水平，100题）；采用"人工主导、LLM辅助"的形式化流程，并经独立专家审核以确保忠实于原始数学。在一系列通用推理模型和形式化定理证明器上进行评估，并分析了失败模式。

Result: 最佳模型 GPT-5.5 在 Level I 上 Pass@8 仅16%，Level II 上仅5%，多数模型在 Level II 上接近0%。两大主要失败模式为 Mathlib 幻觉和不完整证明。自然语言版本的评估揭示了非形式化推理与形式化推理之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: MA-ProofBench 可作为跟踪高级数学领域形式化推理进展的可靠参考基准。当前LLM在数学分析的形式化证明上能力严重不足，未来需要在减少Mathlib幻觉和提高证明完整性方面取得突破。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made notable progress in automated theorem proving, yet existing formal benchmarks remain limited in both mathematical coverage and difficulty. Most are concentrated in areas that are easier to formalize, such as algebra and elementary number theory, and provide limited coverage of subfields that require deeper reasoning, including mathematical analysis. To address this gap, we introduce MA-ProofBench, to the best of our knowledge, the first formal theorem-proving benchmark dedicated to Mathematical Analysis. The benchmark contains 200 formalized theorems covering 6 core topics and 27 subcategories, including measure and integration theory, complex analysis, and functional analysis. The problems are divided into two difficulty levels, an undergraduate level (Level I, 100 problems) and a Ph.D. qualifying level (Level II, 100 problems), to evaluate how well LLMs perform formal reasoning at different mathematical depths. Each problem is constructed through a human-led, LLM-assisted formalization pipeline followed by independent expert review, ensuring that the formal statements remain faithful to the original mathematics. We evaluate a range of recent general-purpose reasoning models and formal theorem provers on MA-ProofBench. However, most models perform poorly: even the best-performing model, GPT-5.5, achieves only 16% Pass@8 on Level I and 5% on Level II, while most models stay close to 0% on Level II. Further analysis identifies Mathlib hallucinations and incomplete proofs as the two dominant failure modes, while an evaluation on the natural-language version of the benchmark exposes a clear gap between informal and formal reasoning. MA-ProofBench is intended to serve as a reliable reference for tracking progress in formal mathematical reasoning in advanced domains.

</details>


### [244] [Poker Arena: Multi-Axis Profiling of Strategic Reasoning and Memory in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13815)
*Pratham Singla,Shivank Garg,Vihan Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Poker Arena 是一个无限注德州扑克锦标赛平台，通过三层记忆架构和九轴认知画像，将LLM的战略推理能力分解为可解释维度进行评估。结果表明，多轴评估揭示了标量排行榜系统性错排的能力结构，跨维度一致性比单一维度峰值表现更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前博弈基准测试将异构的推理维度压缩为单一标量分数，掩盖了前沿LLM的能力结构差异，无法区分模型在不同战略推理维度上的优劣。

Method: 构建Poker Arena无限注德州扑克锦标赛平台，包含：(1)三层记忆架构——手内记忆、会话内记忆、跨会话记忆；(2)九轴认知画像，将战略推理分解为下注尺度校准、位置意识等可解释维度；(3)对7个前沿模型进行50场会话（每场1000手牌）评估，并设置对照记忆消融实验。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6 以+$15,730筹码和14次第一获得锦标赛筹码冠军，但在九轴均分上仅排第五；持久记忆对部分模型有益，对其他模型反而有害；锦标赛筹码排名与轴分排名显著不同。

Conclusion: 标量排行榜会系统性地错排模型的能力结构，多轴评估能有效揭示被掩盖的能力差异；跨维度一致性比单一维度的峰值表现更为关键。

Abstract: Strategic reasoning under uncertainty underpins consequential decisions in negotiation, finance, and policy, but prevailing game-play benchmarks collapse heterogeneous reasoning dimensions into a single scalar, leaving the capability structure of frontier LLMs unexamined. We introduce Poker Arena, a no-limit Texas Hold'em tournament platform that couples a three-layer memory architecture (within-hand, session, and cross-session) with a nine-axis cognitive profile decomposing strategic reasoning into interpretable dimensions such as bet-sizing calibration and positional awareness. We evaluate seven frontier models across 50 sessions of 1,000 hands and a controlled memory ablation; tournament chips and aggregate axis score order the field differently: Claude Opus 4.6 wins +$15,730 chips with 14 first-place finishes, yet ranks only fifth of seven on mean axis score, while persistent memory helps some models and hurts others. These findings show that multi-axis evaluation surfaces capability structure that scalar leaderboards systematically misrank, with cross-dimensional consistency outweighing peak performance on any single axis.

</details>


### [245] [Hyperdimensional computing for structured querying on tabular data embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13871)
*Sebastián Bugedo,Stijn Vansummeren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将超维计算（HDC）中的全息缩减表示（HRR）模型应用于表格行嵌入，利用其代数性质推导出封闭形式的期望相似度值，从而为向量空间中的结构化查询检索设定可解释的阈值，解决了现有嵌入方法无法可靠判断零匹配的问题。在真实数据集上，HDC在行检索上匹配或优于图基线方法EmbDI，且能更鲁棒地处理不等值谓词并实现完美的属性投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格嵌入方法的一个根本局限是缺乏可解释的相似度分数：查询与最近邻之间的相似度值没有内在语义，无法区分真实匹配和"语料库中无有效答案时的最不相似项"，这导致无法设定有原则的检索阈值，尤其影响零匹配检测的实际部署。

Method: 采用超维计算中的全息缩减表示（HRR）模型对表格行进行嵌入。核心思路是利用HDC操作的代数性质（绑定、捆绑、转置等），针对等值谓词和不等值谓词分别推导出封闭形式的期望相似度表达式。这些期望值随着维度增加收敛到可解释的常数，从而为检索阈值设定提供了理论依据。方法在向量空间中直接回答结构化的select-project查询。

Result: 在两个真实数据集上，HDC在所有配置（不同表大小和谓词长度）下的行检索任务中匹配或优于基于图的EmbDI基线；对不等值谓词的处理更加鲁棒；在足够维度下实现了完美的属性投影准确率；通过有原则的阈值设定，能够可靠识别零匹配谓词。

Conclusion: HRR模型为表格行嵌入提供了一个具有理论可解释性的框架，其代数性质使得相似度阈值可以基于封闭形式的期望值进行设定，从而有效解决零匹配检测问题。该方法在检索性能上具有竞争力，同时提供了现有方法所不具备的可解释性与可靠性保障。

Abstract: Tabular data embeddings have become a cornerstone of data profiling and data integration pipelines, enabling tasks such as entity annotation and resolution; schema matching; column type detection; and table search, among others. Existing approaches embed rows, columns, or entire tables into a vector space and rely on nearest-neighbor search to retrieve candidate matches. A fundamental limitation of current embedding methods is the lack of interpretable similarity scores: the concrete similarity value between a query and its nearest neighbour carries no intrinsic meaning, making it impossible to determine whether that neighbour is a true match or simply the least-dissimilar item in a corpus that contains no valid answer. This inability to set principled thresholds for retrieval undermines practical deployment, particularly for zero-match detection.
  We investigate the use of HyperDimensional Computing (HDC), specifically the Holographic Reduced Representations (HRR) model, as a framework for tabular row embeddings when the retrieval task corresponds to answering structured select-project queries in vector space.
